### [1] [DRL-Driven Edge-Aware Utility Optimization for Multi-Slice 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23056)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmessalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G O-RAN网络的智能资源分配与边缘缓存框架，利用深度Q网络（DQN）优化边缘缓存和动态资源供给，有效支持VR服务的超低延迟和高带宽需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中的虚拟现实（VR）服务需要超低延迟和高带宽以确保无缝用户体验，但传统方法难以满足eMBB、URLLC及新兴MBRLLC切片的服务质量需求。

Method: 在O-RAN架构的控制平面集成深度强化学习（DQN）代理，实现跨多网络切片的前瞻性自适应内容分发与实时计算资源分配。

Result: 仿真结果表明，基于DQN的框架在降低延迟和提升吞吐量方面持续优于传统方法，为沉浸式VR应用提供更可靠、响应更快的支持。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效提升6G O-RAN网络中VR服务的性能表现，证明了深度强化学习在下一代网络资源管理与边缘缓存优化中的实用价值。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) services delivered over 6G networks demand ultra-low latency and high bandwidth to ensure seamless user experiences. This paper presents an intelligent resource allocation and edge caching framework for 6G O-RAN networks, leveraging Deep Q-Network (DQN) learning for optimizing edge caching and dynamic resource provisioning across multiple network slices within an O-RAN-compliant architecture. By incorporating DRL agents into the network control plane, the proposed system enables proactive and adaptive content distribution as well as real-time computational resource allocation that meets the quality-of-service demands of eMBB, URLLC, and especially the emerging MBRLLC slices essential for VR. Simulation results demonstrate that the DQN-based framework consistently outperforms traditional methods in reducing latency and improving throughput, leading to more reliable and responsive support for immersive VR applications in 6G environments.

</details>


### [2] [Combined Radar and Magnetometer Sensor Network with LoRa-Mediated Awareness for Wildlife-Vehicle Collision Prevention: A Monte Carlo Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23117)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种集成传感器网络系统，通过雷达、磁力计、动态信息牌和LoRa通信的协同作用，在1公里测试路段上将野生动物-车辆碰撞率降低47.4%，同时将安全穿越通过量提高77%，为传统围栏方案提供了经济可行的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物-车辆碰撞（WVCs）在加拿大每20年造成约570人死亡，阿尔伯塔省占22%，日均直接和间接成本达30万加元。传统围栏结合穿越通道虽可减少约86%的碰撞，但在大部分乡村道路上经济上不可行，仍存在大量碰撞残留问题，亟需更具成本效益的解决方案。

Method: 开发了集成传感器网络系统，包含交替侧边雷达节点（10米间隔基线）、三轴磁力计、动态信息牌，以及相邻雷达间的LoRa通信。通过离散时间蒙特卡洛仿真在1公里测试走廊进行评估，整合了六状态动物行为马尔可夫模型（含车辆威胁相关决策分支）、智能驾驶员模型车辆动力学，以及三模式对比分析以分离感知、驾驶员警报和网络协调的各自贡献。

Result: 在60次独立试验中，集成系统相对于无缓解对照组，将每次道路进入的碰撞率降低47.4%（Welch's t = 2.82, p < 0.01），同时通过降低触发穿越前撤退的车辆威胁感知，使安全道路穿越通过量增加77%。敏感性分析确立了5-20米交替雷达间距和小至中型动物类别（狐狸至鹿类）的统计显著性等效性能区间，并对基线传感器灵敏度十倍降级具有运行稳健性。

Conclusion: 该集成传感器网络系统为野生动物-车辆碰撞问题提供了有效的技术解决方案，在显著降低碰撞率的同时提升了动物安全穿越效率。系统在合理的传感器配置范围内具有良好的性能稳健性，为乡村道路的野生动物保护提供了比传统围栏方案更具经济可行性的替代选择。

Abstract: Wildlife-vehicle collisions (WVCs) cause approximately 570 human fatalities in Canada per 20-year cohort, with Alberta accounting for 22% of these and incurring an estimated CAD $300,000 per day in direct and indirect costs. Wildlife fencing combined with crossing structures reduces collisions by ~86% on well-instrumented sites but remains economically infeasible across the majority of rural road kilometres, leaving a substantial collision residual. We present a combined sensor network integrating alternating-side radar nodes (10-m spacing baseline), three-axis magnetometers, dynamic message signs, and LoRa-mediated awareness propagation between adjacent radars. System performance is evaluated through a discrete-time Monte Carlo simulation on a 1 km test corridor, incorporating a six-state animal behavioural Markov model with vehicle-threat-dependent decision branching, Intelligent Driver Model vehicle dynamics, and a three-mode contrast that isolates the contributions of sensing, driver alerting, and network coordination. Across 60 independent trials, the integrated system reduces the collision rate per road entry by 47.4% relative to an unmitigated control (Welch's t = 2.82, p < 0.01), and simultaneously increases safe road-crossing throughput by 77% by lowering the perceived vehicle threat that otherwise triggers pre-crossing retreats. Sensitivity sweeps establish a statistically significant equivalent-performance band across 5-20 m alternating radar spacing and across small-to-medium animal classes (fox- through deer-class), with operational robustness against tenfold degradation of baseline sensor sensitivity.

</details>


### [3] [Orchestrating Data Collection and Computation in Green IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23152)
*Junfei Zhan,Tengjiao He,Kwan-Wu Chin,Benyu Chen,Fei Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出混合整数线性规划(MILP)方法，用于在能量收集节点上调度和嵌入IoT应用，并通过滚动时域控制(RHC)和贪婪算法两种启发式方法在保证接近最优性能的同时降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来物联网网络将托管涉及数据收集和计算任务的应用程序，这些应用运行在一个或多个服务器上。在能量收集节点上调度和嵌入这些应用面临诸多挑战，需要优化设备采样时间、应用运行决策以及设备、网关和服务器的能耗，同时确保应用能够频繁执行。

Method: 提出首个混合整数线性规划(MILP)模型，优化三个关键决策：(i)设备采样时间，(ii)是否运行应用程序，(iii)设备、网关和服务器的能耗使用。采用最大服务年龄(AoS)指标，将MILP目标设定为最小化应用的最大AoS(min-max AoS)。同时提出两种启发式解决方案：(i)基于滚动时域控制(RHC)的方法，(ii)根据AoS贪婪嵌入应用的解决方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，滚动时域控制(RHC)方法和贪婪方法的min-max AoS分别比MILP最优解高1.07倍和1.13倍，说明这两种启发式方法能够在合理计算时间内达到接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了能量收集IoT节点应用调度与嵌入的优化框架，MILP提供了理论最优解，而RHC和贪婪方法为实际部署提供了可行的近似解决方案，在性能损失可控的情况下大幅降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Future Internet of things (IoT) networks will host applications that involve data collection and computation tasks on one or more servers. To this end, this paper proposes the first mixed integer linear program (MILP) to schedule and embed applications on energy harvesting nodes, where it optimizes (i) the sampling time of devices, (ii) whether to run an application, and (iii) the energy usage of devices, gateways and servers. To ensure applications are run often, we adopt the maximum age of service (AoS) metric, and set the MILP's objective to minimize the maximum AoS or min-max AoS of applications. This paper also proposes two novel solutions: (i) a receding horizon control (RHC) based method, and (ii) a solution that greedily embeds applications according to their AoS. The results show that the min-max AoS of RHC and greedy approach is respectively 1.07x and 1.13x higher than MILP.

</details>


### [4] [On the Performance of DCF in Full Duplex WLANs with Hidden Terminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23276)
*Anastasios C. Politis,Constantinos S. Hilas,Hristos T. Anastassiu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在存在隐藏终端的全双工WLAN中，基于DCF机制的FD技术相比半双工仅有微小的饱和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工技术被认为是现代WLAN发展的下一个重大飞跃，理论上可使系统吞吐量翻倍。然而，FD操作需要MAC层支持两个节点同时访问共享介质，而当前标准的DCF机制正是为避免此类情况而设计的；同时隐藏终端问题在FD通信中依然存在，因此需要研究CSMA/CA协议在FD WLAN中的实际性能表现。

Method: 基于性能建模的方法，分析CSMA/CA协议（DCF机制的基础）在全双工WLAN中的性能，并与标准半双工场景进行对比，重点关注存在隐藏终端的情况。

Result: 研究结果表明，在DCF机制下，全双工技术在饱和吞吐量方面仅表现出微小的性能改进，与其理论上的吞吐量翻倍潜力存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 虽然全双工技术理论上具有显著优势，但在现有DCF机制和隐藏终端存在的实际网络环境中，其性能提升非常有限，说明需要针对性的MAC层协议优化才能真正发挥FD技术的潜力。

Abstract: Full Duplex (FD) technology is considered as one of the next big leap in the evolution of modern WLANs. Allowing a node to simultaneously transmit a data frame while in receive mode, can theoretically double the system throughput. However, several requirements must be fulfilled in order for FD operation to manifest. One obvious prerequisite is that the Medium Access Control (MAC) mechanism must allow two nodes to access the shared medium simultaneously. In modern WLANs the standard MAC layer mechanism is the Distributed Coordination Function (DCF), which is specifically designed to avoid such situations. FD communications may also take place when the physical placement of the communicating parts involves the existence of hidden terminals which, in standard Half Duplex (HD) communications, imposes a significant problem. This paper investigates the performance of the Carrier Sense Multiple Access with Collision Avoidance (CSMA/CA) protocol, which constitutes the basis of the DCF mechanism, in FD WLANs with hidden terminals, and compares it with the standard HD case. Our analysis is based on performance modelling. Results indicate that, under the DCF regime, FD technology exhibits an exiguous performance improvement, in terms of saturation throughput, when compared with its half duplex counterpart.

</details>


### [5] [Purification Strategy Optimization for Entanglement Routing in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23331)
*Javier Vecino Peñas,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo,Sergio Gándara Gándara,Manuel Fernández-Veiga*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究净化感知的量子路由问题，利用动态规划技术寻找最优净化策略，在资源消耗与端到端保真度之间实现最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的噪声、退相干和不完美操作导致纠缠交换过程中纠缠态的保真度逐渐降低，如何在路由协议中维持高质量纠缠成为关键挑战。纠缠净化虽可缓解此问题但需消耗额外资源，因此需要设计能够优化资源与保真度权衡的路由方案。

Method: 将净化感知量子路由问题形式化为优化任务，通过动态规划技术求解最优净化策略选择问题。该方法在路径规划中集成净化机制，系统地评估不同策略组合的性能表现。

Result: 所提方法能够在多种场景下有效识别最优净化策略，成功实现资源消耗与端到端保真度之间的最优平衡，验证了净化感知路由方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态规划优化净化策略选择，可以有效解决量子网络中纠缠质量退化问题，为实用化量子网络路由协议设计提供了有价值的理论框架和解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum networks rely on the efficient distribution of entanglement to enable long-distance quantum communication and information processing. A key challenge in these networks is the design of routing protocols capable of maintaining high quality entanglement in the presence of noise, decoherence, and imperfect operations, which progressively degrade the fidelity of entangled states through entanglement swapping. Entanglement purification provides an effective mechanism to mitigate this degradation at the cost of additional resources. In this work, we study purification-aware quantum routing and formulate the problem of selecting optimal purification strategies as an optimization task. By employing dynamic programming techniques, we identify strategies that optimally balance resource consumption and end-to-end fidelity, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach across different scenarios.

</details>


### [6] [Experimental Evaluation of LPWAN Technologies: mioty, LoRaWAN, Sigfox, NB-IoT, and LTE-M in Deep Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23483)
*Christof Röhrig,Benz Cramer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验比较了mioty、LoRaWAN、Sigfox、NB-IoT和LTE-M五种LPWAN技术在建筑穿透性能方面的表现，旨在为地下空间等难以覆盖区域的智能计量应用提供技术选型参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗广域网（LPWAN）广泛应用于智慧城市、智能建筑和智能计量等场景，但能量计量设备常位于地下空间，无线信号难以穿透。现有研究缺乏对多种LPWAN技术在真实建筑穿透场景下的性能对比，因此需要通过实际测试评估各技术的穿透能力和适用性。

Method: 采用低成本商用现成设备，在真实场景中对mioty、LoRaWAN、Sigfox、NB-IoT和LTE-M五种主流LPWAN技术进行实验测量，评估其在建筑穿透方面的性能表现。实验结果被集成到多特蒙德应用科学技术大学的计量系统中进行验证。

Result: 通过实验测量获得了五种LPWAN技术在建筑穿透性能方面的对比数据，验证了各技术在实际部署环境中的信号覆盖能力，为地下空间等复杂场景的技术选型提供了实测依据。

Conclusion: 不同LPWAN技术在建筑穿透性能上存在差异，实际部署时需根据具体应用场景和环境条件选择合适的技术方案。实验结果为智能计量系统在地下空间等难以覆盖区域的部署提供了重要参考价值。

Abstract: Low Power Wide Area Networks (LPWAN) are often used in applications such as Smart City, Smart Buildings and Smart Metering. Energy meters are often located in underground spaces that are difficult to reach with wireless technology. This paper presents an experimental study comparing different LPWAN technologies in terms of building penetration. The technologies mioty, Low Power Long Range Wide Area Network (LoRaWAN), Sigfox, Narrow Band Internet of Things (NB-IoT), and Long Term Evolution for Machines (LTE-M) are evaluated experimentally. The aim of the research is to investigate the performance of building penetration of different radio technologies in real-life scenarios. The measurements are performed with inexpensive off-the-shelf equipment. The results of the study are integrated in the metering system of the Dortmund University of Applied Sciences and Arts.

</details>


### [7] [Sea Trial Validation of the ROS-DESERT Middleware with Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23553)
*Davide Cosimo,Davide Costa,Riccardo Costanzi,Filippo Campagnaro,Andrea Caiti,Michele Zorzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化软件架构，通过环境感知协调异构AUV以改善水下声学连接性，并在海上试验中验证了深度自适应策略能有效提升约1km水平距离下的数据包接收性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下声学通信受环境因素影响显著，传统AUV系统缺乏环境感知的自适应通信能力，且异构AUV协同作业存在软件兼容性挑战，亟需一种模块化架构来实现环境感知的通信优化并支持遗留系统集成。

Method: 提出将ROS 2应用层与DESERT Underwater通信框架通过rmw_desert中间件集成的模块化架构，并集成ROS 1桥接以兼容遗留车辆控制器；实现轻量级深度优化策略，利用环境感知和AUV机动性改善声学链路性能。

Result: 在拉斯佩齐亚湾约100m水深的三AUV海上试验中验证：深度自适应重定位在约1km水平距离处显著提升数据包接收率，而在较短距离（信号能量高于解调阈值）时性能差异可忽略；同时验证了架构的可行性、模块化和在现有AUV平台上的实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构成功实现了环境感知的AUV协调通信，支持细粒度跨层通信栈配置和机载环境数据处理，深度优化策略在中等距离有效改善链路性能，为异构AUV协同水下通信提供了可行的软件解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a modular software architecture that enables environmental-aware coordination of heterogeneous Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) to improve underwater acoustic connectivity. The architecture combines a Robot Operating System 2 application layer with the DESERT Underwater communication framework through the rmw_desert middleware, and integrates a Robot Operating System 1 bridge to ensure interoperability with legacy vehicle front-seat controllers. This design enables fine-grained, cross-layer configurability of the communication stack and supports onboard processing of environmental measurements to inform adaptive communication behaviors.
  As a representative use case, this architecture is used to implement a lightweight depth-optimization strategy that exploits environmental awareness and AUV mobility to improve acoustic link performance. The complete software stack is validated through sea trials conducted off the Gulf of La Spezia in littoral water with an average depth of approximately 100m using a deployment involving three AUVs with distinct operational roles. Experimental results indicate that depth-adaptive repositioning yields measurable gains in packet reception at horizontal separation of approximately 1km, while differences are negligible at shorter ranges where the received signal energy remains above demodulation thresholds. Beyond link-level performance the sea trials confirm the feasibility, modularity, and practical deployability of the proposed architecture on existing AUV platforms.

</details>


### [8] [SDNator is Not Another SDN Controller: Enabling Extensible Data-Driven Control in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23816)
*Y. Lin,R. Zhang,E. Balta,X. Zhu,J. Zhang,K. Barton,D. Tilbury,Z. Mao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SDNator，这是首个支持可扩展、数据驱动控制的CPS框架，通过应用和数据驱动的设计模式实现了领域无关的控制器架构，并在增材制造等场景中展现了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SDN式的集中控制架构在CPS（如制造、物联网、自动驾驶系统）中越来越流行，但现有框架无法提供领域无关、易于扩展的数据驱动CPS应用支持。

Method: 设计和实现了SDNator框架，采用应用和数据驱动的设计模式，应用程序作为数据消费者和生产者共同定义控制器的工作流；集成了两个数据存储后端，支持事件驱动和数据驱动的编程模式。

Result: 基准测试表明SDNator具有高可扩展性，性能与广泛使用的SDN控制器Ryu相当；通过制造和网络系统的案例研究验证了其能力和可用性；基于SDNator的调度相比分布式方法显著缩短了生产时间并提高了异常情况下的可靠性，能够灵活调整和优化生产计划以应对紧急需求（如COVID-19期间的PPE生产）。

Conclusion: SDNator成功实现了首个配备数字孪生的增材制造舰队中央控制器，证明了数据驱动控制在CPS中的有效性和实用价值，为CPS应用提供了通用的、可扩展的控制框架。

Abstract: An SDN-like centralized control architecture is increasingly popular and has been widely explored in cyber-physical systems (CPS) such as manufacturing, internet-of-things, and autonomous vehicle systems for higher flexibility, programmability and scalability. However, no existing frameworks can offer domain-agnostic, easily extensible support for data-driven CPS applications. In this work, we design, implement, and open-source \textit{SDNator}, the first framework to enable extensible, data-driven control in CPS. SDNator embraces an application- and data-driven design where applications function as data consumers and producers to collectively define the workflows of the controller. SDNator also incorporates two data store backends to support both event-driven and data-driven programming patterns. Benchmarks show that SDNator is highly scalable, and delivers comparable performance to Ryu, a widely used SDN controller.
  Moreover, we demonstrate the capabilities and usability of SDNator through our case studies of manufacturing and networking systems. By integrating applications from respective domains, we build different ``controllers'' for different scenarios. Most notably, we leverage SDNator to implement the first digital-twin-equipped central controller for additive manufacturing fleets. We show through extensive and realistic simulations that SDNator-based scheduling can (1) significantly shorten production time and improve reliability in the presence of anomalies compared to decentralized approaches, and (2) flexibly adjust and optimize production plans upon urgent requests such as producing Personal Protective Equipment during the COVID-19 pandemic.

</details>

### [9] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GENSTRAT框架，通过程序化生成的战略环境评估大语言模型在博弈场景中的表现，发现整体实力相近的模型展现出显著不同的能力特征和局部波动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有战略推理基准测试基于固定博弈，容易饱和且难以泛化到实际部署中复杂多变的战略环境；预测LLM在市场、拍卖等场景中的行为具有挑战性。

Method: 开发GENSTRAT框架：程序化生成双人零和不完全信息纸牌博弈分布，构建六维能力画像评估体系（状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感性、对手建模、风险、脆弱性），并提出锯齿度指标衡量模型在相似博弈中表现的波动性。

Result: 在50个样本博弈、36000场比赛的评估中：前沿模型平均得分更高；整体实力相近的模型（gpt-5、claude、gemini-3.1-pro）展现出质变性的能力差异；前两名模型比第三名具有更显著的局部波动性。

Conclusion: 能力画像与锯齿度指标提供了超越整体排名的部署相关诊断信息，能够更准确预测模型在实际战略环境中的表现差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [10] [BOHM: Zero-Cost Hierarchical Attribution for Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22866)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BOHM方法，通过复用复合AI系统已有的路由权重直接提取分层归因树，在零边际成本下实现多分辨率归因，在多个实验中与SHAP方法高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley的归因方法（如SHAP）需要评估任意组件子集，但对于第三方API、不透明端点和智能体编排器等场景，大多数组件联盟无法被评估，导致传统方法失效。

Method: BOHM直接从系统已维护的路由权重中提取分层归因树：叶节点归因为根到叶路由权重的路径乘积；第k层归因为深度k节点上的诱导分布。该方法零边际成本、无需访问组件内部、同时提供多分辨率归因。

Result: 在18个LLM的3层层级结构实验中，BOHM的Kendall tau达到0.928，SHAP达到0.980但需要9000倍的联盟评估次数。在智能体研究中，BOHM与SHAP的一致性可由驱动器的首选是否为经验最优工具来预测。在US Census数据集上，BOHM在各层级均恢复了真实排名（tau最高0.722）。

Conclusion: BOHM满足效率性、单调性、对称性和弱抑制性但不满足Shapley的可加性，应被理解为互补性原语：一种在路由状态存在处可计算的多分辨率分解方法，其与Shapley方法的差异本身具有诊断价值。

Abstract: Compound AI systems route tasks through hierarchies of specialised components. Attribution is dominated by Shapley-based methods (SHAP), which decompose a coalition value function into per-component marginal contributions and require evaluation of the system on arbitrary component subsets. That requirement fails for third-party APIs, opaque endpoints, and agentic orchestrators that concentrate routing on a few tools, leaving most coalitions un-evaluable from the deployed orchestrator. We introduce BOHM, which extracts a hierarchical attribution tree directly from the routing weights such systems already maintain: leaf attribution is the path product of root-to-leaf routing weights; level-k attribution is the induced distribution over depth-k nodes. The method has zero marginal cost, requires no access to component internals, and provides multi-resolution attribution at every level simultaneously, which flat methods cannot offer at any evaluation budget. BOHM and SHAP answer different questions and converge when the deployed router routes near-optimally. On 18 LLMs in a 3-level hierarchy over 880 LiveCodeBench problems, BOHM yields Kendall tau=0.928; SHAP reaches tau=0.980 at 9,000x more coalition evaluations per seed. On a 5-driver, 7-benchmark agentic study (35 cells, complete coverage), drivers concentrate routing on a single tool (top-share median 0.65), and cell-level tau(BOHM,SHAP) is predicted by whether the driver's top pick is the empirically best tool (mean +0.22 vs ~+0.01). On a US Census hierarchy (475 leaves, 4 levels), BOHM recovers ground-truth rankings at every level (tau up to 0.722). BOHM satisfies efficiency, monotonicity, symmetry, and weak suppression but not Shapley's additivity. It is best understood as a complementary primitive: a multi-resolution decomposition computable wherever routing state exists, whose disagreement with Shapley is itself diagnostic.

</details>


### [11] [NeuroNL2LTL: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Natural Language Translation of Linear Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22874)
*Paapa Kwesi Quansah,Ernest Bonnah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroNL2LTL，一种神经符号架构，通过验证器在环训练实现自然语言到线性时序逻辑(LTL)的可靠翻译，在保证86%输出可满足的同时实现了28%的语义等价率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到形式逻辑的翻译方法存在局限性：基于模板的方法牺牲了表达性以换取可靠性，而神经方法虽然流畅但无法提供正确性保证。这限制了形式验证在安全关键开发中的应用范围。

Method: 提出NeuroNL2LTL神经符号架构，采用验证器在环训练机制。系统通过结构保持的中间表示将翻译路由到LTL，对生成规范进行可满足性和非平凡性检查，并使用最小编辑修复机制纠正近似输出。验证结果作为强化学习的奖励信号，直接优化神经组件的形式正确性。

Result: 在跨越航空航天、机器人、自动驾驶车辆等12个领域的20多万条需求数据集上，NeuroNL2LTL实现了28%的语义等价率和86%的可满足性验证率。系统还能从LTL生成上下文相关的解释，帮助领域专家验证规范。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了形式验证可以作为神经规范系统的训练目标和运行时过滤器，使我们能够构建基于神经的工具，其可靠性源于逻辑保证而非统计置信度，为安全关键系统开发提供了可靠的形式化验证工具。

Abstract: Effectively translating between natural language (NL) and formal logics like Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) requires expertise that limits formal verification's reach in safety-critical development. Template-based approaches sacrifice expressiveness for reliability; neural methods achieve fluency but provide no correctness guarantees. We present NeuroNL2LTL, a neurosymbolic architecture unifying learned translation with formal verification. NeuroNL2LTL routes translation through an intermediate representation whose mapping to LTL is structure-preserving by construction. Generated specifications undergo satisfiability and non-triviality checking; a minimal-edit repair mechanism corrects near-miss outputs before they reach downstream tools. The central innovation is verifier-in-the-loop training: verification outcomes serve as reward signals for reinforcement learning, producing neural components that optimize directly for formal correctness. On 200,000+ requirements spanning aerospace, robotics, autonomous vehicles, and ten additional domains, NeuroNL2LTL achieves 28\% semantic equivalence with reference specifications while ensuring 86\% of outputs are verified satisfiable. The system also generates contextually grounded explanations from LTL, enabling domain experts to validate specifications without specialized training. This work demonstrates that formal verification can function as both training objective and runtime filter for neural specification systems, allowing us to build neural-based tools whose reliability derives from logical guarantees rather than statistical confidence.

</details>


### [12] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Research Math Agents (RMA)框架，用于自动推理研究级数学问题。在First Proof基准测试中，RMA解决了10个问题中的8个，优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中于竞赛数学或形式定理证明，而研究级数学问题需要长时程推理、文献支撑和迭代证明改进，缺乏针对此类问题的自动化框架。

Method: RMA将研究级证明求解分解为专业模块：问题分析、文献搜索与理解、公平比较、知识库构建和证明验证，通过初始化器、提议者和验证者代理在共享结构化内存中协调，在多角色、多轮工作流中协作生成、改进和验证候选证明。

Result: 在First Proof基准测试（由专家数学家贡献的10个跨领域研究级问题）上，RMA解决8/10问题，表现优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia，生成更具逻辑性和可读性的证明。消融研究表明性能提升源于结构化推理模块、迭代改进和验证者反馈的交互作用。

Conclusion: RMA成功实现了研究级数学问题的自动推理，证明了结构化多代理框架在此类复杂问题上的有效性，为数学研究自动化提供了新范式。代码和实现将公开发布。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [13] [SciAtlas: A Large-Scale Knowledge Graph for Automated Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878)
*Shuofei Qiao,Yunxiang Wei,Jiazheng Fan,Bin Wu,Busheng Zhang,Mengru Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Ningyu Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciAtlas，一个大规模跨学科异构学术知识图谱，整合了26个学科超过4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组，并设计了神经符号检索算法，以解决现有学术检索工具缺乏拓扑推理能力的问题，为AI驱动的科研自动化提供认知地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球学术产出的指数级增长导致“信息爆炸”，现有学术检索工具主要依赖浅层关键词匹配或向量空间语义检索，缺乏处理复杂逻辑连接所需的拓扑推理能力；基于代理的深度研究框架则容易产生逻辑幻觉且推理成本高昂。这些局限阻碍了深层次跨学科知识的整合与利用。

Method: 构建大规模多学科异构学术资源知识图谱SciAtlas，整合26个学科4300万篇论文，形成包含1.57亿实体和30亿三元组的科学演化全景网络；设计神经符号检索算法，采用三路径协同召回与图重排序机制，实现从简单语义匹配到确定性关联发现的过渡。

Result: 成功构建了SciAtlas知识图谱，提供了结构化的拓扑认知基底；开发的检索算法实现了语义匹配向确定性关联发现的无缝转换；在文献综述、研究趋势自动综合、创意定位和学术轨迹探索等关键应用场景中展示了有效性，显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: SciAtlas能够作为有效的“认知地图”赋能自动化科研全流程，打破学科壁垒并为AI代理提供全局视角，同时大幅降低推理成本。研究团队已在GitHub开源知识图谱检索及多种下游任务接口，促进学术社区应用。

Abstract: The exponential growth of global academic output has confronted researchers and AI agents with an unprecedented ``information explosion,'' where fragmented and unstructured knowledge organization impedes deep interdisciplinary integration. Current academic retrieval tools predominantly rely on superficial keyword matching or vector-space semantic retrieval, which lack the topological reasoning capabilities required to navigate complex logical connections. Agentic deep-research-based frameworks are often prone to logical hallucinations and consuming high inference costs. To bridge this gap, in this report, we introduce SciAtlas, a large-scale, multi-disciplinary, heterogeneous academic resource knowledge graph designed as a panoramic scientific evolution network. By integrating over 43M papers from 26 disciplines, and a total of 157M entities and 3B triplets, SciAtlas provides a structured topological cognitive substrate that dismantles disciplinary barriers and furnishes AI agents with a global perspective. Furthermore, we develop a neuro-symbolic retrieval algorithm featuring tri-path collaborative recall and graph reranking, achieving a seamless transition from simple semantic matching to deterministic association discovery. We also present key application directions of SciAtlas, including literature review, automated research trend synthesis, idea positioning, and academic trajectory exploration, to demonstrate that SciAtlas can serve as an effective ``cognitive map'' to empower the full loop of automated scientific research while significantly reducing reasoning costs. We have released the interfaces for KG retrieval and various downstream tasks in our GitHub repo.

</details>


### [14] [Energy per Successful Goal: Goal-Level Energy Accounting for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22883)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了A-LEMS框架，将AI能耗计量单位从"每次推理能耗"重新定义为"每成功目标能耗(EpG)"，并引入编排开销指数(OOI)量化智能体系统的编排能耗。实验表明智能体工作流能耗是线性基线的4.33倍，该开销由编排结构而非推理计算驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI能耗基准测试以单次模型调用或训练运行为粒度进行测量。对于智能体系统（单个用户目标可能触发多步编排、工具调用、重试和失败恢复循环），调用次数是实现细节而非任务属性，推理级别的归一化会错误表征目标完成的能耗成本。

Method: 提出A-LEMS（智能体LLM能耗测量系统）跨层测量框架：1）将能耗计量单位重新定义为每成功目标能耗(EpG)，聚合所有执行尝试的总工作流能耗并按成功完成目标归一化；2）通过时间边界模型形式化能耗归因；3）设计五层观测管道将RAPL信号映射到工作流级能耗；4）建立可复现协议，将每次测量绑定到硬件和运行时配置；5）定义编排开销指数(OOI)量化编排相对于线性执行的能耗代价。

Result: 在五个推理和三个工具增强任务族上，智能体工作流每成功目标平均能耗比线性基线高4.33倍（888.1 J vs 205.3 J）。该开销由编排结构驱动而非推理计算。对于工具增强任务，OOI反转至1.0x以下，即智能体执行比线性执行更便宜，验证了该指标捕获的是编排结构而非固定向上偏差。

Conclusion: 每推理能耗指标不足以衡量智能体AI的能耗。EpG和OOI提供了准确基准测试的测量基础，其中编排结构是能耗的主要决定因素。

Abstract: Current AI energy benchmarks measure consumption at the granularity of a single model invocation or training run. For classical single-turn workloads this unit remains coherent. For agentic systems - where a single user goal may trigger multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure-recovery cycles - the invocation count is an implementation artifact rather than a task property, and inference-level normalization misrepresents the energy cost of goal completion. We present A-LEMS (Agentic LLM Energy Measurement System), a cross-layer measurement framework that redefines the unit of AI energy accounting from energy per inference to Energy per Successful Goal (EpG). EpG aggregates total workflow energy across all execution attempts, including failures and retries, normalized by successfully completed goals. A-LEMS formalizes energy attribution through a temporal boundary model, a five-layer observation pipeline mapping RAPL signals to workflow-level energy, and a reproducibility protocol binding every measurement to hardware and runtime configuration. Building on EpG, we define the Orchestration Overhead Index (OOI), isolating the energy cost of orchestration relative to linear execution under identical task criteria.
  Across five reasoning and three tool-augmented task families, agentic workflows consume 4.33x higher mean energy per successful goal than linear baselines (888.1 J vs 205.3 J). This overhead is driven by orchestration structure, not inference compute. For tool-augmented tasks, OOI inverts below 1.0x: agentic execution is cheaper than linear, confirming the metric captures orchestration structure rather than a fixed upward bias.
  These findings establish that energy-per-inference is insufficient for agentic AI. EpG and OOI provide the measurement foundation for accurate benchmarking, where orchestration structure is the primary determinant of energy cost.

</details>


### [15] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ImProver 2，一个用于Lean 4的神经符号化自动证明优化框架，通过数据高效的专家迭代流程和结构化支架，使7B参数模型在证明重构任务上达到与大规模前沿模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库快速扩张，亟需重构已验证证明以提高可维护性并改善神经证明器的训练数据质量。然而，现有方法面临目标异构且启发式定义、数据稀缺、训练推理成本高昂等挑战，难以实现可扩展的证明优化。

Method: 提出ImProver 2神经符号化框架：(1) 数据高效的专家迭代流程；(2) 暴露形式化结构并辅以轻量级非形式化抽象的支架系统；(3) 引入一套捕获证明结构特性的评估指标体系。

Result: 训练的7B参数模型在同族模型中超越大数个数量级的模型，在中 tier前沿模型各指标上具竞争力；神经符号支架显著提升小模型和前沿模型性能；小模型能有效重构研究级证明。

Conclusion: 通过适当的支架和训练，小模型可在复杂多样的指标上有效重构研究级证明，与更大的系统相媲美，证明优化是一个可扩展、可学习的任务。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [16] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为中介模糊逻辑建立了统一的逻辑与语义框架，涵盖类型-1基础及区间类型-2、粒度类型-3和量子扩展，证明了系统的可靠性、次协调性和保守性，并通过自动刹车传感器融合案例展示了其在处理冲突信息时的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中介模糊逻辑作为一种在模糊控制和决策中调和犹豫或冲突评估的实用方案被提出，但其逻辑和语义基础尚不完善，尤其是在操作性类型-1设置之外缺乏统一的理论基础，这限制了其在更高阶模糊系统和智能决策系统中的可靠应用。

Method: 1. 将中介算子刻画为由犹豫和矛盾控制的凸聚合；
2. 将中介真值建模为连续双格状结构中的独立真-假对；
3. 引入一个命题系统，在标准t-范数模糊逻辑基础上扩展中介连接词；
4. 建立语义扩展到区间类型-2真值、粒度索引局部评估以及希尔伯特空间上的效应和密度算子。

Result: 证明了该逻辑系统的可靠性、次协调性和相对于基础模糊逻辑的保守性；在高阶扩展的适当假设下，可归约为类型-1情形；通过自动刹车传感器融合示例验证了框架在处理不完整、异质和轻度矛盾证据时的有效性和安全性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了跨类型层次的中介模糊逻辑统一框架，阐明了各层次间的一致性关系，为未来智能决策系统中的犹豫与冲突推理提供了坚实的理论基础和可靠的形式化工具。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [17] [EVE-Agent: Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22905)
*Yamato Arai,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EVE-Agent，一种证据可验证的自演化搜索代理，通过要求每个生成的训练样本包含可验证的证据片段，解决了自演化系统中缺乏可信证据的问题，显著提升了证据支撑的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无数据自演化搜索代理能够自主生成问题、回答并从自身反馈中学习，但缺乏可验证证据的闭环可能奖励流利但无支撑的样本，导致自生成课程成为不透明且不可靠的训练信号。因此需要确保证据的可验证性作为可信自演化的前提。

Method: 提出EVE-Agent，在提议者-求解者框架基础上进行改进：提议者生成问题、答案和逐字证据片段；证据验证器根据提供证据时的边际准确率增益来奖励该片段。这种方法无需oracle答案、人工标签或外部标注，保持主干模型、检索器、搜索工具和优化框架不变。

Result: 实验表明，EVE-Agent相比先前的自演化搜索代理，显著提升了证据支撑的正确性。生成的训练课程不仅可自生成，而且天生可审计——每个训练样本都带有可检查的来源片段，解释其可信原因。

Conclusion: 证据可验证性是自演化搜索代理可信演化的关键前提，EVE-Agent通过引入证据验证机制成功构建了透明、可审计的自演化训练流程，为自演化系统提供了更可靠的改进路径。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents should not train on examples they cannot justify. Data-free self-evolving search agents offer a scalable route to systems that generate their own questions, answer them, and improve from their own feedback without human annotations. Yet, without verifiable evidence, this loop can reward fluent but unsupported examples, turning the self-generated curriculum into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. We argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents: each generated instance should include not only an answer but also a source-grounded span whose contribution to that answer can be measured. We introduce EVE-Agent, an Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agent that operationalizes this principle through a modification to the proposer--solver framework. The proposer generates a question, an answer, and a verbatim evidence span. An evidence verifier then rewards the span according to the marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal that favors evidence that genuinely helps answer the question, without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations. EVE-Agent leaves the backbone model, retriever, search tool, and optimization framework unchanged. Experiments show that EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction: each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

</details>


### [18] [The Deterministic Horizon: Impossibility Results as Design Specifications for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23024)
*Dongxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文将计算理论中的不可能性结果转化为AI系统的可计算设计规范。核心发现证明Transformer架构存在由层深和嵌入维度决定的确定性推理深度上限，超过该界限后训练无法提升性能，并在12种架构中测得该界限为19-31层。论文共提出16条设计规范，涵盖推理深度、偏好学习、检索管道、拍卖机制和零知识证明等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛应用于软件编写、法律文档起草和临床记录生成等关键领域，但计算理论中的基本限制（如图灵极限、阿罗定理、无免费午餐定理等）约束着AI系统的能力边界。当前研究将这些不可能性结果视为理论好奇，而未将其转化为实用的设计准则。作者旨在建立一套方法论，将抽象的计算限制转化为可计算、可验证的系统设计规范，为可信AI研究提供理论基础。

Method: 论文采用理论证明与实证测量相结合的方法：1) 基于残差流的容量不变性原理，推导架构决定的推理深度上界（确定性视界），并通过信息论分析得出超过该界限后的超指数级精度衰减；2) 提出模幂运算在恒定深度素数模电路上的无条件电路复杂度下界；3) 将类似论证框架扩展至偏好学习（样本复杂度的跳变）、多阶段检索（独立度量数量下界）、拍卖机制（提示依赖估值的诚实性失效）和零知识证明（神经网络推理验证开销）等子领域；4) 构建包含可计算边界、量化违规成本和构造性设计规则的规范目录。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 在12种Transformer架构中测得确定性视界为19-31层，在最优长度轨迹上微调仅能恢复不足4个百分点的性能；2) 证明模幂运算的电路复杂度下界；3) 发现模型误设下偏好学习的样本复杂度呈现跳变式增长；4) 证明多阶段检索管道需要至少与阶段数相等的独立度量；5) 揭示提示依赖估值下标准诚实拍卖失效；6) 测得神经网络推理零知识验证开销为每个非线性激活110-190倍。共形成16条规范，其中2条组合已证明，1条为诚实阻碍，4条仍待解决。

Conclusion: 论文提出了"不可能性规范"方法论，将计算理论的根本限制转化为可操作的设计规则。每条规范包含可计算的边界条件、量化的违规成本以及构造性的设计准则。这种方法论为生成式AI研究提供了理论框架，表明AI系统的根本限制同时也是其设计准则。论文为可信AI系统开发提供了基于计算理论的规范目录，填补了理论限制与工程实践之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Large language models now write software, draft legal documents, and produce clinical notes, yet fundamental limits, from Turing and Arrow to the No Free Lunch theorems, shape what computation can do. This thesis turns such impossibility results from curiosities into design rules. Its flagship result proves an accuracy ceiling set by architecture alone: past a critical reasoning depth, no amount of training moves it, at any adapter rank, sample size, or loss function. Computable before deployment from layer count and embedding width, this Deterministic Horizon is measured between nineteen and thirty-one across twelve transformer architectures, and fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovers under four percentage points. The mechanism is a capacity invariant of the residual stream, and an information-theoretic conversion yields super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon. An unconditional circuit-complexity lower bound for modular exponentiation against constant-depth prime-modulus circuits complements this result. The same argument recasts across subfields: preference learning under any misspecified model jumps discontinuously in sample complexity; multi-stage retrieval pipelines require at least as many independent metrics as stages; standard truthful auctions fail for agents with prompt-dependent valuations; and zero-knowledge verification of neural inference pays a measured overhead of one hundred ten to one hundred ninety times per non-linear activation. Together these form a catalogue of sixteen specifications, each pairing a computable boundary, a quantified violation cost, and a constructive design rule: two compositions are proved, one pairing is an honest obstruction, and four remain open. The impossibility-specification methodology is offered for the generative research programme that trustworthy AI may need. Every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule.

</details>


### [19] [PathCal: State-Aware Reflection-Marker Calibration for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23074)
*Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Tsubasa Takahashi,Dengzhe Hou,Zhengzhong Tu,Kazunori Yamada,Fangzhou Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PathCal，一种无需训练的解码控制器，通过区分不同类型的反思标记（如"wait"、"but"、"alternatively"）并在局部不确定状态进行干预，实现了更好的推理效率-性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理语言模型（LRMs）在推理过程中会产生包含反思标记的长链式思维（CoT）轨迹，但现有研究将这些标记视为单一粗粒度类别，忽略了它们不同的功能角色。本文旨在揭示不同反思标记的独特作用及其影响时机，并据此设计更精细的推理路径控制方法。

Method: 本文提出PathCal方法：1）通过类型级抑制和固定前缀干预揭示反思标记的功能差异；2）在解码时区分标记类型，仅在局部不确定状态进行干预；3）利用反思标记的分布估计当前推理路径与竞争分支之间的局部竞争，当竞争分支证据过多时对标记logits进行软性重平衡。该方法无需外部验证器或额外采样。

Result: 在六个推理基准测试上，PathCal实现了更好的效率-性能权衡：在保持或提升准确率的同时减少了生成长度，无需依赖外部验证器或额外采样。不同类型的反思标记对准确率和生成长度的影响方式不同，且标记选择在模型进入稳定推理轨迹前最为关键。

Conclusion: 不同反思标记具有不同的功能角色和影响时机，区分这些标记类型并针对性地进行干预能够有效提升大推理语言模型的推理效率。PathCal作为一种无需训练的解码控制器，为测试时推理路径控制提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Language Models (LRMs) has paved the way for tackling complex reasoning tasks through test-time scaling by generating long-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories during inference. Meanwhile, these trajectories often contain explicit reflection markers such as ``wait'', ``but'', and ``alternatively'', signaling hesitation, revision, and the consideration of alternative explorations, respectively. Recent studies on test-time control leverage such markers as lightweight handles for steering reasoning, typically treating them as a single coarse-grained category rather than distinguishing their distinct functional roles. In this paper, we conduct type-wise suppression and fixed-prefix intervention, revealing that reflection markers differ not only in their functional roles but also in when they exert the greatest influence. Specifically, different marker classes affect accuracy and generation length in distinct ways, and marker choices are most consequential before the model settles into a stable reasoning trajectory. Motivated by these findings, we introduce PathCal, a novel training-free decoding controller that calibrates reasoning paths by distinguishing marker types and intervening only at locally uncertain states. At each decoding step, PathCal utilizes the distribution over reflection-markers to estimate local competition between maintaining the current reasoning trajectory and initiating a competing branch, and softly rebalances marker logits when competing-branch evidence becomes excessive. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PathCal achieves a better efficiency--performance trade-off, improving or preserving accuracy while reducing generation length, without relying on external verifiers or additional sampling.

</details>


### [20] [Inductive Deductive Synthesis: Enabling AI to Generate Formally Verified Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23109)
*Shubham Agarwal,Alexander Krentsel,Shu Liu,Mert Cemri,Audrey Cheng,Rui Meng,Tomas Pfister,Chun-Liang Li,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Aditya Parameswaran,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica,Mohsen Lesani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IDS（归纳演绎合成）方法，这是首个有效解决AI代理在形式化验证方面不足的方法。IDS通过联合增量式合成实现与证明，并从失败中学习，在分布式键值存储规范任务上达到7/7的成功率，平均耗时6.8小时，比专家快约200倍，成本降低17%，生成的实现性能比已验证系统快3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理在代码生成、测试和优化方面表现出色，但在需要全覆盖形式化保证的任务上存在不足。分布式系统需要在所有可能的事件交错下保证正确性（如读写一致性），而机械化形式验证通常需要数月到数年的专家努力。现有SOTA编码代理（Codex with GPT-5.4和Claude Code with Opus 4.6）在7个分布式键值存储规范中仅成功2个，凸显了这一差距。

Method: 提出了归纳演绎合成（IDS）方法，这是一个智能体LLM系统。该方法联合且增量式地合成实现和证明，通过从失败尝试中学习来系统性地尝试有前景的策略。IDS还将性能反馈纳入同一循环中，实现实现与证明的协同优化。

Result: IDS在7个分布式键值存储规范任务上实现了7/7的完美成功率。平均每个规范耗时约6.8小时，成本约106美元，相比专家努力速度提升约200倍，相比SOTA代理成本降低17%。进一步，生成的实现性能比已发布的验证系统快达3倍。

Conclusion: IDS填补了AI代理在形式化验证方面的关键空白，首次实现了对分布式系统规范的高效自动验证。该方法在速度、成本和性能方面均显著优于现有方法，为自动化形式化验证提供了可行的解决方案，有望大幅降低分布式系统正确性验证的门槛。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly excel at generating, testing, and refining code. However, they fall short on tasks requiring formal guarantees of full coverage that testing alone cannot provide. Distributed systems are a prime example: properties such as consistency between reads and writes must hold under every possible interleaving of events. Mechanized formal verification can guarantee such correctness, but typically demands months to years of expert effort. As evidence, even SOTA coding agents (Codex with GPT-5.4 and Claude Code with Opus 4.6) succeed on only 2/7 distributed key-value-store specifications. In this paper, we present the first effective approach to addressing this gap, Inductive Deductive Synthesis (IDS), which jointly and incrementally synthesizes implementation and proof, and learns from failed attempts to systematically try promising strategies. Built as an agentic LLM system, IDS achieves 7/7 in about 6.8 hours and $106 per spec on average, roughly 200x faster than expert effort and 17% cheaper than SOTA agents. IDS further incorporates performance feedback into the same loop, yielding implementations up to 3x faster than published verified systems.

</details>


### [21] [Redrawing the AI Map: A Theory of Accountability Boundaries in Agentic Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23179)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Farooq Muzaffar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于能力的代理生态系统问责边界理论，指出代理AI编排器虽然降低了系统集成成本，但需要问责资产的验证成本和责任可转移性决定边界策略（组件型、集成型、双轨型），解释了数字模块化何时延伸到组织解绑、何时因问责要求保持能力集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI编排器看似加速了跨组织边界的能力模块化和组织解绑，但需要证据、审查、签字或可分配责任的AI能力，即使技术接口模块化，仍可能保持集成的问责边界，这一矛盾现象需要理论解释。

Method: 整合数字创新、交易成本、互补资产、数字平台治理和信息系统控制视角，提出问责资产概念（使AI输出合法、可审计、可审查、可分配责任），通过验证成本和责任可转移性两个维度确定边界策略，构建七个命题连接代理组装成本降低、问责资产、适当性、编排器意图捕获和边界误配置与边界策略、价值占有和规则债务的关系，通过文档处理、法律服务、审计、临床决策支持和采购等场景进行结构化说明。

Result: 识别出三种边界策略（组件型、集成型、双轨型）；引入规则债务概念（组织决策规则从正式信息系统迁移到不受治理的代理执行环境时累积的治理负担）；阐明验证成本和责任可转移性决定执行边界与问责边界能否同步移动；揭示边界误配置对价值占有和规则债务的影响机制。

Conclusion: 该理论解释了数字模块化何时延伸到组织解绑、何时因问责要求保持能力集成，为代理生态系统的能力级问责边界配置提供了理论框架，指导组织在追求模块化效率与维持问责完整性之间做出战略选择。

Abstract: Agentic AI orchestrators reduce the interface and assembly costs of composing information systems capabilities across organizational boundaries, seemingly accelerating modularization and organizational disaggregation. Yet AI-enabled capabilities whose outputs require evidence, review, signoff, or assignable responsibility may retain integrated accountability boundaries even when their technical interfaces become modular. We develop a capability-level theory of accountability-boundary placement in agentic ecosystems. We introduce accountability assets: complementary assets that make AI-supported outputs legitimate, auditable, reviewable, and assignable to a responsible party. We argue that verification cost and responsibility transferability determine whether the execution and accountability boundaries can move together. The theory identifies three boundary strategies: component, integrated, and dual-track. It also introduces rule debt, the governance burden that accrues when organizational decision rules migrate from formal information systems into ungoverned agentic execution environments. Integrating digital innovation, transaction cost, complementary-assets, digital platform governance, and IS control perspectives, we develop seven propositions linking agentic assembly-cost reductions, accountability assets, appropriability, orchestrator intent capture, and boundary misconfiguration to boundary strategy, value appropriation, and rule debt. The theory explains when digital modularization extends to organizational disaggregation and when accountability keeps capabilities integrated. Structured illustrations across document processing, legal services, audit, clinical decision support, and procurement discipline the boundary logic.

</details>


### [22] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述提出AutoResearch概念，系统分析了AI驱动的科研工作流自动化的发展谱系，从人类主导的Vibe Research到新兴的AI主导系统，梳理了科研工作流的五个核心条件与评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科研系统呈现碎片化特征，在自主性、领域范围、执行环境、验证机制和人类监督等方面存在差异，同时面临证据保存、可复现性、弱方向拒绝、溯源追踪、跨领域鲁棒性和可问责科学闭环等挑战，亟需系统性地梳理和规范这一新兴领域。

Method: 提出AutoResearch概念框架，将AI科研系统分为人类主导的Vibe Research和新兴AI主导系统；围绕五个工作流条件（文献与研究基础、假设形成与规划、实验与工具使用、反馈验证与评审、报告与知识传播）组织研究领域；综合分析AI科学家系统、混合主动协同研究框架、基准测试、领域部署和开源基础设施。

Result: 提出五个评估维度：新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性和溯源性；发现AutoResearch的自主性具有领域条件依赖性：在结构化、可执行、快速可验证的场景中更具可信度，而在具身化、延迟性、异质性、伦理性和机构问责场景中仍受限。

Conclusion: AI驱动的科研工作流自动化正在从任务级辅助向工作流级自动化转型，但当前系统的自主性仍受领域特性约束，需要在证据保存、可复现性和可问责性等方面持续改进，未来发展方向应注重结构化环境与人类监督的平衡。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>
