*Bang Liu,Yongfeng Gu,Jiayi Zhang,Zhaoyang Yu,Sirui Hong,Maojia Song,Xiaoqiang Wang,Mingyi Deng,Zijie Zhuang,Ronghao Wang,Mingzhe Cao,Yutong Zhu,Xingjian Li,Yifan Wu,Jianhao Ruan,Yiran Peng,Shuangrui Chen,Jinlin Wang,Yizhang Lin,Dongjie Zhang,Dekun Wu,Chen Ma,Lizi Liao,Han Yu,Jian Pei,Heng Ji,Qiang Yang,Yuyu Luo,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基础协议(FP)，这是一种面向人机社会的图优先协调层，旨在解决自主智能体规模化后从模型能力向协调能力的瓶颈转变问题，通过统一异构实体、支持多方组织、提供经济原语和治理机制，实现自主性和可问责性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体从工具演变为社会基础设施层，它们开始浏览、购买、部署软件、管理系统并相互交互。当这些系统规模化时，瓶颈从原始模型能力转向协调能力。智能体需要形成可靠的关系、组织多智能体工作、交换价值、支持AI经济，并在现实世界的监督下保持安全和可问责。

Method: 引入基础协议(FP)，一个图优先协调层。FP统一异构实体（包括智能体、工具、资源、人类、机构和组织），支持原生多方组织和基于事件的协作。提供计量、收据和结算的经济原语，并将策略、溯源和审计作为一等关注点。设计上采用包装和桥接现有协议而非替换的方式，支持增量采用并降低集成和治理开销。

Result: FP成功提供了一个协调层框架，能够在保持可组合性的同时确保可问责性不可妥协，使协调本身成为开放、多元和可治理的人机社会的共享基础设施。

Conclusion: 基础协议旨在通过统一的协调层解决自主智能体规模化后的协调挑战，实现自主性和可问责性的平衡，为开放、多元、可治理的人机社会奠定基础设施基础，支持AI经济的可持续发展。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are moving from tools into a layer of social infrastructure: they browse, purchase, deploy software, manage systems, and increasingly interact with one another. As these systems scale, the bottleneck shifts away from raw model capability toward coordination. Agents need to form reliable relationships, organize multi-agent work, exchange value, support an AI economy, and stay safe and accountable under real-world oversight. This paper introduces the Foundation Protocol (FP), a graph-first coordination layer for an emerging human-AI society. FP unifies heterogeneous entities, including agents, tools, resources, humans, institutions, and organizations, and supports native multi-party organization and event-based collaboration. It also provides economic primitives for metering, receipts, and settlement, and treats policy, provenance, and audit as first-class concerns. FP is designed to wrap and bridge existing protocols rather than replace them, enabling incremental adoption while reducing integration and governance overhead. The aim is to keep autonomous agency composable while keeping accountability non-negotiable, so that coordination itself can become shared infrastructure for a human-AI society that is open, pluralistic, and governable.

</details>


### [24] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为本体知识块（OKB）的可编程治理基础设施，将AI服务的监管义务编译为机器可检查的结构化约束，实现了从文档中心的人工合规向自动化验证的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统的治理合规流程高度依赖文档、静态检查清单和人工审查，这种以文档为中心的方法无法扩展到自动化AI系统，亟需一种可编程、可扩展的治理基础设施。

Method: 形式化定义OKB为五元组，将规范性义务绑定到RDF/OWL概念模式、可执行的SHACL验证规则、明确的证据需求和PROV-O溯源链接；通过确定性监管编译器将结构化中间表示记录转换为可组合的知识块模块，实现基于配置文件的治理重配置。

Result: 在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中进行了24次验证运行和四种治理配置测试，实现了配置敏感验证、严格的违规累加、12.6ms至100.3ms的SHACL验证延迟，配置等价性测试确认Combined配置为最全面的严格配置。

Conclusion: OKB基础设施成功实现了监管义务的机器可检查验证，为AI系统提供了可扩展、可组合的自动化治理方案，所有成果已开源发布。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [25] [Design and Report Benchmarks for Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23262)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套三步方法论，用于明确基准测试任务如何代表其所声称的知识工作能力，通过定义工作活动、明确测试设置和评分工作产出，使基准测试结果更能反映真实的知识工作部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识工作AI系统的评估和基准测试设计仍沿袭传统NLP任务的逻辑，导致更高的基准测试分数并不能可靠地表明系统在实际部署环境中能够执行知识工作，存在基准测试任务与现实工作场景脱节的问题。

Method: 提出三步方法：1）定义所评估的工作活动（从O{*}NET职业任务数据库中提取18项工作活动清单）；2）明确测试设置（包括材料、工具、角色和约束）；3）对适当的工作产出进行评分。通过GDPval、OfficeQA Pro和APEX-SWE三个基准测试案例进行验证分析。

Result: 发现知识工作通过角色职责、本地材料工具和下游工作流中可用的工件来组织；展示了基准测试设计选择如何影响分数所能支持的最强工作声明，以及基准测试任务、测试设置、评分产品和更广泛工作声明之间存在的差距。

Conclusion: 基准测试设计需要明确映射任务与工作活动的关系，测试设置应详细说明材料、工具、角色和约束，评分应聚焦于系统产出的工作产品，从而弥合基准测试分数与实际知识工作能力之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The development of LLM agents has led to a growing body of work on knowledge-work AI, including coding, research, and healthcare. However, current knowledge-work evaluation and benchmark design still largely follow the logic of traditional NLP tasks. As a result, higher benchmark performance does not reliably show that a system can carry out knowledge work in real-world deployment settings. This paper contributes a three-step approach for making explicit how benchmarked tasks represent the work claims attached to their scores: defining the work activity under evaluation, specifying the tested setting, and scoring the appropriate work product. We review work studies showing that knowledge work is organized through roles and responsibilities, local materials and tools, and artifacts that must remain usable in downstream workflows. We then translate these concerns into benchmark design and reporting guidance, covering how tasks should be mapped to work activities, how tested settings should specify materials, tools, roles, and constraints, and how scoring should focus on the work product left by the system. To name the work activity being evaluated and distinguish it from common benchmark tasks, we derive an inventory of 18 work activities from the O{*}NET occupational task database. We demonstrate the approach through three benchmark case analyses: GDPval, a non-code occupational deliverable benchmark; OfficeQA Pro, a grounded document-analysis benchmark scored by final answers; and APEX-SWE, a software-engineering benchmark with executable scored products. These cases show how benchmark design choices shape the strongest work claim a score can support, and where gaps arise between the benchmarked task, tested setting, scored product, and broader work claim.

</details>


### [26] [Parallel Context Compaction for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23296)
*Musa Cim,Burak Topcu,Chita Das,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行压缩方法解决长时序LLM智能体上下文溢出问题，在匹配解码量下减少端到端时间并提升压缩吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序LLM智能体的对话历史会超出上下文窗口限制。现有的基于LLM摘要的顺序同步压缩方法存在三个问题：摘要过程具有固有损失性；阻塞调用会导致智能体推理停滞数十秒；操作者难以精细控制摘要量，导致模型保留的知识在不同运行间不可预测。

Method: 提出并行压缩方法用于长时序智能体流程，并在四个骨干模型（参数量从8B到120B，涵盖dense和MoE架构，包括推理和非推理模型）上，使用HotpotQA多跳QA和LoCoMo长上下文对话基准进行实验对比。

Result: 在匹配压缩解码量的条件下，并行压缩方法减少了端到端实际耗时，提升了压缩吞吐量，同时赋予操作者对摘要量更精细、可预测的控制能力，并支持针对每个模块进行更有针对性的提示工程。

Conclusion: 并行压缩方法有效解决了顺序同步压缩的阻塞和可控性问题，在保持压缩质量的同时提升了系统效率，为长时序LLM智能体提供了一种更优的上下文管理方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate growing conversation histories that eventually exceed the model's context window. Context compaction via LLM-based summarization keeps the conversation bounded, but summarization is inherently lossy and the blocking call stalls agent inference for tens of seconds. Moreover, the operator has no fine-grained control over summary volume since prompt instructions are largely ignored, and as context grows, both the amount of output tokens the model produces and the information it retains fluctuate substantially from run to run, making the agent's retained knowledge unpredictable across runs. We introduce \textbf{parallel compaction} for long-horizon agentic flows and characterize it against the sequential synchronous baseline across four backbones spanning 8B to 120B parameters, mixing dense and MoE architectures with reasoning and non-reasoning models, on the HotpotQA multi-hop QA and LoCoMo long-context dialogue benchmarks. Parallel compaction gives the operator fine-grained, predictable control over summary volume and enables more targeted prompt engineering per block. At matched compaction decode volume, it reduces end-to-end wall time and improves compaction throughput over the sequential baseline.

</details>


### [27] [DART: Semantic Recoverability for Structured Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23311)
*Ke Yang,Panpan Li,Zonghan Wu,Kejin Xu,Huaxi Huang,Xiaoshui Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了语义可恢复性概念，并开发了DART运行时系统，通过显式的可接受性检查来解决结构化工具代理在承诺敏感场景下的安全局部恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复机制存在关键缺陷：完全重放任务安全但浪费资源，局部检查点恢复高效但可能导致已提交的下游工作与不存在的上游历史绑定。在承诺敏感场景中，这种矛盾尤为突出，因为回滚针对单个失败实例，但下游消费者已基于其输出采取了行动。现有方法仅提供机械回滚，缺乏判断局部恢复是否保持语义有效性的标准。

Method: 提出语义可恢复性概念并开发DART模块化运行时系统。DART包含四个核心组件：(1)定位失败实例；(2)认证该实例的语义可恢复边界；(3)将检查点与这些边界对齐；(4)在依赖和效果约束下选择保留已提交下游工作的可接受恢复点，否则阻止恢复。系统通过显式的可接受性检查确保局部恢复的语义有效性。

Result: 在三个LLM驱动领域和基于LangGraph基板的外部验证中，DART在所有评估的承诺敏感案例中均正确恢复，而基线局部恢复方法在这些案例上均失败。五域安全审计未发现任何不安全的已准入回滚，证明了系统的安全性。实验表明控制器合法性与语义有效性不等价。

Conclusion: 控制器合法性并不意味着语义有效性，可靠的局部恢复需要显式的可接受性检查。DART成功填补了语义可恢复性这一空白，为承诺敏感场景下的结构化工具代理提供了既安全又高效的恢复机制。

Abstract: When a structured tool agent fails mid-execution, the runtime faces a dilemma: replaying the entire task is safe but wasteful, while restoring from a local checkpoint is efficient but can leave committed downstream work tied to an upstream history that no longer exists. This tension is acute in commitment-sensitive settings, where rollback targets a single failed instance yet downstream consumers have already acted on its output. Existing recovery approaches provide mechanical rollback but no criterion for whether a local restore remains semantically valid after downstream commitment. We formalize this gap as semantic recoverability and address it in DART, a modular runtime that localizes the failed instance, certifies semantically recoverable boundaries of that instance, aligns checkpoints to those boundaries, and selects an admissible restore point that preserves committed downstream work under dependency and effect constraints-or blocks otherwise. Across three LLM-driven domains and external validation on a LangGraph-based substrate, DART correctly recovers all evaluated commitment-sensitive cases where baseline local recovery fails, and a five-domain safety audit finds no unsafe admitted rollbacks. These results show that controller legality does not imply semantic validity, and that sound local recovery requires an explicit admissibility check.

</details>


### [28] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VDSS，一种人机多智能体呼吸机决策支持框架，通过结构化接口协调决策组件，实现在线偏好适应，提高了临床可用性和交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的方法难以实现个性化，端到端强化学习或单一大型语言模型系统难以控制和审计。呼吸机决策支持需要跟踪生理和疾病轨迹的顺序决策，同时尊重安全边界和临床医生的个性化调整风格。

Method: 提出呼吸机决策支持系统（VDSS），一种人在回路的多智能体框架。通过契约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，生成可追溯的证据。使用上下文赌博机进行在线偏好适应，根据每次调整周期接受的最终决策更新临床医生特定偏好，引导后续推荐。结构化拒绝反馈触发定向重新规划，减少无效迭代。

Result: 在ICU轨迹回放和专家评审的回顾性研究中，VDSS表现出更高的推荐接受度，达到可接受方案所需的交互轮次更少。

Conclusion: VDSS支持临床上可部署的人机协作，实现了个性化、可控且可审计的呼吸机决策支持。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [29] [When Planning Fails Despite Correct Execution: On Epistemic Calibration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23414)
*Zehao Wang,Shilong Jin,Zhao Cao,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EPC-AW框架解决LLM多智能体系统中的认知校准失误问题，通过信息一致性计划选择和一致性引导的认知状态优化，使系统成功率平均提升9.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统存在认知校准失误（epistemic miscalibration）问题：智能体在评估计划可行性时会误判自身知识。与执行错误不同，这种失误在规划阶段是隐性的，生成的计划可能保持自洽且可执行而不产生可观察错误；同时具有动态性，新信息可能改变可行性评估，掩盖过去的校准失误信号并导致其反复出现。

Method: 提出认知规划校准智能体工作流（EPC-AW），包含两个核心组件：(1)基于信息一致性的计划选择，选择在智能体间评估稳定的计划；(2)一致性引导的认知状态优化，利用过去的差异指导未来规划，随时间自适应调整校准。该方法评估计划在不同信息条件下是否保持受支持，而非直接验证可行性。

Result: 实验表明，EPC-AW使系统级成功率平均提升9.75%。

Conclusion: EPC-AW有效解决了LLM多智能体系统中的认知校准失误问题，通过评估计划在不同信息条件下的稳定性并利用历史差异进行自适应优化，显著提升了系统的整体性能。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when planned actions are executed correctly because agents may misjudge their knowledge when evaluating plan feasibility, a phenomenon we term epistemic miscalibration in planning. Unlike execution errors, epistemic miscalibration is latent during planning, as generated plans can remain self-consistent and executable without observable errors; the miscalibration is also dynamic, as new information can alter feasibility assessments, potentially obscuring past miscalibration signals and causing them to recur over time. To address this, we propose the Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW), which assesses whether plans remain supported under varying information conditions rather than directly verifying feasibility. EPC-AW employs Information-consistency-based Plan Selection, selecting plans whose evaluations are stable across agents, together with Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement to adapt calibration over time by leveraging past discrepancies to guide future planning. Experiments show that EPC-AW improves system-level success by an average of 9.75%.

</details>


### [30] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGE-OPD方法，通过引导式rollout和证据掩码机制，解决了传统OPSD在学习稀有token/身份设定时无法有效学习且容易产生副作用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法在利用特权上下文（如人设、私有事实）训练学生模型时面临挑战：特权信息可能导致超出预期的行为变化，包括修改推理过程、降低通用能力，以及影响响应长度、风格或局部token偏好等性能指标，使学生模型学习到副作用而非期望的可迁移行为，特别是在稀有token/身份设定下完全失败。

Method: 提出证据引导的在策略蒸馏（EDGE-OPD），包含两个核心组件：a) 引导式rollout，在采样时注入特权上下文行为，确保稀有目标行为实际存在于在策略数据中；b) 证据掩码，学生模型仅在特权上下文支持采样token的位置进行更新，而非rollout中的每个token位置。

Result: 实验表明：OPSD及其变体RLSD（无论有无验证器）完全无法学习目标身份，而集成引导式rollout后能够成功学习；掩码区域消融实验显示人设信号定位于正证据尾部，证明了该方法在学习目标行为的同时有效保留通用能力。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD通过引导式rollout和证据掩码的组合，实现了特权上下文行为向学生模型的有效迁移，同时避免了对通用能力的损害，为高效知识迁移和通用能力保持提供了有价值的见解和方法论基础。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [31] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态规划(DP)作为主搜索框架、约束规划(CP)作为子程序以利用全局约束传播的混合方法，并在部分车间调度问题(PSSP)上验证了该方法的可行性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划与约束规划是求解组合优化问题的两种成熟范式，但通常各自独立使用。本文旨在展示这两种方法可以优雅且有效地结合，充分发挥各自优势。

Method: 以DP作为主搜索框架，将CP作为子程序利用其高效的全局约束传播算法。该方法应用于部分车间调度问题(PSSP)，支持任意优先约束图，并可兼容随时DP策略（如随时列搜索）。此外，利用CP建模的灵活性设计了大规模邻域搜索(LNS)方案，在重启间重用DP模型并施加偏序调度以改进当前解。

Result: 在PSSP上实现了该混合方法，展示了能够自然处理任意优先约束图，并成功集成了LNS改进策略。虽然在求解效率上尚未达到针对该问题的最先进纯CP求解器的水平，但验证了混合集成的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于证明了DP与CP混合集成的可行性与潜力，为后续进一步优化与拓展提供了基础框架，而非追求在特定问题上超越现有的纯CP求解器。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [32] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Co-ReAct框架，通过在推理时注入细粒度评分标准来指导智能体的每一步行动决策，在DeepResearchBench和SQA-CS-V2上持续优于ReAct及测试时计算基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct风格的智能体在搜索密集型多步推理任务中过度依赖自身判断来决定证据搜索、行动选择和停止时机，常产生浅显、冗余或目标不明确的轨迹。现有评分标准的使用主要是评估性的（作为训练奖励或事后评估器），而非行动指导性的，且在深度研究设置中通常是粗粒度、报告级别的，而非步骤级别的。

Method: 提出Co-ReAct框架，在推理的每个决策步骤将评分标准注入智能体上下文，指导下一步的推理或行动决策（证据搜索、查询、推理或自评估）。训练专用的评分标准生成器，采用GRPO方法，基于多专家共识排序优化列表级Spearman秩相关奖励，而非传统的成对或二值偏好优化，从而生成具有判别力而非仅合理的评分标准。

Result: 在DeepResearchBench和SQA-CS-V2数据集上，基于8B/14B开源模型和前沿闭源模型构建的搜索智能体上，Co-ReAct均持续优于ReAct和代表性测试时计算基线。训练好的评分标准生成器可作为即插即用组件提升基线性能，无需改变其底层决策机制。

Conclusion: 将评分标准作为步骤级指导信号引入推理过程是提升ReAct风格智能体性能的有效方法，通过判别性评分标准生成器的训练，可以显著改善多步推理任务中的行动选择质量，且具有良好的通用性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [33] [Solving the Aircraft Disassembly Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23592)
*Charles Thomas,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对飞机报废拆解调度问题，提出了约束规划（CP）模型和混合整数规划（MIP）模型两种求解方法，并在包含多达1450个任务的真实工业数据实例上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机报废拆解过程复杂，虽对可持续发展至关重要，但对航空公司而言利润微薄。高效的拆解调度对确保流程盈利性和激励实践操作至关重要，因此需要针对大规模、多约束的拆解任务进行优化调度。

Method: 针对飞机拆解调度问题，考虑了技师资质认证、设备需求、任务优先级关系、飞机平衡约束以及作业空间受限等约束条件，提出了约束规划（CP）模型和混合整数规划（MIP）模型两种建模求解方法。

Result: 在基于工业合作伙伴提供的真实运营数据构建的不同规模实例上进行了测试，实例包含多达1450个拆解任务，验证了所提模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了能够处理大规模飞机拆解调度问题的数学模型，为航空业提供了实用的优化工具，有助于提高拆解过程的盈利性和可持续性。

Abstract: Dismantling aircrafts reaching their end of life is a complex endeavour that is necessary in terms of sustainability but yields small income margins for air transport companies. An efficient scheduling of the disassembly procedure is thus crucial to ensure the profitability of the process and incentivize practice. This is a large scheduling problem that involves thousands of tasks and many different constraints: Extracting parts that are destined to be reused requires technicians with specific certifications and equipment. Extraction operations might be subject to precedence relations. Furthermore, the aircraft must be kept balanced during the whole process. Finally, some of the locations of the aircraft have a limited space that caps the number of technicians able to work there concurrently. This article presents the problem in details and proposes two approaches to solve the problem: a Constraint Programming model and a MIP model. The models are tested on instances of varying sizes involving up to 1450 tasks, which are based on real operational data provided by an industrial partner.

</details>


### [34] [One Policy, Infinite NPCs: Persona-Traceable Shared RL Policies for Scalable Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23652)
*Yoosung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCSP方法，通过单一强化学习策略实现可扩展、实时且个性条件化的NPC控制，在300人人生模拟基准测试中，其组合零样本角色识别能力比随机高17倍，推理速度比LLM基线快22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人生模拟游戏需要成百上千个非玩家角色（NPC），这些角色既要表现一致的独特个性，又要通过设计师编写的自然语言保持可控性。现有方法无法同时满足角色一致性、可控性和实时推理等约束条件。

Method: 引入PCSP（Persona Conditioned Shared Policy），一种基于冻结的LLM自由形式角色描述嵌入作为条件的单一强化学习策略。方法包括：每位NPC一次性的角色编码、低秩角色投影、神经角色条件化，以及PPO + InfoNCE一致性 + KL多样性的训练目标。

Result: 在300人人生模拟基准测试中，PCSP实现组合零样本角色识别比随机高17倍，Spearman rho约0.73的语义-行为对齐度，推理速度比LLM-as-policy基线快22倍。消融实验表明InfoNCE轨迹一致性目标至关重要，移除后零样本角色识别降至随机水平。在Melting Pot 2.4.0环境的外部验证确认了多智能体战略环境中的角色条件化行为差异。UE5部署在64个智能体下重现了引擎内角色条件化消融实验，失败率低，证明亚帧推理轮廓在商业游戏引擎中可行。

Conclusion: 证明了共享强化学习策略能够支持可扩展、实时、角色条件化的NPC控制，为游戏中的大规模个性化和可控NPC行为提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: On a 300-persona life-simulation benchmark, pcsp achieves compositional zero-shot persona identification up to 17x above chance, Spearman rho approx 0.73 semantic-behavioral alignment, and 22x faster inference than an LLM-as-policy baseline. Life simulation games require hundreds to thousands of non-player characters (NPCs) that behave consistently with distinct personalities while remaining controllable through designer-authored natural language. Existing methods fail on constraints like persona consistency, controllability, or real-time inference. We introduce pcsp (Persona Conditioned Shared Policy), a single reinforcement learning policy conditioned on frozen LLM embeddings of free-form persona descriptions. pcsp combines once-per-NPC persona encoding, low-rank persona projection, neural persona conditioning, and a PPO + InfoNCE consistency + KL diversity training objective. Across three experimental settings, ablations show that the InfoNCE trajectory-consistency objective is load bearing: removing it collapses zero-shot persona identification to chance. External validation on Melting Pot 2.4.0 substrates confirms that our method produces persona-conditioned behavioral divergence in multi-agent strategic environments. We distinguish two senses of held-out evaluation: compositional zero-shot and vocabulary-expansion held-out. Finally, a UE5 deployment reproduces the in-engine persona-conditioning ablation at 64 agents with a low failure rate, showing that the sub-frame inference profile survives in a commercial game engine. These results prove that shared RL policies can support scalable, real-time, persona-conditioned NPC control.

</details>


### [35] [MemAudit: Post-hoc Auditing of Poisoned Agent Memory via Causal Attribution and Structural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23723)
*Zhewen Tan,Yilun Yao,Huiyan Jin,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Mengyuan Fan,liang lu,Feng Liu,Xiangzheng Zhang,Duohe Ma,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemAudit，一个针对记忆增强型LLM智能体的后验因果记忆审计框架，通过结合反事实记忆影响评分和记忆一致性图来识别导致有害行为的恶意记忆，成功将查询攻击成功率从70%降至0%，推理攻击成功率从83.3%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体依赖持久化记忆存储过去交互、检索相关示例并改进长期任务执行，但这一机制存在安全漏洞：对抗用户可通过普通交互向智能体记忆注入恶意记录，这些记录随后被检索以操纵智能体的推理和行为。现有防御主要关注在线干预（如提示过滤或输出阻断），无法解决有害行为发生后确定哪些存储记忆应负责的事后问题。

Method: 提出MemAudit后验因果记忆审计框架，结合两个互补信号：(1)反事实记忆影响评分，衡量每个记忆对有害输出的因果贡献；(2)记忆一致性图，识别记忆库中结构异常的记忆。针对MINJA仅查询记忆注入攻击进行评估，该攻击通过正常智能体交互而非直接修改记忆库来生成和存储恶意记录。

Result: 在问答(QA)和推理智能体(RAP)两种设置下，MemAudit在现实后验审计场景中大幅降低攻击成功率。QA攻击成功率从70%降至0%，RAP攻击成功率从83.3%降至0%。

Conclusion: MemAudit为记忆增强型LLM智能体提供了有效的后验安全审计能力，通过因果分析和结构异常检测成功识别并缓解了记忆注入攻击，填补了现有在线干预方法无法处理事后责任归属问题的空白。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to store past interactions, retrieve relevant demonstrations, and improve long-horizon task execution. However, this memory mechanism also creates a practical security vulnerability: an adversarial user may inject malicious records into the agent's memory through ordinary interaction, and these records can later be retrieved to steer the agent's reasoning and actions. Existing defenses primarily focus on online intervention, such as prompt filtering or output blocking, but they do not address the post-hoc question of which stored memories are responsible after harmful behavior has already been observed. We propose \textbf{MemAudit}, a post-hoc causal memory auditing framework for memory-augmented LLM agents. The framework combines two complementary signals: (1) a counterfactual memory influence score that measures each memory's causal contribution to harmful outputs, and (2) a memory consistency graph that identifies structurally anomalous memories within the broader memory store. We evaluate MemAudit against MINJA, a query-only memory injection attack in which malicious records are generated and stored through normal agent interactions rather than direct memory-bank modification. Across both QA and reasoning-agent settings, MemAudit substantially reduces attack success rates under realistic post-hoc auditing scenarios. The results show that QA attack success is reduced from $70\%$ to $0\%$, while RAP attack success drops from $83.3\%$ to $0\%$.

</details>


### [36] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了Claude Code在CLEVER基准（Lean 4可验证代码生成基准）上的表现，发现其在端到端程序生成与验证流程中达到98.1%的成功率，表明现代智能体证明器的能力已超越现有程序验证基准的难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统（Agentic systems）已成为形式数学中自动定理证明的最先进方法。为评估这些能力在程序验证领域的适用范围，作者选择对Claude Code在可验证代码生成任务中进行系统性评估。

Method: 在CLEVER基准（基于Lean 4的可验证代码生成基准）上，采用智能体证明框架对Claude Code进行评估，测试其规范生成、实现认证以及端到端程序生成与验证流程的能力。

Result: Claude为98.8%的问题生成了有效规范（其中81.3%通过CLEVER基于同构的评分）；对87.5%的问题成功根据正确的基本真值规范认证实现；在前提自洽的条目上端到端程序生成与验证流程成功率达98.1%。Claude还能为自身尝试提供高质量反馈，识别失败原因及数据集中的遗留bug。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了现有程序验证基准的难度与现代智能体证明器能力之间日益增长的不匹配，指出需要更严格、抗bug的评估方法论，特别是替代基于同构的规范评分方案。更广泛地说，紧凑的编译器在环智能体范式是当前基础程序验证最有效的方法。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [37] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAM框架以增强多模态大语言模型内在知识编辑的泛化能力，通过对抗性语义变体发现脆弱区域，并利用秩约束子空间学习实现鲁棒编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内在多模态知识编辑方法虽然可靠性好、局部性强，但泛化能力有限，无法在语义等价的视觉和语言变体间传播编辑。该问题源于缺乏显式语义监督、编辑范围僵化以及高维多模态空间中对单个样本的偏置锚定。

Method: 提出两个核心技术：(1)潜在对抗性强化(LAR)，在联合潜在空间中生成对抗性但语义一致的变体以暴露脆弱语义区域；(2)秩约束子空间学习(RCSL)，通过基于奇异值的目标函数在编辑层强制对抗性表示的低秩对齐。通过知识单元形式化鲁棒性，将泛化性定义为单元内一致预测。

Result: 通过广泛分析证明了ASAM方法的有效性，能够显式针对泛化性实现鲁棒的内在多模态知识编辑，解决了语义等价变体间编辑传播失败的问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式语义监督和低秩约束对齐机制，成功提升了多模态知识编辑的泛化鲁棒性，为构建更可靠的可编辑多模态大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [38] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceNum框架评估VLMs的空间数值理解能力，发现现有模型无法真正建立数字与空间含义的映射，性能接近随机猜测，主要依赖浅层空间线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在具身环境中需要输出数值（如动作幅度、空间坐标），但尚不清楚这些数值输出是否真正基于空间感知，缺乏对VLMs空间数值理解能力的系统评估。

Method: 提出SpaceNum统一框架，包含两种互补设置：空间探索中的动态转换和空间推理中的静态布局；设计Num2Space和Space2Num双向任务，系统评估VLMs在视觉侧空间结构与语言侧数值表示之间的映射能力。

Result: 模型在动态转换和静态布局任务中均未能将数字建立空间含义，性能接近随机猜测；模型严重依赖浅层空间线索，难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示，无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化空间布局；显式推理仅带来微小提升，微调可部分改善空间数值理解并迁移至外部空间推理基准。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs缺乏真正的空间数值理解能力，主要依赖浅层线索而非稳定的空间表示，未来需要改进模型的空间感知和数值建地能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [39] [From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23899)
*Zisu Huang,Jingwen Xu,Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Qihao Yang,Muzhao Tian,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Xue Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个覆盖技能完整生命周期的评估框架，发现模型生成的技能平均有益但存在非平凡的负迁移，并提出了元技能方法显著提升技能质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语言代理通过重用技能（特别是领域级和模型生成的技能）不断改进，但目前缺乏对技能完整生命周期（经验生成、技能提取、技能消费）的全面理解，无法回答这些技能是否有效、何时有效以及成功或失败的原因。

Method: 构建了一个基于效用的评估框架，系统性地在五个多样化的代理任务领域中进行实验，覆盖不同的技能提取器和目标代理；深入剖析每个生命周期阶段；提出元技能方法指导技能提取。

Result: 模型生成的技能平均有益但表现出非平凡的负迁移；提取器和目标代理行为不统一，强提取器可能是弱消费者；技能效用与模型规模或基线任务强度无关；经验组成影响技能质量；提出了元技能方法，在各领域持续提升技能质量并大幅减少负迁移。

Conclusion: 技能的有效性取决于多个生命周期阶段的复杂交互，通过深入理解各阶段特性并采用元技能引导提取，可以显著提升技能质量和减少负迁移，为语言代理的技能重用提供了理论和实践指导。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.

</details>


### [40] [SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23904)
*Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Weiquan Huang,Qihao Yang,Ziwei Zhou,Zisu Huang,Yan Li,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xue Yang,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOpt是首个针对智能体技能的可控文本空间优化器，通过优化器模型将评分的执行轨迹转化为对技能文档的有界编辑，仅在验证分数严格提升时接受编辑。在52个评估单元中全部最佳或并列最佳，在GPT-5.5上平均提升无技能准确率约20-25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能的创建方式（手工设计、一次性生成或松散控制的自修订）缺乏深度学习优化器的特性，无法在反馈下可靠地改进。作者提出应将技能作为冻结智能体的外部状态进行训练，采用与权重空间优化相同的原则来保证可复现性。

Method: SkillOpt采用独立的优化器模型将带评分的执行轨迹转化为对单一技能文档的有界添加/删除/替换编辑，仅当编辑严格提升留出验证分数时才接受。引入文本学习率预算、拒绝编辑缓冲区和逐轮慢速/元更新机制，在部署时不增加推理时的模型调用。

Result: 在6个基准、7个目标模型和3种执行环境（直接对话、Codex、Claude Code）的52个评估单元中，SkillOpt全部最佳或并列最佳，超越人类、一次性LLM、Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA和EvoSkill等方法。在GPT-5.5上，直接对话提升+23.5点，Codex环境+24.8点，Claude Code环境+19.1点。迁移实验表明优化后的技能可跨模型规模、执行环境和相近基准迁移。

Conclusion: SkillOpt证明了将技能训练视为冻结智能体外部状态优化的有效性，实现了首个系统可控的文本空间优化器。该方法不仅显著提升性能，还具有良好的迁移性，为零推理成本部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.

</details>

### [41] [MARGIN: Runtime Confidence Calibration for Multi-Agent Foundation Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22949)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARGIN方法，通过在线校准解决多智能体系统中基础模型置信度校准差的问题，在分布偏移下校准误差降低3-6倍，成对分辨率从低于随机提升至70-89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖智能体自报告的置信度权重，但基础模型的置信度存在系统性校准偏差，在困难任务上与准确率呈负相关。传统设计时校准方法（温度缩放、Platt缩放等）基于固定数据集拟合，无法适应分布偏移。

Method: 提出MARGIN（Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization）在线校准方法。该方法从任务流本身学习每个智能体、每个置信度区间的校准因子，使用对称指数加权移动平均结合贝叶斯收缩混合。无需模型访问权限、无需留出数据、无需重训练，仅有三个超参数且具有鲁棒默认值。

Result: 在19个基础模型、8个基准测试和超过50,000次观测上：MARGIN在分布偏移下的校准误差比最佳设计时基线低3-6倍。原始置信度在困难基准上的成对分辨率低于随机（45-56%），MARGIN将其提升至70-89%，在四个基准中的三个上超越始终选择最佳模型的oracle。

Conclusion: MARGIN成功解决了多智能体部署中的置信度校准问题，完全纠正了原始置信度的反向相关性，通过六个形式化命题刻画了收敛性、跟踪速度和对称更新的最优性，所有预测均得到实证验证。

Abstract: Foundation model agents increasingly operate in multi-agent deployments where a coordinator must decide which agent's response to trust. The standard approach weights agents by their self-reported confidence, but recent evidence shows that foundation model confidence is systematically mis-calibrated and, on hard tasks, inversely correlated with accuracy. Design-time calibration methods (temperature scaling, Platt scaling, histogram binning) cannot address this problem because they fit a fixed correction to held-out data and degrade under distribution shift.
  We present MARGIN (Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization), an online calibration method that learns per-agent, per-confidence-band calibration factors from the task stream itself, requiring no model access, no held-out data, and no retraining. MARGIN uses symmetric exponentially weighted moving averages with Bayesian shrinkage blending, and has three hyperparameters with robust defaults. Across 19 foundation models, 8 benchmarks, and over 50,000 observations, MARGIN achieves 3-6x lower calibration error than the best design-time baseline under distribution shift. In multi-agent selection, raw verbalized confidence produces pairwise resolution worse than random (45-56%) on hard benchmarks. MARGIN corrects this completely, raising pairwise resolution to 70-89% and surpassing the always-best-model oracle on three of four benchmarks. Six formal propositions characterize convergence, tracking speed, and the optimality of symmetric updates for non-strategic agents, with all predictions illustrated empirically.

</details>


### [42] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Cache Flow (LCF)方法，通过联合翻译和压缩KV缓存，使用极小的适配器（13MB）实现了比现有方法更高的准确性和更快的速度，解决了LLM智能体间通信的延迟和信息损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过文本通信存在显著延迟和信息损失，需要自回归解码和重新编码。现有方法C2C虽然尝试直接交换KV缓存，但其适配器体积大、训练昂贵，且要求上下文完全一致，不适用于不同上下文的智能体通信场景。

Method: 提出Latent Cache Flow (LCF)方法：1）通过联合翻译和压缩键值（KV），将适配器大小缩减至C2C的约4%；2）设计适配器传输目标模型未知的新信息摘要，而非逐个token翻译，从而处理不同上下文的场景。

Result: 在共享上下文设置下，13MB的LCF适配器比956MB的C2C适配器更准确；在不同上下文场景下，LCF比文本通信准确率提升23%，速度提升8.5倍。

Conclusion: LCF方法有效解决了LLM智能体间高效通信的问题，在保持或提升准确性的同时大幅降低了模型大小和通信延迟，为智能体协作提供了更优的通信机制。

Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

</details>


### [43] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何特征的语言模型不确定性量化方法，通过追踪层级MLP更新路径提取11个可解释特征，在选择性拒绝任务上相比默认MSP方法提升高达21个AURC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大softmax概率(MSP)方法虽然计算高效但校准性差；基于内部激活的方法将隐藏状态视为静态快照输入黑盒分类器，忽略了表示形成的层级轨迹信息，而相似终点可能来自不同路径，证据在深度方向上的累积、强化或反转可能揭示最终概率掩盖的不确定性。

Method: 提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪每层MLP更新的累积路径，将这些特征输入稀疏线性探针进行不确定性量化，所有特征均具有闭式几何含义。

Result: 该方法在选择性拒绝任务上优于MSP基准，性能增益随基准误校准程度增加可达21个AURC点；探针系数能够追溯错误形成的具体方式和位置。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提升了不确定性量化的性能，还提供了可解释性，能够识别哪些层过早决策、哪些层与运行状态矛盾、以及轨迹在何处偏离终点，为理解语言模型内部错误形成机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [44] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FusionSense框架，通过融合感知的智能传感方法，在边缘系统实现运行时计算与通信联合优化，在双模态设置下最高降低33倍能耗并保持任务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统和智能工业部署中，多模态传感器套件在边缘端快速普及，但现有方法要么在服务器端融合导致传输冗余，要么采用忽略跨模态依赖的单模态近传感器滤波，导致传输浪费或事件遗漏。在严格的能耗、延迟和可靠性约束下，亟需运行时自适应地决策各传感器的计算与传输策略。

Method: FusionSense采用三步训练流程：(1) 服务器端融合模型学习下游任务；(2) 引入FoS（Filter-out-safe）标签量化各模态相对于融合决策的必要性；(3) 将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入，压缩边缘端融合模型。最终形成运行时决策层，随传感器数量线性扩展。

Result: 在SynDrone双模态（RGB+Depth/LiDAR）实验中：低事件发生率（1% FoI）下能耗降低33倍，10%发生率下降低11倍；在固定30%数据缩减下质量损失减少92.3%；相比最佳基线滤波方法能耗节省提升约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense通过融合感知的近传感器分类器设计，成功实现计算与通信的联合优化，在多模态边缘系统中显著降低能耗同时保持任务质量，为能源受限的自主边缘系统提供了有效的运行时自适应解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [45] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FuRA框架，通过保留预训练谱结构实现高效微调，在LLaMA-3-8B上比全量微调提升1.37分，且4位量化版本超越QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法（全量微调和LoRA等）在更新权重时未考虑预训练阶段建立的谱结构，导致有限微调数据产生的噪声梯度会破坏鲁棒的预训练特征。

Method: 提出FuRA（Full-Rank Adaptation）框架：基于块张量列车分解W=LSR，将大核心L固定为预训练块SVD基，仅优化紧凑核心R和块奇异值S。该方法同时实现全秩谱预条件化、保持全秩更新表达能力，并达到与LoRA相当的参数、内存和步时效率。

Result: FuRA在多个场景持续优于全量微调：LLaMA-3-8B常识推理任务提升1.37分、LLM数学推理强化学习、VLM视觉指令调优；4位量化版本QFuRA也超越QLoRA。

Conclusion: 谱预条件化是微调的关键要素，FuRA通过约束更新到预训练列空间，在不牺牲表达能力的前提下有效保护预训练特征，实现了性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [46] [The Readout Shortcut: Positional Number Copying Dominates Arithmetic CoT Readout in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22870)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小模型CoT推理主要依赖位置捷径（复制末尾数字）而非逻辑推演，这挑战了CoT作为真实推理能力指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在算术任务中需要CoT推理，但打乱步骤后性能基本保持，这引发了对CoT实际贡献的疑问：如果不是逻辑序列，CoT到底在做什么？

Method: 在三个1-3B指令微调语言模型上，通过前缀完成隔离答案读出阶段，测试位置捷径假设；采用干扰项插入、头部消融等方法验证机制。

Result: 发现位置捷径：模型复制答案分隔符前的末尾数字，贡献54-92pp准确率；即使推理错误，最终答案与末尾数字匹配率仍达95-96%；复制通道优先于正确推理；不同架构模型表现不同，7-8B模型出现内容选择性门控。

Conclusion: CoT的步骤级忠实性评估可能混淆位置答案传输与真实计算，这是基于CoT监督的失效模式；CoT在小模型中更多作为答案传输机制而非推理机制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting is necessary for arithmetic in small language models, yet shuffling its steps preserves most performance. What does CoT contribute if not logical sequencing? In three 1-3B instruction-tuned LMs on GSM8K, we isolate the answer-readout stage via prefix completion and identify a positional shortcut: the model copies whichever number occupies the trailing position before the answer delimiter, regardless of intermediate reasoning. Gold-answer presence accounts for 54-92 pp of accuracy (89-92% of each model's teacher-forcing ceiling); even on incorrect items, the final answer matches the last CoT number 95-96% of the time. The copy channel takes precedence over retained-context completion: replacing the trailing number with a wrong value collapses accuracy to near-zero despite correct intermediates, yet removing it recovers 5-32 pp above that floor--even single-step arithmetic the model can otherwise perform is suppressed when a copyable number is present. Qwen and Llama copy novel distractors 87-95% of the time; Gemma gates selectively. Head-level ablation implicates architecture-specific head sets; the effect replicates on GSM-Symbolic. On non-arithmetic BBH tasks, shuffle retention drops sharply; at 7-8B, content-selective gating emerges. Step-level faithfulness evaluations risk conflating positional answer transport with genuine computation--a failure mode for CoT-based oversight.

</details>


### [47] [Approximate Machine Unlearning through Manifold Representation Forgetting Guided by Self Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22871)
*Weiqi Wang,Zhiyi Tian,Chenhan Zhang,Luoyu Chen,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ManiF-SMC方法，通过在表示空间中将被遗忘样本推离原始流形中心并向保留数据的语义邻居靠近，实现了与重训练等价且不依赖标签的机器遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法（如标签操纵或任务梯度反转）遗忘效果有限，可能破坏原始学习目标，且无法保证与标准重训练方法的等价性。作者观察到重训练后的模型倾向于根据被删除样本与保留数据的语义相似性对其进行分类，这为设计新的遗忘机制提供了动机。

Method: 提出ManiF-SMC（流形遗忘与自模式连通性）方法：1）将近似遗忘重新表述为表示空间操作，将每个被删除样本推离其原始学习的流形表示中心，并推向保留数据中最近的语义邻居；2）使用基于边界的三元组损失封装遗忘和表示保留目标；3）设计自模式连通性模块，快速重建局部流形以指导每个遗忘案例的自适应边界生成。

Result: 在四个代表性数据集上的大量实验表明，ManiF-SMC在仅操作模型表示空间的情况下，达到了与最先进近似方法相当的遗忘效果，同时保持了与重训练行为的一致性。

Conclusion: ManiF-SMC成功地将机器遗忘转化为流形表示空间中的优化问题，摆脱了对标签和任务特定梯度的依赖，实现了与重训练等价的遗忘效果，为机器遗忘领域提供了一种新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is a fundamental mechanism that enforces the right to be forgotten. Existing unlearning studies that rely on label manipulation or task-gradient reversal often deliver limited unlearning effectiveness. Moreover, they can undermine the original learning objective and typically do not guarantee equivalence to standard unlearning by retraining.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{ManiF-SMC} (\textbf{Mani}fold \textbf{F}orgetting with \textbf{S}elf \textbf{M}ode \textbf{C}onnectivity), motivated by the observation that a model retrained on the remaining data tends to classify erased samples by their semantic similarity to the retained data. We begin with systematically recasting the approximate unlearning as pushing each erased sample away from its original learned manifold representation centroid toward its nearest semantic neighbors in the retained data. This reformulation aligns unlearning with retraining behavior and operates purely in representation space, reducing reliance on labels and task-specific gradients. To tackle the manifold representation-based unlearning problem, ManiF-SMC encapsulates the unlearning and representation preservation goals in a margin-based triplet loss. Because finding a suitable margin for unlearning is challenging, we propose a self-mode-connectivity module that rapidly reconstructs the local manifold to guide the adaptive margins generation for each unlearning case. Extensive experiments on four representative datasets show that ManiF-SMC achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to state-of-the-art approximate methods while operating solely within the model's representation space.

</details>


### [48] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedExpMem框架，通过记忆诊断失败经验来提升医学视觉语言模型的鉴别诊断能力，在放射学基准测试上实现了最高7.0%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验丰富的医生通过临床实践发展诊断专长，不仅获得疾病知识，还具备区分易混淆疾病的能力。然而当前的医学视觉语言模型缺乏这种能力，其参数编码的是静态知识，无法在诊断过程中进化。现有检索增强生成方法检索百科全书式的疾病描述，但缺乏鉴别性经验的积累。

Method: 提出MedExpMem经验记忆框架，使基于VLM的诊断代理能够积累鉴别诊断专长。该框架记忆代理自身诊断失败中衍生的鉴别性经验，将其组织为编码关键鉴别因素、可操作决策规则和推理错误模式的成对鉴别笔记。采用两阶段构建过程模拟医生学习：初始实践暴露知识缺口，反思性再诊断完善理解。遇到新病例时，代理检索经验记忆以指导鉴别推理。

Result: 在涵盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上进行评估，结果显示在各种模型和规模上均有一致的准确率提升，最高提升7.0%。分析性实验验证了经验质量和鲁棒性，证明MedExpMem是解决参数学习无法触及的医学适应需求的有竞争力方法。

Conclusion: MedExpMem成功模拟了医生的专家经验积累过程，通过记忆鉴别性经验而非静态知识，有效提升了医学VLM的诊断能力，为医学人工智能的自适应学习提供了新思路，证明了超越参数学习的方法在医学适应任务中的价值。

Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

</details>


### [49] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EDRM框架，通过早期解码熵动态预测CoT推理收益，实现自适应推理策略选择，在15个基准上节省27-55% tokens并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理并非在所有任务中都有效，在事实性和开放性任务中往往收益有限甚至为负，同时显著增加token消耗。现有方法缺乏对何时应启用显式推理的理解，导致资源浪费。

Method: 发现LLM推理是一种动态解码状态，早期熵动态可可靠指示该状态：受益于CoT的任务呈现一致的熵降低，而其他任务则显示不稳定或增加模式。基于此提出EDRM框架，将熵轨迹嵌入紧凑可解释的流形表示，利用早期解码熵自适应选择推理策略，无需训练即可部署。

Result: 在15个基准和4个不同规模架构的LLM上，EDRM持续优于静态基线。数据集层面：仅用50个校准样本即可减少41-55% tokens并提升准确率；实例层面：准确率最高提升4.7%，同时保持27-45%的token节省。

Conclusion: 推理应当选择性调用而非默认启用，熵驱动的解码控制为高效自适应LLM推理提供了有效途径，证明了推理是动态解码状态而非任务或模型的静态属性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the default strategy for enhancing LLM capabilities, yet its application raises a fundamental question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial? Empirical evidence reveals a striking paradox: CoT often provides marginal or even negative gains on factual and open-ended tasks while multiplying token consumption. In this work, we show that LLM reasoning is not a static property of tasks or models, but a \emph{dynamic decoding state} that emerges during generation. Through systematic analysis, we find early-stage entropy dynamics provide a reliable signal of this state: tasks benefiting from CoT exhibit consistent entropy reduction, while others display unstable or increasing patterns. This behavior can be interpreted as a phase-transition-like shift from a high-entropy exploratory regime to a low-entropy structured reasoning regime. Based on these insights, we propose \textbf{EDRM} (Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold), a lightweight and training-free routing framework that leverages early decoding entropy to adaptively select inference strategies. EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact and interpretable manifold representation, enabling both zero-shot deployment and fine-grained instance-level adaptation. Across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs of varying scales and architectures, EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines. At the dataset level, EDRM achieves \textbf{41--55\%} token reduction while improving accuracy with as few as 50 calibration samples. At the instance level, it further improves accuracy by up to \textbf{4.7\%} while maintaining \textbf{27--45\%} token savings. These results suggest that reasoning should be invoked selectively rather than by default, and demonstrate the effectiveness of entropy-driven decoding control for efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

</details>


### [50] [WeCon: An Efficient Weight-Conditioned Neural Solver for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22876)
*Xuan Wu,Jinbiao Chen,Yang Li,Lijie Wen,Chunguo Wu,Yuanshu Li,Yubin Xiao,Chunyan Miao,You Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WeCon神经求解器，通过改进权重条件上下文建模、缓解权重信号稀释和高效偏好优化，在保持SOTA性能的同时将推理时间减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOCOP神经求解器存在两个关键局限：1)权重向量仅在解码时注入一次或主要在编码时注入，导致权重条件上下文建模不足或解码时权重信号被稀释；2)偏好优化方法依赖随机采样构建解对，产生信息量低的训练样本，降低训练效率。

Method: 提出WeCon求解器：1)设计包含三个注意力块和门控残差融合(GRF)块的编码器层，促进实例特征与权重的协调交互；2)在解码器中引入即插即用的残差融合(RF)块缓解权重信号稀释；3)提出高效偏好优化(EPO)方法构建高质量解对以提升训练效果。

Result: 在四种MOCOP变体、不同问题规模和分布模式的实验中，WeCon实现了与SOTA求解器POCCO-W相当的HyperVolume(HV)值，同时推理时间减少40%。消融研究验证了所有设计组件的贡献。

Conclusion: WeCon通过权重条件上下文建模、权重信号保持和高效偏好优化的协同设计，有效解决了现有MOCOP神经求解器的关键缺陷，在性能和效率间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Existing neural solvers for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems (MOCOPs) commonly adopt decomposition-based strategies that scalarize an MOCOP into multiple subproblems associated with distinct weight vectors. However, they either inject weights only once during decoding, limiting weight-conditioned context modeling, or primarily during encoding, causing weight-signal dilution during decoding. Moreover, preference optimization methods rely on purely random sampling to construct solution pairs for training solvers, which often produces less informative pairs and thus leads to low training effectiveness. To better address these limitations, we propose an efficient Weight-Conditioned neural solver (WeCon). Specifically, we design an encoder layer with three attention blocks and our proposed Gated Residual Fusion (GRF) block to facilitate harmonious interaction between instance features and weights, thereby generating informative weight-conditioned context. We further introduce a plug-and-play Residual Fusion (RF) block in the decoder to alleviate weight-signal dilution. Finally, we propose Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO), which constructs high-quality solutions, thereby generating more informative pairs to improve training effectiveness. Experiments on four MOCOP variants across different problem scales and distribution patterns demonstrate that WeCon achieves HyperVolume (HV) values comparable to SOTA solver POCCO-W, while reducing inference time by 40%. Ablation studies validate the contributions of all designs.

</details>


### [51] [Tensor Cache: Eviction-conditioned Associative Memory for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22884)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tensor Cache，一种结合滑动窗口注意力与固定大小外积快速权重记忆的两级缓存机制，在限制内存的同时显著提升了长上下文建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer的KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，而滑动窗口缓存虽然限制内存但完全丢弃被驱逐的token，导致窗口外的相关证据无法访问。需要在内存约束与长距离信息保留之间寻求平衡。

Method: 设计两级缓存架构：L1为滑动窗口softmax注意力（保留精确局部注意力），L2为固定大小外积快速权重记忆（存储被驱逐的KV对）。被驱逐的KV对被压缩为层级矩阵A，未来查询通过矩阵乘法读取（利用线性注意力恒等式）。引入学习标量门控融合两级输出，训练层级衰减和写入率参数。识别并提出修正分块均值训练shortcut中的跨token外积伪影问题，采用并行加权和扫描实现精确逐token写入。

Result: 在系统扩展、可控联想回忆、长上下文语言建模和内存容量诊断等实验中，Tensor Cache在有界状态基线方法上改进了内存-质量前沿。

Conclusion: Tensor Cache成功地将经典的外积记忆机制与滑动窗口注意力结合为两级缓存系统，有效解决了长上下文建模中的内存约束问题，同时避免了现有训练方法的数值精度问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformer KV caches grow linearly with context length; sliding-window caching bounds memory but discards evicted tokens entirely, so relevant evidence outside the window becomes inaccessible. We introduce \emph{Tensor Cache}, a two-level cache that pairs sliding-window softmax attention as a first-level cache (L1) with a fixed-size outer-product fast-weight memory as a second-level cache (L2) fed by KV pairs evicted from the window. Recent tokens remain in exact local attention; evicted pairs are compressed into a per-layer matrix $A$ and read by future queries through a single matrix multiplication, exploiting the linear-attention identity $q_t(k_i \otimes v_i)=\langle q_t,k_i\rangle v_i$. A learned scalar gate fuses the L1 and L2 outputs, and per-head decay and write-rate parameters are trained end-to-end. The outer-product memory and the read identity are well-known; our contribution is their use as an L2 cache fed exclusively by sliding-window evictions, plus identifying that the common chunked-mean training shortcut $A\!\leftarrow\!λA\!+\!η(\bar k\!\otimes\!\bar v)$ silently introduces $C^2{-}C$ spurious cross-token outer products per chunk, and closing the gap with a parallel weighted-sum scan equivalent to per-token writes within float32 epsilon. Across systems scaling, controlled associative recall, long-context language modeling, and memory-capacity diagnostics, Tensor Cache improves the memory--quality frontier over bounded-state baselines.

</details>


### [52] [Pointwise Metrics Mislead: An Evaluation Protocol for Multimodal Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22891)
*Mads H. Baattrup,Jörn Bach,Laurids Jeppe,Finn Labe,Alexander Grohsjean,Christian Schwanenberger,Peer Stelldinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统点估计指标（如RMSE、MAE）在多模态后验的逆问题中存在结构性缺陷，会导致边缘谱变窄并丢失关键光谱特征；本文提出三部分评估协议（CRPS、光谱保真度诊断、覆盖度校准）以全面评估重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学重建评估长期被点对点指标（RMSE、MAE、单事件分辨率）主导，隐含假设是误差越低重建越好。然而这一假设在多模态后验的逆问题中结构性失效：根据全方差定律，最小化MSE或MAE的点估计器会产生比真实值更窄的边缘谱，这种偏差独立于架构、训练和数据集大小，且恰好压缩了下游科学测量所依赖的光谱特征（尾部、模态、形状）。

Method: 提出三部分评估协议，每一步针对其他步骤遗漏的失效模式：（1）通过CRPS（连续排名概率分数）评估单事件分布准确性；（2）通过光谱保真度诊断评估群体级边缘准确性；（3）通过基于覆盖度的校准评估不确定性可信度。

Result: 在具有解析后验的合成基准测试和来自粒子物理的 realistic 多对一逆问题上，模型排名在点对点指标和分布指标之间发生逆转；校准进一步区分了在CRPS下无法区分的架构。

Conclusion: 评估协议而非模型本身决定了科学结论。传统点对点指标在多模态后验逆问题中系统性误导评估，必须采用包含分布准确性、光谱保真度和不确定性校准的综合评估框架才能得到可靠的科学推断。

Abstract: Evaluation in scientific reconstruction is dominated by pointwise metrics - RMSE, MAE, per-event resolution - under the implicit assumption that lower error means better reconstruction. We show that this assumption fails structurally for inverse problems with multimodal posteriors. By the law of total variance, point estimators trained to minimize MSE or MAE produce a marginal spectrum strictly narrower than the truth whenever the posterior has nonzero width. The resulting bias is independent of architecture, training, and dataset size, and it compresses precisely the spectral features - tails, modes, shapes - that downstream scientific measurements rely on. We propose a three-part evaluation protocol where each step targets a failure mode the others miss: per-event distributional accuracy via CRPS, population-level marginal accuracy via a spectrum-fidelity diagnostic, and uncertainty trustworthiness via coverage-based calibration. On a synthetic benchmark with an analytic posterior and on a realistic many-to-one inverse problem from particle physics, model rankings reverse between pointwise and distributional metrics, and calibration further separates architectures indistinguishable under CRPS. The evaluation protocol, not the model, determines the scientific conclusion.

</details>


### [53] [From Residuals to Reasons: LLM-Guided Mechanism Inference from Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22897)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MARICL框架，通过LLM多智能体分析基础模型的失败案例并生成显式修正项，在多个科学数据集上持续提升预测性能，并在跨批次测试中验证了修正公式的机制泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用中的机器学习面临预测与理解难以兼得的挑战：统计模型虽在结构化数据上表现出色但如同黑箱，现有可解释性方法仅能回答"哪些特征重要"，无法阐明特征交互方式或与人类理解进行迭代优化。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL)框架：(1)以基础模型为锚点，而非让LLM直接预测目标；(2)LLM智能体分析基础模型的高残差失败案例；(3)从上下文提供的示例中假设缺失的结构；(4)通过多轮文本梯度优化生成显式修正项。

Result: 在涵盖科学、生物医学、社会经济和合成设置的九个基准测试中，MARICL在所有数据集上均持续优于基础模型。在Cell-Free Protein数据集的跨批次泛化测试中，冻结的公式在相同试剂协议下于92%以上的案例中提升预测，但在不同协议下系统性失效，成功边界与生物化学机制而非批次计数对齐。

Conclusion: MARICL成功实现了预测性能与机制理解的统一，修正项能够捕捉真实的数据结构而非批次特定噪声，验证了方法的机制泛化能力，为科学应用中黑箱模型的可解释性提供了新路径。

Abstract: A persistent challenge in machine learning for scientific applications is jointly achieving prediction and understanding. Statistical models excel on structured data but operate as black boxes, while existing interpretability methods are largely inspective: they answer "which features matter?" but do not articulate how features interact or refine explanations iteratively alongside human understanding. Asking an LLM to predict the target directly forces it to search the entire output space; we instead anchor predictions with a base model and ask the LLM the narrower question of what that model is missing. We introduce Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL), an agentic framework in which LLM agents analyze where a base-model fails, hypothesize missing structure from high-residual examples provided in context, and produce explicit correction terms refined through multi-turn textual gradient optimization. Across nine benchmarks spanning scientific, biomedical, socioeconomic, and synthetic settings, MARICL improves consistently over its base model on all datasets. To test whether these corrections reflect real structure or batch-specific noise, we freeze formulas learned on one experimental batch of the Cell-Free Protein dataset and apply them (with no retraining and no further LLM calls) to held-out batches. Within the same reagent protocol, the frozen formulas improve predictions in over 92% of cases; across a different protocol, they fail systematically. The success boundary aligns with the biochemistry, not the batch count; direct evidence of mechanistic generalization.

</details>


### [54] [FIRMA: FIbonacci Ring Model Aggregation for Privacy-preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22898)
*Rachid Hedjam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出FIRMA系列协议，通过Fibonacci加权的环形拓扑聚合，在无需中心服务器的前提下，同时实现了分类头永久隐私保护和非对称邻居加权，解决了联邦学习中的结构性三难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议面临结构性三难困境：传统基于服务器的聚合存在单点故障和梯度反演风险；去中心化环形八卦方法会通过无信息均匀权重向半诚实对等节点暴露分类头；而个性化方法又重新引入了中心聚合。目前尚无协议能同时实现无服务器操作、永久私有分类头、环形拓扑以及原则性非对称邻居加权。

Method: 提出FIRMA系列三种递进增强的联邦学习协议：(1) fibfl作为基础，采用Fibonacci加权的邻居混合进行无服务器环形聚合，并保持分类头永久私有；(2) fibflp在此基础上增加精度门控邻居抑制，选择性降低收敛不佳对等节点的权重，同时保留Fibonacci方向偏好；(3) fibflpp作为完整系统，引入2-opt环形置换最大化相邻客户端类别多样性，通过K_g=⌈N/2⌉次八卦传递实现全局环形覆盖，并采用余弦退火自保留校准。论文建立了收敛率边界和三个支持性命题，涵盖归一化、覆盖、保留和多样性最优性。

Result: 在28种配置（四个基准数据集与七种异质性设置交叉）的系统实验中，fibflpp在所有12个标签偏斜配置上均超越FedAvg，在CIFAR-10的K=1设置下峰值优势达+20.7个百分点。在Dirichlet异质性下，fibflpp在所有无服务器协议中实现帕累托占优，在28个配置中的17个获得最高精度。

Conclusion: FIRMA协议族成功解决了联邦学习结构性三难问题，首次同时实现无服务器运行、永久私有分类头、环形拓扑和原则性非对称邻居加权。理论分析证明了收敛性和各设计组件的最优性，实验验证了在多种异质性设置下的优越性能，为联邦学习提供了新的范式选择。

Abstract: Federated learning protocols face a structural trilemma: canonical server-based aggregation~\cite{mcmahan2017} creates a single point of failure and gradient inversion risk; decentralised ring-gossip alternatives~\cite{hu2019segmented} expose classification heads to semi-honest peers via uninformed uniform weights; and personalised methods~\cite{collins2021exploiting} reintroduce central aggregation. No existing protocol simultaneously achieves server-free operation, permanently private heads, ring topology, and principled asymmetric neighbour weighting. We propose FIRMA (\textbf{FI}bonacci \textbf{R}ing \textbf{M}odel \textbf{A}ggregation), a family of three progressively enhanced federated learning protocols: 1) \fibfl\ establishes the foundation: server-free ring aggregation with Fibonacci-weighted neighbour blending and permanently private classification heads. 2) \fibflp\ augments this with accuracy-gated neighbour suppression, selectively down-weighting poorly-converged peers while preserving the Fibonacci directional bias. 3) \fibflpp, the full system, completes the family with a 2-opt ring permutation that maximises adjacent-client class diversity, global ring coverage via $K_g{=}\lceil N/2\rceil$ gossip passes, and cosine-annealed self-retention calibration. We establish a convergence rate bound and three supporting propositions governing normalisation, coverage, retention, and diversity optimality. Systematic experiments across 28 configurations -- four benchmarks crossed with seven heterogeneity regimes -- demonstrate that \fibflpp\ surpasses \fedavg\ in all 12 label-skew configurations, with a peak advantage of $+20.7$\,pp on CIFAR-10 at $K{=}1$. Under Dirichlet heterogeneity, \fibflpp\ is the Pareto-dominant method among all server-free protocols, achieving the highest accuracy in 17 of 28 configurations.

</details>


### [55] [Transcoders Trace Visual Grounding and Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22902)
*Dimitrios Damianos,Leon Voukoutis,Georgios Skyrianos,Vassilis Katsouros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transcoder的功能中心框架来解释视觉语言模型，发现其比稀疏自编码器能更准确地识别视觉-语言交互路径，并能预测模型幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的可解释性研究主要使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs），该方法仅分解静态残差表示，忽略了驱动跨模态交互的功能更新，无法充分理解视觉输入如何转换为文本。

Method: 采用基于Transcoder的功能中心框架，将MLP子层进行稀疏近似作为层级计算的因果代理，应用于Gemma 3-4B-IT模型，将模型分解为连接图像块与token生成方向的可解释计算路径。

Result: Transcoder归因在视觉基础token的patch消融实验中比SAE归因产生更强且稳定的效果，与语义相关图像区域对齐更好；错误视觉基础反事实分析证实了路径对视觉-语言交互的特异性；基于Transcoder电路追踪的图特征预测幻觉的AUC达到0.68。

Conclusion: 功能中心的电路分解方法为视觉语言模型的多模态计算提供了可解释且具有预测性的分析框架，证明了该方法在理解视觉-语言交互机制方面的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on multimodal reasoning, but how visual inputs are transformed to text remains poorly understood. Existing interpretability work on VLMs uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), which decompose static residual representations and miss the functional updates that drive cross-modal interaction. We adopt a function-centric framework based on Transcoders, sparse approximations of MLP sublayers that act as a causal proxy for layer-wise computation. Applied to Gemma 3-4B-IT, the framework decomposes the model into interpretable computational pathways linking image patches to directions in token generation. Transcoder attributions produce stronger and more stable effects on visually grounded tokens under patch ablation than SAE attributions, and align better with semantically relevant image regions. A False Visual Grounding counterfactual analysis confirms that the recovered pathways are specific to vision-language interaction.Finally, we perform a structural analysis of hallucinated generations, by extracting graph-based indicators from circuit traces produced by the transcoders. A logistic classifier over these mechanistic graph features predicts hallucinations at AUC $0.68$. These results show that function-centric circuit decomposition yields interpretable and predictive accounts of multimodal computation in VLMs.

</details>


### [56] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段联邦推荐系统，通过分离非敏感偏好数据和敏感上下文数据，在保护隐私的前提下实现移动端个性化内容推荐，并提供了跨平台生产级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的移动端个性化内容服务需要将敏感用户数据集中存储在中心服务器上，这种做法与现代隐私期望和地区法规（如GDPR）日益冲突，亟需一种既能提供个性化推荐又能保护用户隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段联邦推荐系统管道：第一阶段在云端对非敏感的应用上下文数据运行协同过滤模型，生成相关候选物品的短名单；第二阶段在设备端利用敏感的移动信号对这些候选物品进行重新排序，只有模型更新/梯度会离开设备。实现了非敏感偏好数据与敏感上下文数据的分离。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI人类活动识别数据集和一个专有试点数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。提供了生产就绪的实现，作为Kotlin Multiplatform库，可部署在Android和iOS平台上。

Conclusion: 该两阶段联邦推荐系统成功实现了隐私保护与个性化推荐的平衡，通过数据分离原则和联邦学习架构，既避免了敏感数据离开设备，又保持了推荐质量，为移动端隐私保护推荐系统提供了可行的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [57] [Human-Centered Learning Mechanics: A Dynamical Framework for Entropy-Regulated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22940)
*Kim Phuc Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了以人为中心的学习机制（HCLM）框架，通过有效信息力和几何熵代理来改进深度学习中的熵正则化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论将训练视为封闭的优化系统，但在现实AI应用中，模型面临不确定性、资源约束、分布偏移、下游决策风险和人类反馈等开放环境挑战，需要新的理论框架来处理开放和受控的学习系统。

Method: 提出有效熵的概念，研究基于方差和对数行列式协方差的几何熵代理。通过信息力分析熵正则化的有效性，建立信息注入、熵耗散和剩余风险之间的动态平衡理论框架。

Result: 证明了当熵代理生成非退化信息力时熵正则化才有效；推导了收敛性、熵流、Wasserstein梯度流和噪声表示泛化结果；实验表明对数行列式协方差熵比softmax归一化熵产生更强、更稳定的信息力。

Conclusion: 熵正则化成功的关键在于产生有效的信息力，几何熵代理（尤其是对数行列式协方差熵）在表示学习任务中表现优异，为理解和改进深度学习动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Deep learning is increasingly viewed as a dynamical process in parameter space, yet many existing theories still treat training as a closed optimization system. This view is limited for real-world AI, where models operate under uncertainty, resource constraints, distribution shift, downstream decision risks, and human feedback. We propose Human-Centered Learning Mechanics (HCLM), a dynamical and information-theoretic framework for open and controlled learning systems. The central idea is that entropy regularization is useful only when the chosen entropy surrogate generates a non-degenerate information force along the optimization trajectory. Otherwise, entropy terms may produce weak, unstable, or misaligned gradients, causing the dynamics to collapse toward ordinary loss minimization. We introduce the notion of effective entropy and study tractable geometric entropy surrogates, including variance-based and log-determinant covariance proxies. The paper makes three contributions.
