  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界环境具有固有的不确定性和复杂的未来行为，现有的确定性预测模型无法满足需求，单点预测不足以应对，因此需要能够量化和表示不确定性的概率预测方法。

Method: 提出PaP-NF概率预测框架，采用Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型(LLM)对齐，并将正规化流解码器条件化于LLM提取的全局上下文，使用连续排序概率分数(CRPS)作为概率预测的标准评估指标。

Result: 在多种长期预测基准测试中，PaP-NF能够稳健地捕捉多模态不确定性，同时保持具有竞争力的点预测精度。

Conclusion: PaP-NF框架成功实现了概率时间序列预测，有效解决了现实环境中不确定性量化的问题，为时间序列预测领域提供了一种新的有效方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a central role in many real-world applications and has been extensively studied. Most existing approaches rely on deterministic models. However, real-world environments exhibit inherently uncertain and complex future behaviors, making single-point predictions insufficient. This highlights the need for probabilistic forecasting methods that can quantify and represent uncertainty. In this work, we propose PaP-NF, a probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns continuous time series representations with a frozen large language model (LLM) using a Prefix-as-Prompt mechanism, and conditions a normalizing flow decoder on the global context extracted by the LLM. The quality of the resulting predictive distributions is evaluated using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS), a standard metric in probabilistic forecasting. Across a variety of long-term forecasting benchmarks, PaP-NF robustly captures multi-modal uncertainty while maintaining competitive point forecasting accuracy. The official implementation is available at: https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF

</details>


### [94] [WMAttack: Automated Attack Search for Adversarial Evaluation of World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23220)
*Zhixiang Guo,Siyuan Liang,Shi Fu,Cheng Guo,Andras Balogh,Mark Jelasity,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了WMAttack框架，用于自动评估世界模型智能体的对抗鲁棒性，通过SCAS和RGAR组件在有限预算下搜索更强攻击，在DreamerV3上显著提升了攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为决策智能体的应用日益增长，但其对抗鲁棒性研究不足。主要障碍在于攻击评估需要兼顾准确性和效率：弱攻击会高估鲁棒性，而穷举式超参数搜索因需要闭环展开而成本过高。

Method: 提出WMAttack框架：1) 将鲁棒性评估建模为有限预算攻击配置搜索问题；2) Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) 利用奖励下降、动作不稳定性、运行成本和展开变异性反馈来改进攻击提议分布；3) Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) 从表示相似任务中检索有效历史配置作为新环境的热启动。

Result: 在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack相比基线方法持续发现更强攻击：DreamerV3 Atari上的标准化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，DMC上从0.319提升至0.682。消融实验证明RGAR改善初始候选质量，SCAS在固定预算下提升最终攻击效用。

Conclusion: WMAttack有效解决了世界模型智能体对抗鲁棒性评估中的准确性和效率权衡问题。理论分析表明提议优化通过将概率质量向高效用攻击转移来改进有限预算搜索，实验验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of world models as decision-making agents, their adversarial robustness remains underexplored due to the lack of dedicated automated evaluation methods. A key obstacle is that attack evaluation must be both accurate and efficient: weak manually tuned attacks can overestimate robustness, while exhaustive hyperparameter search is prohibitively expensive because each candidate requires closed-loop rollouts through learned latent dynamics. We introduce WMAttack, an automated attack-search framework for adversarial evaluation of world-model agents. WMAttack formulates robustness evaluation as a finite-budget search over attack configurations, including attack families, perturbation budgets, optimization steps, restarts, and allocation rules. To improve search accuracy, Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) refines the attack proposal distribution using feedback from reward degradation, action instability, runtime cost, and rollout variability. To improve search efficiency, Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) retrieves effective historical configurations from representation-similar tasks, providing a warm start for unseen environments. We provide a theoretical explanation showing that proposal refinement improves finite-budget search when it shifts probability mass toward high-utility attacks. Across Atari and DeepMind Control tasks, WMAttack consistently discovers stronger attacks than the evaluated baselines, improving normalized reward drop from 0.497 to 1.034 on DreamerV3 Atari and from 0.319 to 0.682 on DMC. Ablations further show that RGAR improves initial candidate quality and SCAS improves final attack utility under fixed evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [95] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的空间公平性问题：考虑个体移动模式的公平性评估，并提出了一种基于多分辨率空间划分和空间扫描统计的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间公平性研究假设每个个体仅关联单一地理位置（如居住地），但个体在不同区域间的移动模式同样会影响公平性。因此，需要将空间公平性概念扩展到包含个体移动轨迹的情境。

Method: 提出一种两阶段方法：首先将个体移动模式关联到多个不同分辨率和位置的空间分区；然后应用合适的空间扫描统计量来评估预测模型是否存在基于移动模式的公平性问题。

Result: 在数千个合成不公平数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效检测这种新型的不公平性并识别被不公平对待的对象集合，同时定位性能展现出一致的多分辨率权衡特性。

Conclusion: 将空间公平性概念扩展到移动模式领域具有重要意义，所提出的方法为检测预测模型中基于个体移动轨迹的新型不公平性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

</details>


### [96] [Enhancing Deep Neural Network Reliability with Refinement and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23249)
*Ramya Hebbalaguppe,Ajay Shastry,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Chetan Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RefCal框架，通过联合优化校准、精化度和准确率来提升深度神经网络可靠性，解决了现有校准方法以牺牲精化度为代价的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽然预测准确率高，但置信度估计往往不可靠。现有校准方法通常通过后处理技术改善校准指标，但这只是模拟训练时的不确定性，并未真正提升模型理解能力。更重要的是，许多校准方法在提升校准性能的同时会降低模型的精化度（refinement），即模型区分正确和错误预测的能力。

Method: 提出了两个核心贡献：（1）一种新型损失函数，通过监督对比学习显式促进精化度，使模型能够为正确和错误预测分配显著不同的置信度分数；（2）统一训练框架RefCal，联合优化校准、精化度和准确率三个目标，以提升DNN的整体可靠性。

Result: 在具有10%类别不平衡的CIFAR-100-LT数据集上，RefCal达到准确率58.81%、精化度95.67%、ECE（期望校准误差）0.08的性能；相比广泛使用的Correctness Ranking Loss（准确率46.27%、精化度93.7%、ECE 0.22），在所有三个指标上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了同时提升校准和精化度的可行性，为构建更可靠的深度神经网络提供了有效方法。RefCal框架成功解决了校准与精化度之间的权衡问题，为提升DNN可靠性提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Although deep neural networks (DNNs) achieve high predictive accuracy, their confidence estimates are often unreliable, potentially compromising user trust in their decisions. This has motivated research on calibrated models, where calibration measures how well a model's predicted confidence aligns with the empirical probability of correctness. However, calibration metrics can often be improved through post-processing techniques that merely mimic training-time uncertainty without genuinely improving the model's understanding. For this reason, statisticians recommend that models be not only calibrated but also refined. Intuitively, a model is considered more refined if it assigns significantly different confidence scores to correct and incorrect predictions, a property also referred to as sharpness. We observe that many existing calibration methods improve calibration at the cost of reduced refinement. To address this limitation, we propose: (1) a novel loss function that explicitly promotes refinement and can be optimized through supervised contrastive learning; and (2) a unified training framework, RefCal, that jointly optimizes calibration, refinement, and accuracy to improve DNN reliability. On the CIFAR-100-LT dataset with 10 percent class imbalance, RefCal achieves (accuracy, refinement, ECE) of (58.81, 95.67, 0.08), substantially outperforming the widely used Correctness Ranking Loss, which achieves (46.27, 93.7, 0.22).

</details>


### [97] [Convex Low-resource Accent-Robust Language Detection in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23235)
*Miria Feng,William Tan,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了凸优化语言检测(CLD)框架，通过多GPU ADMM算法实现，在低资源条件下对多样化方言和口音达到97-98%检测准确率，解决了现有对话系统在代表性不足方言上的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球化与多元文化产生了日益多样化的语音变体，但现有口语对话系统在代表性不足的方言和口音上频繁失败，经常错误识别输入语言并导致下游对话任务出现级联故障。在低资源约束下解决这种方言差异仍然是一个开放性挑战，因为标准微调方法计算成本高且容易在高维语音数据上过拟合。

Method: 提出了凸优化语言检测(CLD)框架，将理论支撑的凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统流程中。方法通过JAX中的多GPU交替方向乘子法(ADMM)高效实现，提供全局最优性保证和多项式时间的快速训练。理论上证明了凸目标函数能够诱导认证边界稳定性，并提供针对特征扰动的保证。

Result: 在具有挑战性的低资源场景下实现了97-98%的准确率。经验上证明了方法的样本效率和对抗输入方言变化的鲁棒性。开源包已在PyPI上发布：https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

Conclusion: CLD框架成功解决了低资源条件下的方言差异问题，通过凸优化技术实现了理论保证与实践性能的统一，为处理多样化语音变体提供了有效解决方案，对提升口语对话系统在多元化语言环境中的鲁棒性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Globalization and multiculturalism continue to produce increasingly diverse speech varieties. Yet current spoken dialogue systems frequently fail on under-represented dialects and accents, often misidentifying the input language and causing cascading failures in downstream dialogue tasks. Addressing this dialectal variance under low-resource constraints remains an open challenge, as standard fine-tuning is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting on high-dimensional speech data. We propose Convex Language Detection (CLD), a novel framework that integrates theoretically grounded convex optimization techniques into the spoken dialogue systems pipeline. Our method is efficiently implemented via multi-GPU Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) in JAX, thus providing global optimality guarantees and fast training in polynomial time. Theoretically, we prove that our convex objective induces certified margin stability and provide guarantees against feature perturbations. Empirically, we demonstrate sample efficiency and robustness to input dialectical variation, achieving 97-98% accuracy in challenging low-resource regimes. Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

</details>


### [98] [Multi-Gate Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23259)
*Zhizhan Zheng,Feiyun Zhang,Shuchun Liu,Tian Xia,Xi Liu,Dasheng Hu,Hongquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多门控残差（MGR）机制，在不增加通信开销的情况下解决深度残差网络中激活值无界增长问题，实现大规模训练的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力残差方法虽然能缓解深度残差网络中激活值无界增长的问题，但会引入显著的通信开销，成为系统瓶颈。

Method: 设计多门控残差（MGR）机制，采用简单的评分与门控机制维持多流上下文，并结合注意力池化从流状态中提取隐藏状态，在不增加额外通信负担的情况下稳定激活尺度。

Result: 实验表明MGR适用于大规模训练和部署，相比现有架构实现了实质性的性能提升。

Conclusion: MGR是一种实用且有效的解决方案，在保持通信效率的同时解决了激活值无界增长问题，适用于大规模场景。

Abstract: While Attention Residuals has shown some effectiveness in addressing the widespread issue of unbounded activation growth across deep residual layers, it inevitably incurs significant communication overhead. To circumvent this bottleneck, we propose Multi-Gate Residuals (MGR), which stabilizes activation scales without additional communication burden. It utilizes a straightforward scoring and gating mechanism to maintain multi-stream context, coupled with Attention Pooling to extract hidden states from the stream states. Empirical experiments demonstrate that MGR is practical for large-scale training and deployment, offering tangible performance improvements over existing architectures.

</details>


### [99] [Self-supervised Adversarial Purification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23239)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图自编码器(GPR-GAE)的自监督对抗净化框架，通过独立的净化器在分类前清洗输入数据，有效解决了GNN对抗攻击防御中准确性与鲁棒性的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗训练方法将准确性和鲁棒性这两个冲突目标交织在单一分类器中，难以平衡二者关系，导致防御效果不佳。需要一种新方法来解耦这两个目标，提高图神经网络的对抗攻击防御能力。

Method: 提出自监督对抗净化框架，引入专用净化器在分类前清洗输入数据；设计GPR-GAE图自编码器作为专用净化器，采用自监督策略训练以适应多样化图结构；利用多个广义PageRank(GPR)滤波器捕获多样化结构表示；通过多步净化过程实现精确图恢复和鲁棒防御。

Result: 在多种数据集和攻击场景下的实验表明，GPR-GAE达到了最先进的鲁棒性水平，能够作为独立的即插即用净化器应用于GNN分类器，有效防御结构扰动攻击。

Conclusion: GPR-GAE成功将鲁棒性与分类器分离，通过自监督对抗净化实现了准确性与鲁棒性的有效平衡，为GNN对抗防御提供了一种独立、通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Defending Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) against adversarial attacks requires balancing accuracy and robustness, a trade-off often mishandled by traditional methods like adversarial training that intertwine these conflicting objectives within a single classifier. To overcome this limitation, we propose a self-supervised adversarial purification framework. We separate robustness from the classifier by introducing a dedicated purifier, which cleanses the input data before classification. In contrast to prior adversarial purification methods, we propose GPR-GAE, a novel graph auto-encoder (GAE), as a specialized purifier trained with a self-supervised strategy, adapting to diverse graph structures in a data-driven manner. Utilizing multiple Generalized PageRank (GPR) filters, GPR-GAE captures diverse structural representations for robust and effective purification. Our multi-step purification process further facilitates GPR-GAE to achieve precise graph recovery and robust defense against structural perturbations. Experiments across diverse datasets and attack scenarios demonstrate the state-of-the-art robustness of GPR-GAE, showcasing it as an independent plug-and-play purifier for GNN classifiers. Our code can be found at https://github.com/woodavid31/GPR-GAE.

</details>


### [100] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对符号回归中“好结构差评分”的瓶颈问题，提出SAGE-Fit框架，利用符号表达式的结构和语义先验来优化参数拟合，显著提升各类SR系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法在双层优化框架中，内层参数拟合因非线性算子导致高度非凸性，依赖快速局部求解器（如BFGS）常陷入差的局部最小值，导致正确结构的评分被低估。这种“好结构差评分”现象降低了效率并误导搜索，成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SAGE-Fit框架，利用符号表达式的双重原生先验（结构先验和语义先验），针对每种特性设计定制模块，从而有效缓解内层参数优化的瓶颈问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，SAGE-Fit作为即插即用模块，显著提升了评估保真度，并普遍改善了各种SR系统的性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用符号表达式独特的结构和语义先验，SAGE-Fit有效解决了符号回归中的参数拟合瓶颈问题，提升了系统整体性能。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

</details>


### [101] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RelPrism框架，通过构建内在、关系和混合属性的多视角多粒度自监督预训练方法，解决了关系数据库表示学习中单一视角预训练目标适应性不足的问题，在分类和回归任务上分别提升4.15% ROC-AUC和降低10.75% MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系深度学习（RDL）方法将RDB转换为图进行端到端预测，但有效的自监督预训练仍具挑战性。RDB下游任务需求多样化：如用户流失分类依赖交互模式，而消费价值预测需结合用户-物品行为和内在属性进行细粒度回归。这种异构性要求预训练目标覆盖全面信息以适应下游任务。然而现有SSL方法通常仅从单一视角（如节点级属性或子图级结构）获取监督信号，适应性有限。

Method: 提出RelPrism多视角自监督学习框架：1）从不同视角构建内在属性、关系属性和混合属性三类表示；2）对每个视角应用多粒度聚类形成对应的伪任务池；3）通过在这些伪任务池上进行预训练，使表示暴露于更广泛的视角和粒度层次，为下游适应提供更强的表示基础。

Result: 在5个真实数据集的14个任务上进行实验，RelPrism相比最先进基线方法：分类任务ROC-AUC提升4.15%，回归任务MAE降低10.75%。

Conclusion: RelPrism通过多视角多粒度的自监督预训练策略，有效解决了RDB表示学习中单一视角预训练目标适应性不足的问题，为多样化下游任务提供了更全面的表示学习基础，显著提升了关系数据库预测任务的性能。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

</details>


### [102] [Reinforcement Learning for Microcanonical Graph Ensemble with Assortativity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23285)
*Hoyun Choi,Junghyo Jo,Deok-Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度微正则图生成器(DMGG)，这是一个强化学习框架，能够通过保度重连精确达到指定的度度相关性，为生成硬约束图提供了高效且准确的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络结构决定功能是一个基础问题，现有方法存在局限：指数随机图模型(ERGM)仅在期望上强制约束，允许个体实现围绕目标波动；微正则系综施加精确的硬约束，但除固定度序列外，实际采样方法仍然难以实现。需要一种能够精确控制结构特性且实用的图生成方法。

Method: 提出深度微正则图生成器(DMGG)，一个强化学习(RL)框架，通过保度重连将任意给定图转换为精确达到预配置 assortativity（表征相邻节点的度度相关性）的图。DMGG采用策略引导搜索，最大化改变联合度矩阵，而非依赖ERGM的熵主导的Metropolis-Hastings动力学。这种方法避免了详尽的参数调优。

Result: DMGG相比ERGM方法，生成速度提升至少一个数量级，同时保持构型多样性。该方法能够泛化到各种图大小、稀疏性和拓扑结构，提供精确的零模型，允许定量分离次要观测量（如聚类系数）。DMGG成功地将强化学习确立为生成硬约束图的实用且强大的范式。

Conclusion: DMGG建立了强化学习作为生成硬约束图的实用且强大的范式，消除了系综伪影，为研究结构-功能关系开辟了新途径，能够无偏差地定量分析网络的次要结构特征。

Abstract: How network structure determines function is a fundamental question, and it can be investigated by graph ensembles with precisely controlled structural properties. Canonical approaches, formulated as exponential random graph models (ERGMs), enforce constraints only in expectation, allowing individual realizations to fluctuate around the target. Conversely, microcanonical ensembles impose hard constraints exactly, but practical sampling methods beyond fixing the degree sequence have remained out of reach. Here we introduce the Deep Microcanonical Graph Generator (DMGG), a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that transforms any given graph through degree-preserving rewirings to exactly reach a prescribed assortativity, which characterizes the degree--degree correlation of adjacent nodes. Instead of relying on the entropically dominated Metropolis--Hastings dynamics of the ERGM, DMGG employs a policy-guided search that maximally alters the joint-degree matrix. This eliminates exhaustive parameter tuning and accelerates generation by at least an order of magnitude while preserving configurational diversity. As DMGG generalizes across various graph sizes, sparsities, and topologies, it provides exact null models that allow for the quantitative isolation of secondary observables, such as the clustering coefficient. These results establish RL as a practical and powerful paradigm for generating hard-constrained graphs, opening avenues to investigate structure-function relationships free from ensemble artifacts.

</details>


### [103] [Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23244)
*Miria Feng,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了COALA算法，首个将凸优化应用于大语言模型偏好微调的方法。相比DPO，计算量仅需~17.6%，VRAM消耗显著降低，可在单GPU上高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐方法如RLHF计算成本高且复杂，DPO虽简化了流程但存在排名准确性不一致、GPU资源需求大、超参数调节昂贵等问题，亟需一种轻量级且理论保证强的替代方案。

Method: 提出COALA（Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning Algorithm），利用神经网络的凸优化重构来消除对参考模型的依赖，从而显著减少训练时间和显存消耗。

Result: 在四个数据集（含26621样本的合成教育反馈数据集）和六个模型（包括Llama-3.1-8B）上的实验表明，COALA仅需DPO约17.6%的总TFLOPs即可达到竞争性能，训练效率显著提升，奖励稳定单调递增，峰值边际在更短时间内达到。

Conclusion: COALA首次有效将凸优化应用于LLM偏好微调，实现了轻量化、高效且理论保证强的对齐训练，为LLM对齐领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) to align with human preferences has driven the success of systems such as Gemini and ChatGPT. However, approaches like Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) remain computationally expensive and complex. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a simpler alternative but has limitations such as inconsistent ranking accuracy, high dependence on GPU resources, and expensive hyperparameter tuning. We propose the Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning Algorithm (COALA): a novel lightweight strategy with strong theoretical guarantees. By leveraging the convex optimization reformulation of neural networks, COALA eliminates the need for a reference model and obtains significant reduction in both training time and VRAM consumption, thus enabling efficient training on a single GPU. Experiments across four datasets--including a 26621-sample synthetic Educational Feedback dataset--and six models (including Llama-3.1-8B) demonstrate COALA's competitive performance and efficiency while utilizing as little as ~17.6% of DPO's total TFLOPs. COALA exhibits stable, monotonically increasing rewards and reaches peak margins in significantly shorter time in comparison to traditional methods such as DPO and ORPO. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time convex optimization has been effectively applied to preference fine-tuning of LLMs.

</details>


### [104] [Accelerating Divisible Load Processing Through Machine Learning: A Practical Framework for Large-Scale Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23247)
*Bharadwaj Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次提出基于前馈神经网络的机器学习框架，用于预测单层树网络(SLTN)架构下可分负载理论(DLT)的最优处理时间，实现97-99%的预测准确率和10-100倍的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLT计算需要显式构建负载分配方程，计算耗时，难以满足实时调度、设计空间探索和云资源分配等场景对快速优化的需求。

Method: 构建包含16个工程特征的前馈神经网络(FNN)，在10万个合成配置上训练模型，直接预测最优处理时间而无需显式求解DLT方程。

Result: 模型达到97-99%的R²准确率，平均绝对百分比误差为1-5%，推理时间小于1毫秒，相比传统DLT计算实现10-100倍加速；特征重要性分析表明模型隐式捕获了DLT的数学结构；在n=3到20、负载大小1-100GB的多样化配置下表现一致良好。

Conclusion: 证明了机器学习可以在保持近乎最优准确性的同时加速分布式计算优化，为实时调度、设计空间探索和云资源分配等应用提供了可行方案，但在极大规模或高度异构系统上性能略有下降。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the first machine learning framework for predicting optimal processing times in Single-Level Tree Network (SLTN) architectures for the Divisible Load Theory (DLT) paradigm. Using a feedforward neural network(FNN) with 16 engineered features, we train a model on 100,000 synthetically generated configurations to predict optimal processing times without explicit formulation of DLT equations. The model achieves 97-99% accuracy (R-square factor) with mean absolute percentage error of 1-5%, demonstrating that neural networks can effectively learn complex load distribution relationships. Feature importance analysis reveals that the model implicitly captures DLT mathematical structure, including load conservation and simultaneous finishing constraints. With inference times under 1 millisecond, the approach provides 10-100x speedup over traditional DLT computation, enabling applications in real-time scheduling, design space exploration, and cloud resource allocation. The method generalizes well across diverse system configurations (n=3 to 20, load size =1 to 100 GB) with consistent accuracy, though performance degrades slightly for very large or highly heterogeneous systems. This work demonstrates the feasibility of using machine learning to accelerate distributed computing optimization while maintaining near-optimal accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23365)
*Kyungyoon Kim,Donghyeon Ki,Hee-Jun Ahn,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SOM算法，通过Q函数直接构建目标速度场，实现了强化学习中的单步生成策略，在保持SOTA性能的同时大幅降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配方法在强化学习中虽然表达能力强，但多步去噪过程带来巨大的推理计算开销，这在在线强化学习场景中尤为严重。MeanFlow虽然可以通过单次网络评估实现生成，但需要目标分布的样本来构建目标速度场，而在线强化学习中这些样本不可用。

Method: 提出基于分数的单步MeanFlow策略优化（SOM）算法，这是一个actor-critic方法。核心创新是通过分数估计和概率流ODE直接从Q函数构建目标速度场，从而将概率质量集中到高价值的模式上，避免了需要目标分布样本的限制。

Result: 在完全在线强化学习设置下，SOM算法在运动控制任务上实现了单步生成的SOTA性能。与传统扩散模型和流匹配策略相比，SOM在训练和推理时间上都实现了大幅降低。

Conclusion: SOM成功解决了MeanFlow在在线强化学习中的样本依赖问题，实现了计算效率和策略性能的良好平衡，为在线强化学习中的高效策略学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching have emerged as expressive policy classes in reinforcement learning, but their reliance on multi-step denoising imposes substantial computational overhead at inference time, which is particularly problematic in online RL. MeanFlow offers a promising alternative by learning an average velocity field that maps noise to data in a single network evaluation. However, MeanFlow typically requires samples from the target distribution to construct its target velocity field, which are unavailable in online RL. We propose Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization (SOM), an actor-critic algorithm that resolves this by constructing the target velocity field directly from the Q-function via score estimation and a probability flow ODE, thereby concentrating probability mass on high-value modes. In the fully online RL setting, SOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on locomotion tasks with a single generation step, while substantially reducing both training and inference time compared to prior diffusion- and flow-matching-based policies.

</details>


### [106] [Curriculum reinforcement learning with measurable task representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23372)
*Yongyan Wen,Siyuan Li,Mingjian Fu,Yiqin Yang,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可度量任务表示学习的自动课程生成方法，通过变分自编码器将任务空间映射到潜在空间以实现有效的任务相似性度量，在复杂导航任务中超越了现有的插值和生成对抗网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在课程强化学习（CRL）中，基于插值的CRL范式依赖于有意义的距离度量来衡量任务相似性，但在具有非欧几里得上下文（任务）空间的复杂导航任务中，这一假设失效，导致无法有效生成中间任务。

Method: 提出基于可度量任务表示学习的自动课程生成方法：利用变分自编码器结构编码奖励和状态转移，将任务空间转换到潜在空间，获得具有任务相似性度量属性的任务表示；基于学习到的任务表示开发自动课程生成方案，逐步生成与目标任务越来越相似的新任务。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的导航任务上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法超越了基于插值和生成对抗网络的最先进CRL方法。

Conclusion: 通过将任务空间映射到具有可度量性的潜在空间，该方法有效解决了复杂导航任务中任务相似性度量困难的问题，实现了自动课程生成，为挑战性任务的学习提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In curriculum reinforcement learning (CRL), an agent incrementally accumulates knowledge over a sequence of tasks (i.e., a curriculum), and the learning process is aimed at using the accumulated knowledge to finally solve a challenging target task. While early CRL works focus on sequencing candidate tasks, recent research explores automatic curriculum generation. Among the rich CRL literature, the interpolation-based CRL paradigm is a main body, which automatically generates intermediate tasks by interpolating between the initial task distribution and the target task distribution in task space with meaningful distance metrics (i.e., can measure the task similarity). However, in challenging navigation tasks, the non-Euclidean context (task) space invalidates this assumption. To achieve automatic curriculum generation in complex task, we propose a novel automatic curriculum generation approach based on measurable task representation learning. To better measure the similarity, we propose to transform the task space to a latent space. Through a variational autoencoder structure that encodes the reward and the state transitions, we achieve a latent task representation with a task similarity measurement property, and two close task embeddings correspond to two similar tasks in terms of rewards and state transitions. Based on the learned task representation, we further develop an automatic curriculum generation scheme, which can effectively generate new tasks more and more similar to the target task. We evaluate our method in a variety of challenging navigation tasks, and the experiment results indicate that the proposed approach surpasses state-of-the-art CRL approaches based on interpolation and generative adversarial networks.

</details>


### [107] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统研究学习增强调度中延迟优化与抢占开销的权衡，提出了在保持O(1)竞争比的同时将每作业抢占次数限制为O(1)的算法，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习增强在线调度研究主要优化作业延迟但依赖频繁的“盲目”抢占，忽略了算法性能与抢占复杂性之间的基本权衡。

Method: 建立理论框架桥接延迟界限与抢占开销之间的差距，设计基于预测的竞争算法，针对单机、不相关并行机和可塑机等场景提出有限抢占策略，其中开销随预测误差对数增长。

Result: 在准确预测下实现了单机和不相关并行机上每作业仅O(1)次抢占的O(1)竞争算法，开销随预测误差呈对数增长；首次为不相关和可塑机器提供了有界抢占保证。

Conclusion: 通过建立坚实的理论基础，证明了学习增强框架可有效应用于更受限、更现实的调度场景，在优化延迟的同时控制抢占开销，理论界限通过实验得到验证。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations. Our results include $O(1)$-competitive algorithms for single and unrelated parallel machines with only $O(1)$ preemptions per job under accurate predictions, with overhead scaling logarithmically with the prediction error. By providing the first bounded-preemption guarantees for unrelated and malleable machines, we extend the theoretical reach of the learning-augmented framework to more constrained and realistic settings. Finally, our algorithms are validated through experiments.

</details>


### [108] [Every Component is a Lookup: Token Attribution and Composition from a Single Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23393)
*Po-Kai Chen,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unpack方法，通过反向递归分解transformer中attention和MLP子层的贡献，无需干预、梯度或辅助训练即可实现组件交互分析、端到端路径识别和token级归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的可解释性研究不仅需要识别关键组件，还需要揭示这些组件如何组合成产生预测的计算路径。现有方法难以有效追踪attention和MLP子层之间的信用传递和组合方式。

Method: 利用attention和MLP共享的key-value模板φ(S)U结构，开发了Unpack——一种反向递归算法，通过两个子层分解信用传递，产生组件间的交互强度、带有K/Q/V组合标签的端到端路径，以及单次前向传播的token级归因。

Result: 在GPT-2 small的间接对象识别任务上，成功恢复Wang等人描述的全部三种组合连接及其模式特定路由；在重复名称检测中，首次出现的名称保持强信用而重复检测位置被抑制；该抑制模式在Pythia系列（160M到6.9B参数）的所有规模上一致出现。

Conclusion: Unpack方法能够在无需真实电路标签的情况下有效追踪机制结构，证明了该方法在不同模型规模上的有效性和一致性，为transformer的可解释性研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires identifying not just which components matter but how they compose into the computational route that produced a prediction. Both attention and MLP follow a shared key-value template $φ(S)U$. We exploit this structure to develop Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through both sublayers, producing interaction strengths between any two components, named end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass, without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training. We evaluate on the indirect object identification task. On GPT-2 small, the method recovers all three composition connections described by Wang et al. (2023), including the mode-specific routing of each connection (K, Q, or V). To test token-level attribution beyond trivial copying, we compare two occurrences of the same name in the same decomposition: the first mention retains strong credit while the duplicate-detection position is suppressed, a pattern absent in matched control prompts. Across the Pythia family from 160M to 6.9B parameters, this suppression pattern is consistently recovered at every scale, demonstrating that the method tracks mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels. Code is available at https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm.

</details>


### [109] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR是一种即插即用的KV cache压缩增强方法，通过引入保留、近似和驱逐三路令牌路由，结合重要性和可重构性信号，在中高压缩率下显著改善了质量-内存权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV cache的增长是主要瓶颈。现有方法主要依赖二元驱逐或表示近似，可能未充分利用那些非关键但可重构的令牌，导致有用信息的不可逆丢失。

Method: 提出VECTOR方法，在基于驱逐的流水线中引入三路令牌路由机制：保留、近似和驱逐。结合基础评分器的重要性信号与离线校准的回归值估计的可重构性信号，利用可重构性恢复在二元驱逐下会丢失的有价值信息，同时保持关键向量以维持注意力路由稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，VECTOR在中高压缩率下改善了质量-内存权衡，尤其在更严格的预算约束下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: VECTOR通过三路路由和可重构性信号的有效结合，为KV cache压缩提供了更优的解决方案，在保持推理质量的同时实现了更好的内存效率。

Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

</details>


### [110] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参数化先验映射(PPM)框架，将参数化结构先验注入生成模型，在概率多变量时间序列预测中实现了表达性与鲁棒性的平衡，在非平稳数据上取得更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率多变量时间序列预测方法存在两难困境：参数化方法具有强归纳偏置和鲁棒性但缺乏灵活性；深度生成模型表达能力强但需要大量数据和计算资源才能捕捉复杂时间依赖。需要一种方法能同时兼顾参数化方法的效率和生成模型的表达能力。

Method: 提出参数化先验映射(PPM)框架，核心是利用参数化估计器推导动态自适应先验，通过可学习映射指导复杂预测分布的学习。该设计使用混合目标函数训练，将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程，使模型保留参数化方法的效率同时利用生成模型的表达能力。

Result: 实证结果表明PPM在处理非平稳数据方面优于现有基线方法，能够产生精确预测并提供良好校准的不确定性估计，在准确性与计算效率之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: PPM框架成功地将参数化方法的优势与生成模型的表达能力相结合，为非平稳多变量时间序列的概率预测提供了有效的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping. This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

</details>


### [111] [Reflex: Reinforcement Learning with Reflection Symmetry Exploitation in State-Based Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23415)
*Shuai Zhen,Yifan Zhang,Yuling Wang,Yanhua Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex框架，通过利用反射对称性来提升强化学习的样本效率，在状态连续控制任务中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习长期面临样本效率低下的问题。现有的利用群不变马尔可夫决策过程的方法主要关注基于图像的强化学习和旋转对称性（如SO(2)），而基于状态的强化学习和反射对称性研究较少。

Method: 提出Reflex范式，定义轴向反射和双侧反射两种反射类型，基于对称性保持的最优值函数和策略的理论分析，通过原则性的对称性正则化机制将反射对称性集成到策略学习中。Reflex可与on-policy和off-policy强化学习算法无缝集成，文中实现与PPO和SAC的结合。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准测试套件上进行评估，Reflex相比标准基线表现出更优越的性能，同时提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 反射对称性的利用是提升强化学习样本效率的有效途径，Reflex框架成功地将理论分析与实际算法实现相结合，为基于状态的连续控制任务提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has long struggled with poor sample efficiency. One promising approach to mitigate this problem is leveraging group-invariant Markov Decision Processes ($G$-invariant MDPs). Existing works in this direction have primarily focused on image-based RL and rotational symmetry such as $\mathrm{SO(2)}$, leaving state-based RL and reflection symmetry largely underexplored. In this work, we focus on state-based continuous control tasks and exploit reflection symmetry by introducing Reflex, a paradigm that seamlessly integrates with both on-policy and off-policy RL algorithms. We formalize two types of reflection-axial reflection and bilateral reflection, and characterize their corresponding transformations. Building on a theoretical analysis of symmetry-preserving optimal value functions and policies, Reflex integrates reflection symmetry into policy learning through principled symmetry regularization mechanisms. We integrate Reflex with PPO and SAC, and evaluate it on a suite of OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance over standard baselines while improving sample efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Individual Dynamics from Sparse Cross-Sectional Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23470)
*Christian Lagemann,Kai Lagemann,Steven L. Brunton,Sach Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CADENCE框架，能够在仅有稀疏快照数据的情况下，通过静态个体上下文锚定潜在动力学，恢复连续的个体轨迹，打破了现有方法在密集纵向数据与横截面数据之间的严格妥协，并在多个基准测试中达到了与密集全轨迹数据训练模型相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹推断方法存在一个严格的妥协：序列模型（如潜在ODE）需要密集的纵向数据，而横截面方法（如最优传输、流匹配）只能映射聚合群体，丢失个体动力学。当仅有极稀疏或完全横截面的数据时，推断个体化连续时间轨迹在根本上是一个不适定问题，这限制了在个体衰老、流行病传播、物理系统退化等场景中的应用。

Method: 提出CADENCE概率框架，通过将潜在动力学锚定到静态个体级上下文，从孤立快照恢复连续个体轨迹。方法结合了基于分数的空间编码器（双射概率流ODE）以消除微分同胚歧义，以及软专家混合路由器，证明了个体动力学参数和路由函数的联合可识别性，为单时间点轨迹推断提供了新颖的可识别性保证。

Result: 在跨越物理系统到真实生物数据的基准测试套件中，CADENCE仅在极稀疏快照和上下文结构上训练，其性能匹配或超过了在密集全轨迹数据上训练的最先进序列模型。证明了单时间点轨迹推断的可识别性理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了密集纵向数据与横截面方法之间的二分法可以被打破，为从稀疏快照数据中恢复个体轨迹提供了原则性的概率框架。这不仅扩展了轨迹推断的适用范围，也为仅使用横截面数据研究个体动力学开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Predicting how a dynamical unit evolves over time - how an individual ages, an epidemic spreads, or a physical system degrades - typically requires dense longitudinal tracking. When only extremely sparse or entirely cross-sectional data is available, inferring individualized, continuous-time trajectories is fundamentally ill-posed. Existing methods force a strict compromise: sequence models (e.g. latent ODEs) require dense longitudinal data, while cross-sectional methods (e.g. optimal transport, flow matching-based) map aggregate populations, losing individual dynamics. In this paper, we demonstrate that this dichotomy can be broken. We introduce CADENCE, a principled probabilistic framework that recovers continuous individual trajectories from isolated snapshots by anchoring latent dynamics to static, individual-level contexts. We provide novel identifiability guarantees for single-timepoint trajectory inference. By combining a score-based spatial encoder (bijective Probability Flow ODE) to eliminate diffeomorphic ambiguities with a Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) router, we show that individual dynamical parameters and routing function are jointly identifiable. Across a suite of benchmarks spanning physical systems to real-world biological data, CADENCE, trained strictly on extremely sparse snapshots with context structure, matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art sequential models trained on dense, full-trajectory data.

</details>


### [113] [Diffusion Domain Expansion: Learning to Coordinate Pre-trained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23275)
*Egor Lifar,Semyon Savkin,Timur Garipov,Shangyuan Tong,Tommi Jaakkola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散域扩展（DDE）方法，通过协调预训练扩散模型的去噪输出，高效扩展模型以生成更大对象并处理更复杂条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在生成超出其原始训练域的更大对象和处理更复杂条件时能力受限，需要一种高效方法扩展其能力边界。

Method: 设计一个紧凑的可训练协调网络来协调预训练扩散模型的去噪输出，该协调器结构简单且能泛化到比训练时更大的域。

Result: 在长音频轨道生成和条件图像生成任务上验证了DDE的有效性，在定性和定量评估中均优于其他协调生成方法。

Conclusion: DDE方法成功实现了扩散模型能力的有效扩展，具有跨域适用性，为扩散模型在更大规模和更复杂条件下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Diffusion Domain Expansion (DDE), a method that efficiently extends pre-trained diffusion models to generate larger objects and handle more complex conditioning beyond their original capabilities. Our method employs a compact trainable network designed to coordinate the denoised outputs of pre-trained diffusion models. We demonstrate that the coordinator can be universally simple while being capable of generalizing to domains larger than those observed during its training time. We evaluate DDE on long audio track generation and conditional image generation, demonstrating its applicability across domains. DDE outperforms other approaches to coordinated generation with diffusion models in qualitative and quantitative evaluations.

</details>


### [114] [CBANet: A Compact Attention-Based CNN-BiLSTM Network for Aggressive Driving Event Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23471)
*Hanadi Alhamdan,Ghadah Alosaimi,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei,Farshad Arvin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强的深度学习框架，通过构造车辆动力学特征、稳定训练策略和安全导向的决策策略，有效解决了真实场景下激进行为检测中的数据不平衡、驾驶员差异性大等问题，在召回率和安全性指标上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进行为驾驶是交通事故的主要原因，现有深度学习方法在真实场景中的性能受限于严重的数据不平衡、驾驶员间的大差异性以及缺乏物理可解释的车辆动力学表示。

Method: 提出基于多变量车辆动力学信号的增强深度学习框架，构造了捕捉转向、加速和制动行为的工程化动态特征；采用结合SMOTE过采样和类别加权损失的稳定训练策略；引入基于类别特定阈值校准的安全导向决策策略。

Result: 在新收集的自然驾驶数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法在少数类召回率和安全关键F-score指标上显著优于标准深度学习基线，同时保持实际计算效率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了真实驾驶环境中激进行为检测的关键挑战，为提高道路安全提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Aggressive driving is a major cause of traffic accidents and poses a serious threat to road safety. Although deep learning methods have shown promising results in detecting risky driving behaviours from vehicle sensor data, their performance in real-world conditions is often limited by severe data imbalance, large variability between drivers, and the lack of physically interpretable vehicle dynamics representations. In this paper, we propose an enhanced deep learning framework for aggressive driving detection using multivariate vehicle dynamics signals. Instead of relying solely on raw measurements, the proposed approach constructs engineered dynamic features that capture steering, acceleration, and braking behaviour. To address the extreme rarity of aggressive events in naturalistic driving data, we introduce a stable training strategy that combines controlled SMOTE-based oversampling with a class-weighted loss formulation, and evaluates focal loss variants for imbalance handling. Furthermore, a safety-oriented decision strategy based on class-specific threshold calibration is adopted to better reflect the asymmetric risks of missed detections and false alarms in real-world applications. The proposed framework is evaluated on a newly collected naturalistic driving dataset. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method consistently outperforms standard deep learning baselines with significant improvements in minority-class recall and safety-critical F-score metrics while maintaining practical computational efficiency. Code: \url {https://github.com/halhamdan/CBANet}

</details>


### [115] [Automated Random Embedding for Practical Bayesian Optimization with Unknown Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23473)
*Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Xiang Xia,Xuhui Liu,Yangde Fu,Bei Liang,Huibin Wang,Liang Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSEBO方法，通过动态调整子空间维度和跨子空间共享解，解决了高维贝叶斯优化中有效维度未知的难题，显著提升了优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在高维空间中面临维度灾难问题。随机嵌入作为降维策略虽有效，但需要预先确定任务的有效维度，这一难题限制了子空间维度的选择和优化性能。现有方法依赖专家指定固定维度或通过试错估计，资源消耗大且效率低。

Method: 提出动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化（DSEBO）方法：1）从低维度子空间开始优化；2）当当前子空间解呈现初步收敛时切换到更高维子空间；3）根据不同子空间中解的质量动态确定下一子空间维度；4）将已查询的解共享到新子空间以实现更好的初始化。理论上推导了DSEBO的遗憾界，证明其能更好地平衡近似误差和优化误差。

Result: 在具有不同数量级维度的函数和有效维度未知的实际任务上进行了大量实验。与最先进方法相比，DSEBO在不同子空间间交替优化显著提升了高维优化性能，在优化遗憾和时间方面均有显著改善。

Conclusion: DSEBO成功实现了对未知有效维度的高维贝叶斯优化问题的自动化随机嵌入，通过动态维度调整和跨子空间知识共享机制，有效平衡了近似与优化误差，为高维黑盒函数优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely employed for optimizing complex black-box functions but struggles with the curse of dimensionality. Random embedding, as a dimension reduction strategy, simplifies tasks that possess the effective dimension by optimizing within a low-dimensional subspace. However, determining the effective dimension of a task in advance remains a significant challenge, which influences the selection of the subspace dimensionality and the optimization performance. Traditional methods use fixed subspace dimensions provided by experts or rely on trial and error to estimate subspace dimensions with resources consumed. To this end, this paper proposes an automated random embedding for high-dimensional Bayesian optimization with unknown effective dimension, called Dynamic Shared Embedding Bayesian Optimization (DSEBO). DSEBO starts with a low dimension and switches to a higher subspace if the solutions in the current subspace show preliminary convergence. DSEBO dynamically determines the dimension of the next subspace based on the quality of the solutions in different subspaces and shares the queried solutions with the new subspace for a better initialization. Theoretically, we derive a regret bound for DSEBO and demonstrate that DSEBO can better balance approximation and optimization errors. Extensive experiments on functions with dimensionality of varying magnitudes and real-world tasks with unknown effective dimensions reveal that, compared with state-of-the-art methods, alternating optimization across different subspaces results in significant improvements in high-dimensional optimization, both in terms of optimization regret and time.

</details>


### [116] [Contrastive Distribution Matching for Amortized Sequential Monte Carlo in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23346)
*Jaihoon Kim,Taehoon Yoon,Prin Phunyaphibarn,Seungjun Kim,Morteza Mardani,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了对比分布匹配（CDM）框架，通过学习参数化扭曲函数来降低离散扩散模型中扭曲SMC推理的计算成本，在少于5%的额外计算开销下显著提升了奖励倾斜分布采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中的奖励倾斜分布采样是一个核心挑战。虽然扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）能够提供渐近精确性，但在离散状态空间中估计最优扭曲函数需要昂贵的蒙特卡洛近似，导致推理时存在严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 引入对比分布匹配（CDM）框架，通过正负样本学习参数化扭曲函数来摊销SMC推理成本。重新设计梯度估计器以利用离散扩散模型的闭式前向核，从而实现高效训练。

Result: 学习到的扭曲函数评估仅需少于5%的额外计算开销（相比基础模型的单次前向传播）。在相同计算时间下，CDM在各种应用中均优于现有基线方法，包括有毒文本生成、调控DNA序列设计、蛋白质可设计性以及扩散大语言模型对齐等任务。

Conclusion: CDM框架成功解决了离散扩散模型中扭曲SMC推理的计算瓶颈问题，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升了性能，具有广泛的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have emerged as powerful frameworks for generating structured categorical data. However, efficiently sampling from reward-tilted distributions remains a fundamental challenge. While Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) offers asymptotic exactness for this task, estimating the optimal twist function in discrete state spaces necessitates costly Monte Carlo approximations, resulting a severe computational bottleneck at inference. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contrastive Distribution Matching (CDM), a novel framework that amortizes the cost of SMC inference by learning a parameterized twist function via positive and negative samples. For efficient training, we reformulate the gradient estimator to leverage the closed-form forward kernels of discrete diffusion models. In practice, evaluating our learned twist function incurs less than 5% additional computational overhead compared to a single forward pass of the base model. Through extensive empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that CDM consistently outperforms existing baselines under matched wall-clock time. We validate the effectiveness and versatility of our approach across a diverse range of applications, including toxic text generation, regulatory DNA sequence design, protein designability, and diffusion large language model alignment.

</details>


### [117] [CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23491)
*Zhangyi Hu,Chenhui Liu,Tian Huang,Jindong Li,Yang Yang,Jiemin Wu,Zining Zhong,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CoSPlay框架，一种无需真实标签（GT-free）且无需训练的代码生成方法，通过代码与单元测试的协同自我博弈，在无真实数据的情况下实现了与强化学习方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码生成方法（RLVR和TTS）都严重依赖Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs)：RLVR方法需要GT UTs进行昂贵的训练，而TTS方法在没有GT UTs时竞争力下降。现有的GT-free TTS方法直接使用自生成的单元测试，但这些测试往往存在噪声或与错误代码存在虚假耦合，且在没有可靠代码的情况下无法验证测试质量，因此需要同时改进代码和测试。

Method: 提出CoSPlay框架，通过协同自我博弈联合改进代码和单元测试：(1) 探索多样化解决方案并识别潜在失败模式以生成有区分度的测试思路；(2) 利用Code-UT执行矩阵的双向通过计数信号迭代修剪或修复弱代码、刷新或替换不可靠的测试，使两个池协同进化；(3) 当多个代码具有相同最高通过计数时，从最大输出一致性聚类中选择最终代码，因为正确代码在相同输入上达成一致而错误代码会发散。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，CoSPlay使Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的平均Best-of-N (BoN)从22.1%提升至33.2%，单元测试准确率从14.6%提升至78.3%，达到或超越RLVR模型CURE-7B的性能。应用于CURE-7B时，BoN进一步提升了5.7%。CoSPlay在不同backbone上具有良好的泛化性，在可比token预算下优于GT-free TTS基线方法，且随着预算增加持续获得性能提升。

Conclusion: CoSPlay证明了无需任何真实标签数据的可扩展推理策略可以实现具有竞争力的代码生成性能，为解决代码生成中对Ground-Truth数据的依赖问题提供了有效方案，具有重要的实践意义和应用价值。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and Test-Time Scaling (TTS) have advanced LLM code generation through executable verification. Yet Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) remain a bottleneck: SOTA RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing TTS methods lose competitiveness without them. This motivates GT-free TTS, where existing methods directly use self-generated UTs to refine and select code candidates. Yet such UTs are often noisy or spuriously coupled with wrong code, and UT quality in turn cannot be validated without reliable code. The key challenge is therefore to jointly improve both. To this end, we present CoSPlay, a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly improves codes and UTs through cooperative self-play. It first explores diverse solution ideas and identifies their potential failure modes to produce discriminative UT ideas. It then uses bidirectional pass-count signals from the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune or fix weak codes and refresh or replace unreliable UTs, letting the two pools co-evolve. Finally, when multiple codes remain tied at the highest pass count, it picks the final code from the largest output-consensus cluster, since correct codes agree on the same inputs while wrong codes diverge. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks show that CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct improves average BoN from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. When applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. CoSPlay also generalizes across diverse backbones and outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, with continued gains as the budget scales up. These results suggest a scalable inference strategy for competitive code generation without any GT data.

</details>


### [118] [Prudent-Banker: No Extra Fees for Baseline Safety in Adversarial Bandits With and Without Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23351)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Prudent-Banker算法，首次在延迟反馈的对抗性多臂赌博机问题中实现了安全性与鲁棒性的最优权衡，达到了最小最大最优的最坏情况伪遗憾Õ(√T+√D)，同时保持相对于安全基准策略的近乎常数遗憾Õ(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全感知对抗性多臂赌博机算法在即时反馈下能够平衡学习性能与安全性，但在存在延迟反馈时，延迟会导致保守与探索之间的时机错配，从而危及安全性保证。延迟反馈环境下的安全性约束问题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 提出Prudent-Banker算法，将延迟自适应的在线镜像下降方法与改进的分阶段激进机制相结合。关键技术贡献是设计了延迟校准的重启阈值，严格考虑了未观测反馈引起的最坏情况失真，可靠地检测比较器次优性。同时建立了安全约束延迟赌博机的新的下界。

Result: Prudent-Banker算法在有延迟和无延迟情况下均实现了最优的安全-鲁棒性权衡：伪遗憾Õ(√T+√D)加上相对于安全比较器的Õ(1)遗憾。实验表明，与标准延迟鲁棒基线方法不同，Prudent-Banker在各种延迟分布下有效平衡了安全性与学习性能。

Conclusion: Prudent-Banker是首个在有延迟和无延迟设置下均达到最优安全-鲁棒性权衡的算法，其遗憾保证在对数因子范围内不可改进，填补了延迟反馈环境下安全性约束赌博机研究的空白。

Abstract: We study adversarial multi-armed bandits with and without delayed feedback under a safety-aware goal: achieving minimax-optimal worst-case regret while keeping nearly constant regret relative to a designated "safe" baseline policy. Existing approaches can balance this trade-off with immediate feedback for smooth comparators, but arbitrary delays can mistime transitions between conservatism and exploration, endangering the safety guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose Prudent-Banker, a novel algorithm that combines a delay-adapted variant of Online Mirror Descent with a modified phased-aggression mechanism. Its key technical contribution is a delay-calibrated restart threshold that rigorously accounts for the worst-case distortion induced by unobserved feedback and reliably detects comparator suboptimality. We also establish new lower bounds for safety-constrained adversarial delayed bandits, showing that the regret guarantees of Prudent-Banker are unimprovable, up to logarithmic factors, under the baseline-safety requirement. To the best of our knowledge, Prudent-Banker is the first algorithm to achieve the optimal safety--robustness trade-off: pseudo-regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ together with $\widetilde{O}(1)$ regret against the safe comparator, both with and without delays. Experiments across diverse delay distributions show that, unlike standard delay-robust baselines, Prudent-Banker effectively balances safety and learning.

</details>


### [119] [VACE: Learning Geometrically Structured Representations for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23504)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Isaac Triguero,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VACE方法，通过速度一致性目标学习紧凑且方向一致的正常表示，结合位置和方向分数实现多元时间序列异常检测，在TSB-AD-M基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列异常检测的核心挑战是学习足够精确的正常性表征以标记偏离。现有对比式自监督学习方法通过成对采样启发式策略间接塑造嵌入空间，无法显式控制基于距离评分所需的几何结构，即正常表示的紧密程度和距离的方向意义。

Method: 提出VACE（速度对齐通道嵌入）方法：(1) 通过速度一致性目标训练通道感知编码器，无需负样本或合成异常；(2) 使正常轨迹局部平滑且对齐，将正常性表示为嵌入空间中紧凑、方向一致的区域；(3) 测试时将Mahalanobis位置分数和速度库方向分数进行乘法组合，同时识别分布外和动态异常点。

Result: VACE在TSB-AD-M基准的严格评估下达到最先进性能，显著优于在更大训练预算下训练的更复杂方法。

Conclusion: VACE通过简单的速度一致性目标实现了对嵌入空间几何结构的显式控制，证明了无需负样本和合成异常的自监督方法可以有效解决多元时间序列异常检测问题，为该领域提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a critical task across a wide range of real-world applications, where abnormal behaviour is rare, labels are unavailable, and the cost of a miss is high. The central challenge is learning a characterisation of normality precise enough to flag deviations. Representation self-supervised learning, typically through contrastive approaches, addresses this by embedding temporal patches into a latent space where normality occupies a well-defined region, with anomalies detected by geometric deviation. However, contrastive approaches shape this space indirectly through pair-sampling heuristics, providing no explicit control over the geometric structure that distance-based scoring requires. This means how tightly normal representations are grouped, and whether distances are directionally meaningful. We present VACE (Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings), a self-supervised anomaly detection method that represents normality as a compact, directionally coherent region in the embedding space. To this end, VACE trains a channel-aware encoder through a velocity-consistency objective, with no negatives and no synthetic anomalies, so that normal trajectories are locally smooth and aligned. At test time, a Mahalanobis positional score and a velocity-bank directional score are combined multiplicatively, flagging points that are simultaneously off-distribution and dynamically atypical. Despite its simplicity, VACE achieves state-of-the-art performance on TSB-AD-M under rigorous evaluation, significantly outperforming more complex methods trained on substantially larger budgets.

</details>


### [120] [Instance-Optimal Estimation with Multiple LLM Judges on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23362)
*Junghyun Lee,Sanghwa Kim,Yassir Jedra,Alexandre Proutière,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定预算下，如何将评估查询分配给异质化的LLM评判者以获得最准确的评分估计，提出了逆方差加权估计器(IVWE)及自适应算法EST-IVWE，并证明了其实例最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估协议越来越普及，但评估成本高昂：不同评判者具有不同的价格和可靠性，且每个提示-响应对的难度差异很大。这引出一个基础的资源分配问题：在固定预算下，如何将评估查询分配给异质化的评判者和实例，以获得最准确的评分估计？

Method: 将问题形式化为"预算约束异方差多评判者估计"问题。首先分析逆方差加权估计器(IVWE)，推导最小化错误率的oracle分配；针对未知方差的实际情况，提出EST-IVWE自适应算法，构建并利用乐观偏置的方差估计来稳定经验分配。理论证明中使用Assouad型期望论证而非Fano型高概率论证，以保持局部方差结构。

Result: EST-IVWE算法在预算范围内匹配oracle IVWE的收敛速率（仅差低阶项）；证明了匹配的局部极小极大下界，确立了所提算法的实例最优性；在合成数据集和HelpSteer2数据集上的数值实验验证了该方法优于朴素均匀分配策略。

Conclusion: 本文为预算约束下的LLM评估提供了理论基础和实用算法，通过新颖的Assouad型论证技术获得了敏锐的分配依赖下界，证明了所提方法的实例最优性，为实际LLM评估中的资源分配问题提供了理论指导和解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models increasingly relies on LLM-as-a-judge protocols, but such evaluations remain costly: different judges have different prices and reliabilities, and the difficulty of each prompt-response pair can vary substantially. This raises a basic allocation question: under a fixed budget, how should one distribute evaluation queries across heterogeneous judges and instances to obtain the most accurate score estimates? We formalize this question as *budgeted heteroskedastic multi-judge estimation*. Given $K$ prompt-response pairs, $J$ judges with known costs, and unknown query-judge variances, the goal is to estimate a bounded score vector while minimizing an $\ell_p$-error. Our first contribution is to analyze the inverse-variance weighted estimator (IVWE) and to derive the oracle allocation that minimizes its error rate. Since this allocation depends on the unknown variances, we then address the practical unknown-variance setting by proposing EST-IVWE, an adaptive algorithm that constructs and leverages *optimistically biased* variance estimates to stabilize the empirical allocation. We prove that EST-IVWE matches the oracle IVWE rate up to lower-order terms in the budget. Our second and central theoretical contribution is a matching *local* minimax lower bound, which establishes the instance-optimality of the proposed algorithms. A key technical insight is that Fano-type high-probability arguments are too coarse for this problem: their packing construction loses the local variance structure that governs the optimal allocation. We instead use an Assouad-type in-expectation argument, based on local perturbations, which preserves this structure and yields the sharp allocation-dependent lower bound. Finally, we numerically validate the superiority of our approach over naïve uniform allocation on synthetic and HelpSteer2 datasets.

</details>


### [121] [Precise: SDE-Consistent Stochastic Sampling for RL Post-Training of Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23522)
*Jade Zou,Tao Huang,Weijie Kong,Junzhe Li,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jianwei Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Zhao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Precise，一种用于流匹配生成器强化学习对齐的新型随机采样器，通过平衡探索与稳定性并保持去噪轨迹的SDE一致性，显著提升了训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将在线强化学习应用于流匹配生成器时，需要将确定性采样轨迹转换为随机策略。现有采样器在离散化SDE时存在探索与稳定性的矛盾，且可能偏离流匹配过程，导致过度离散化噪声或无法保证收敛到数据分布，影响奖励优化性能。

Method: 提出Precise随机采样器：1) 分析去噪过程中探索与稳定性的内在矛盾，推导出平衡两者的SDE调度；2) 通过冻结干净潜变量后验均值的新型近似方法，保持去噪轨迹的SDE一致性，解决标准采样器中的过量噪声问题。

Result: 实验表明该方法在强化学习奖励优化方面实现了更快更稳定的训练：达到最先进的对齐分数（PickScore、HPSv2.1），同时比先前采样器减少13.1-53.2%的实际训练时间即可匹配最佳域内性能。

Conclusion: Precise采样器通过精心的SDE设计和一致性保持，成功解决了流匹配强化学习中的探索-稳定性权衡问题，在保证收敛性的同时显著提升了训练效率和对齐质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective way to improve prompt alignment and perceptual quality in diffusion and flow-matching generators. A critical step for applying online RL to flow matching is turning the deterministic sampling trajectory into a stochastic policy, typically by replacing the reverse-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) with a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). The stochastic sampler, controlling the exploration behavior and denoising dynamics, is thus part of the policy, and its design can significantly affect the reward optimization performance. We break down the sampler design into two interdependent components: choosing the right amount of stochastic exploration, and discretizing the resulting SDE faithfully at the small step counts used in RL. To address the first component, we analyze the inherent tension between exploration and stability in denoising and derive an SDE schedule that balances the two. Turning to the discretization challenge, we use a toy example to show that existing samplers can deviate from the flow-matching process, either by introducing excessive discretization noise or by relying on heuristic rules that do not guarantee convergence to the data distribution. To address these issues, we propose Precise, a new stochastic sampler that balances effective exploration with stability. Crucially, Precise keeps the denoising trajectory SDE-consistent through a novel approximation that freezes the clean-latent posterior mean, resolving the excess noise issue in standard samplers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this formulation leads to significantly faster and more stable reward optimization via reinforcement learning, achieving state-of-the-art alignment scores (e.g., PickScore, HPSv2.1) while requiring 13.1-53.2% less wall-clock training time to match the best in-domain performance of prior samplers.

</details>


### [122] [Goal-Conditioned Agents that Learn Everything All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23551)
*Michael Matthews,Matthew Jackson,Michael Beukman,Thomas Foster,Alistair Letcher,Scott Fujimoto,Cédric Colas,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEO方法，通过单次网络前向传播并行输出所有目标的价值和动作，实现高效的all-goals学习，在目标条件Craftax上显著优于其他方法，相比朴素重标记方法实现>250倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件强化学习智能体在轨迹中观察到大量信息，但仅对命令目标进行在线策略更新时会丢弃大部分信息。虽然all-goals学习（每个转换用于所有目标的离策略学习）能提取最大信息，但通过朴素重标记在计算上不可行。

Method: 提出Learning Everything all at Once (LEO)方法，通过联合输出每个目标的价值和动作，允许单次网络传递实现高效并行的all-goals更新，避免昂贵的朴素重标记过程。进一步将LEO作为教师网络而非直接行动者使用以增强效果。

Result: 在目标条件Craftax环境中显著优于其他方法，在连续控制环境中与现有基线方法具有竞争力，相比all-goals重标记实现超过250倍加速。开源了代码。

Conclusion: LEO通过解锁大规模all-goals学习，为复杂环境中的强化学习实践者提供了有用工具，显著提升了目标条件强化学习的效率和性能。

Abstract: A goal-conditioned reinforcement learning agent exploring an environment will see a wealth of information throughout a trajectory, most of which is discarded when only performing on-policy updates with respect to the commanded goal. All-goals learning, where each transition is used for learning off-policy with respect to every goal, allows agents to extract maximal information, however it is usually computationally infeasible when done via naive relabelling. This can be overcome by jointly outputting values and actions for every goal at once, allowing for efficient, parallel all-goals updates with a single pass through the network, in a process we call Learning Everything all at Once (LEO). We show that this approach significantly outperforms other methods on goal-conditioned Craftax and is competitive with existing baselines on continuous control environments, while achieving a >250x speed-up compared to all-goals relabelling. We then go on to show that this approach can be made even more powerful by using LEO as a teacher network, rather than a direct actor. We hope that, by unlocking all-goals learning at scale, LEO can serve as a useful tool for RL practitioners in complex environments. We open source our code.

</details>


### [123] [Coupling-Robust Accuracy in Multiphysics Physics Informed Neural Networks via Kronecker-Preconditioned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23391)
*Youngjae Park,Jaemin Kim,Junghwa Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINNs在强耦合多物理场系统中精度下降的问题，提出基于NTK分析的理论解释，并开发SOAP+GN优化方法实现耦合鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在处理强耦合多物理场系统时存在系统性的精度退化问题，随着方程间耦合强度增加，准确性显著下降。这一现象缺乏理论解释，严重制约了PINNs在复杂多物理场问题中的应用。

Method: 通过神经正切核(NTK)分析提供理论解释：证明了标准NTK的谱半径随耦合强度γ呈Ω(γ²)增长，限制了稳定学习率；提出块对角高斯-牛顿(GN)预条件方法，使预条件NTK的谱半径有界于网络数S，与γ无关。结合Kronecker预条件优化器SOAP与逆梯度范数损失平衡，形成SOAP+GN方法。

Result: 理论预测在对称、非线性和非线性耦合PDE系统中得到数值验证。在234个实验中(涵盖三个1D非线性系统和2D电渗流基准)，SOAP+GN在耦合参数变化1-2个数量级时保持最终epoch的L₂退化≤1.1×，而Adam+GN退化>10²×。在2D、6-PDE电渗流系统(EDL解析条件)中，Adam+GN完全失效(L₂>0.9)，而SOAP+GN成功求解。

Conclusion: 工作建立了耦合多物理场PINNs性能退化的理论基础，提出的SOAP+GN方法实现了耦合鲁棒的准确性，首次成功将PINN应用于EDL解析条件下的复杂电渗流问题，突破了已有研究必须依赖物理简化的局限。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for coupled multiphysics systems suffer systematic accuracy degradation as inter-equation coupling strengthens. We provide a theoretical explanation for this phenomenon through neural tangent kernel (NTK) analysis: for linearly coupled systems, we prove that the standard NTK's spectral radius grows as $Ω(γ^2)$ with coupling strength $γ$, shrinking the stable learning rate, while block-diagonal Gauss--Newton (GN) preconditioning yields a preconditioned NTK $K_P = J H^{+} J^\top$ (where $H$ is the block-diagonal GN Hessian) whose spectral radius is bounded by $S$ ($S$ = number of networks), independent of $γ$. We verify the $Ω(γ^2)$ growth numerically across symmetric, asymmetric, and nonlinear coupled PDE systems, and confirm $λ_{\max}(K_P) = S$ with equality in all cases. Combining the Kronecker-preconditioned optimizer SOAP with inverse-gradient-norm loss balancing (SOAP+GN) yields coupling-robust accuracy: across 234 experiments spanning three 1D systems of increasing nonlinearity and a 2D electroosmotic flow benchmark, SOAP+GN maintains final-epoch $L_2$ degradation $\leq 1.1\times$ (ratio of strong- to weak-coupling error) even as coupling parameters vary over one to two orders of magnitude, compared with $> 10^2\times$ for Adam+GN. SOAP+GN further scales to a 2D, 6-PDE electroosmotic flow system at EDL-resolved conditions -- a regime that all prior PINN electrokinetics studies have avoided through simplified physics -- where Adam+GN fails entirely ($L_2 > 0.9$).

</details>


### [124] [Understanding Goal Generalisation in Sequential Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23565)
*Jason Ross Brown,Edward James Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在策略梯度方法，能够预测强化学习智能体在分布外环境中的行为，发现显著特征驱动泛化且早期目标具有持久影响，为理解目标泛化提供了可解释的计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体在训练分布之外常表现出意外的目标导向行为，但当前缺乏对智能体如何基于训练历史泛化到新环境的系统性理解。研究者希望填补这一空白，特别是针对在多个任务上顺序训练的智能体。

Method: 研究超过100个顺序训练流水线，在250多个分布外环境中评估行为。引入潜在策略梯度方法：通过模拟低维潜在变量在训练过程中的演化来预测分布外行为，该演化依据简单模型下能获得高奖励的行为映射进行优化。

Result: 发现显著特征驱动泛化，训练早期学习的目标能够持续存在并影响后续获得的目标。所提出的方法实现强预测准确性，能泛化到未见过的训练流水线类型，且具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 虽然分布外强化学习智能体行为依赖于整个训练流水线，但这种依赖具有可捕获的潜在结构，为从发展角度理解目标泛化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents often exhibit unintended goal-directed behaviour outside their training distribution, but we currently lack a principled understanding of how such agents will generalise to novel environments based on their training history. We address this gap for agents trained sequentially on one or more tasks. We study over 100 sequential training pipelines, evaluating behaviour across over 250 out-of-distribution environments. We find that salient features drive generalisation, and that goals learnt early in training can persist and influence those acquired later. To explain these phenomena, we introduce latent policy gradients, a method that predicts what out-of-distribution behaviour a training pipeline will likely induce. Our method simulates the evolution of low-dimensional latent variables during training according to what would achieve high reward on the training objective with respect to a simple model of how the latent variables map to behaviour. It achieves strong predictive accuracy, generalises to unseen types of training pipeline, and is interpretable. Our findings demonstrate that while out-of-distribution RL agent behaviour is dependent on the whole training pipeline, this dependence has an underlying structure we can capture, laying groundwork for understanding goal generalisation from a developmental perspective.

</details>


### [125] [Cost-Effective Model Evaluation with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23595)
*Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MetaEvaluator，一个成本效益高、模型无关的框架，能够无需标签快速评估新发布模型在未见数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型快速增长，验证新模型在未标记数据上的可靠性变得困难；传统评估流程依赖昂贵的标注、重复微调或窄假设，难以跨模型族迁移。

Method: 提出MetaEvaluator框架，利用元学习在参考模型池上获得可迁移的初始化，实现对新模型的准确评估，在池中分摊成本并消除每模型重新训练的需求。

Result: 广泛实验表明，MetaEvaluator能产生稳定准确的性能估计，与传统方法相比显著降低成本，使在未标记数据上对新兴模型进行可扩展基准测试成为实用。

Conclusion: MetaEvaluator是首个能够在完全未标记数据集上评估新模型的模型无关框架，为新兴模型的可扩展基准测试提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of machine learning has produced an ever-expanding ecosystem of models, making it increasingly challenging to verify the reliability of newly released models on unseen, unlabeled data. Conventional evaluation pipelines depend on expensive annotation, repeated fine-tuning, or narrow assumptions that fail to transfer across model families. We present MetaEvaluator, a cost-effective, model-agnostic framework for rapid, label-free assessment of unseen models spanning diverse architectures and modalities. MetaEvaluator leverages meta-learning over a pool of reference models to obtain a transferable initialization, enabling accurate evaluation of new models while amortizing cost across the pool and removing the need for per-model retraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first model-agnostic framework capable of evaluating new models on entirely unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

</details>


### [126] [Convex Compositional Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23395)
*Meir Roketlishvili,Semyon Semenov,Maksim Bobrin,Viktor Kovalchuk,Albert Baichorov,Abduragim Shtanchaev,Fakhri Karray,Dmitry V. Dylov,Martin Takáč,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出凸组合能量最小化框架（CCEM），通过使用输入凸神经网络参数化因子能量函数并优化紧凸松弛可行集，解决了组合推理中能量景观非凸几何的关键瓶颈，实现了从小规模子问题到大规模实例的有效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合能量模型虽然可以通过重用学习的因子能量在更大的组合推理问题中泛化，但作者发现组合推理的关键瓶颈不在于组合本身，而在于学习到的能量景观的非凸几何特性，这阻碍了有效的优化和泛化。

Method: 提出凸组合能量最小化框架（CCEM）：1）使用输入凸神经网络参数化每个因子能量函数，由于凸性在求和运算下保持不变，全局松弛目标保持凸性；2）对可行集进行紧凸松弛，实现确定性投影一阶优化；3）两阶段训练：因子级对比学习塑造局部能量盆地，然后通过展开投影求解器进行端到端优化。

Result: 在小规模子问题或单一问题规模上训练的模型能够迁移到更大的实例，且无需重新训练，展示了出色的泛化能力和可扩展性。

Conclusion: CCEM框架成功解决了组合能量模型中非凸能量景观的优化难题，通过凸松弛和凸神经网络参数化实现了高效的确定性优化，为组合推理问题提供了一种可扩展且具有良好泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Compositional energy-based models can generalize to larger combinatorial reasoning problems by reusing a learned factor energy across many local constraints. In our paper, we show that a key bottleneck in compositional reasoning is not composition itself, but the non-convex geometry of the learned energy landscape. To solve this problem, we introduce Convex Compositional Energy Minimization (CCEM), a framework that parameterizes each factor with an input-convex neural network and optimizes the composed energy over a tight convex relaxation of the feasible set. Because convexity is preserved under summation, the global relaxed objective remains convex, enabling deterministic projected first-order optimization. CCEM is trained in two stages: factor-level contrastive learning to shape local energy basins, followed by end-to-end refinement through an unrolled projected solver. Our experiments show that our models trained on small subproblems or a single problem size transfer to larger instances without retraining.

</details>


### [127] [Preisach Attention: A Hysteretic Model of Sequential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23603)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Preisach注意力层(PAL)，一种基于Preisach磁滞算子的序列建模架构，具有O(1)深度图灵完备性，在历史范围统计任务上比Transformer更高效，总推理成本为O(n log n)而非标准注意力的O(n²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的注意力机制在处理长序列记忆时存在O(n²)的计算成本，且需要O(log n)深度才能实现图灵完备。本文旨在借鉴数学物理中的Preisach磁滞算子理论，设计一种更高效的序列建模架构，特别适用于长程情景记忆和弱位置依赖的任务。

Method: PAL用二元继电器算子替代了softmax注意力机制，该算子由可学习的激活和去活阈值参数化，并维护一个局部极值栈作为内部状态。通过模拟双栈下推自动机实现理论分析，并证明了极值栈构成了所有速率无关泛函的最小充分统计量。

Result: 1）单层PAL-Transformer在任意精度算术下具有O(1)深度的图灵完备性，而标准硬注意力Transformer需要O(log n)深度；2）PAL和Transformer计算的函数类不可比：PAL在O(1)层内计算历史范围统计，而Transformer需要O(log n)层，但Transformer支持的随机访问检索PAL无法在没有辅助状态的情况下执行；3）PAL的总推理成本为O(n log n)，相比标准注意力的O(n²)更高效。

Conclusion: PAL通过速率无关性——仅响应局部极值序列而非绝对令牌位置或时间间隔——实现了独特的计算特性。极值栈对应经典磁滞理论中的擦除性质，是输入历史的最小充分统计量。PAL为长情景记忆和弱位置依赖任务提供了一种高效的架构选择。

Abstract: We introduce the Preisach Attention Layer (PAL), a novel sequence modelling architecture grounded in the classical Preisach hysteresis operator from mathematical physics. PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterised by learned activation and deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is Turing-complete under arbitrary precision arithmetic, achievable through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton -- in contrast to the O(log n) depth required by standard hard-attention transformers. Second, we prove that the function classes computable by PAL and by the transformer are incomparable: PAL computes historical range statistics in O(1) layers that require O(log n) layers for transformers, while transformers support random-access retrieval that PAL cannot perform without auxiliary state. The separating property is rate-independence -- PAL responds only to the sequence of local extrema, not to absolute token positions or temporal spacing. Third, we show that the extremum stack constitutes a minimal sufficient statistic of the input history for all rate-independent functionals, providing a formal analogue of the wiping property in classical hysteresis theory. PAL is thus an efficient architecture for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence, with O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention.

</details>


### [128] [DiLaDiff: Distilled Latent-Augmented Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23605)
*Jean-Marie Lemercier,Tomas Geffner,Karsten Kreis,Morteza Mardani,Arash Vahdat,Ante Jukić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DiLaDiff模型，通过引入连续潜在空间和一致性蒸馏，解决了扩散语言模型中解码token相关性缺失的问题，在提升采样质量的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型本质上无法捕捉解码token之间的相关性，导致采样质量与吞吐量之间存在严酷的权衡问题。

Method: 提出DiLaDiff，一种掩码扩散语言模型变体，包含三个核心组件：（1）通过从现有掩码扩散语言模型微调的自编码器学习具有语义能力的连续潜在空间；（2）学习编码器分布先验的潜在扩散模型；（3）将学习到的先验蒸馏为少步潜在生成模型的一致性模型。

Result: 即使没有蒸馏，潜在引导的扩散模型也优于掩码扩散基线，同时显著加速推理；一致性蒸馏进一步降低了连续扩散的计算开销，使得潜在变量生成时间相比离散解码可忽略不计。

Conclusion: DiLaDiff成功解决了扩散语言模型中质量与速度的权衡问题，通过潜在空间引导和一致性蒸馏实现了高质量、高效率的文本生成。

Abstract: Diffusion language models intrinsically fail to capture correlations between decoded tokens, which leads to a harsh trade-off between sampling quality and throughput. To solve this issue, we propose DiLaDiff, a variant of masked diffusion language models with three components: (1) a continuous latent space with semantic capabilities, learned by an auto-encoder fine-tuned from an existing masked diffusion language model; (2) a latent diffusion model learning the prior over the encoder distribution; (3) a consistency model distilling the learned prior into a few-step latent generative model. We show that, even without distillation, our latent-guided diffusion model outperforms the masked diffusion baseline while significantly accelerating inference. Consistency distillation further lowers the computational overhead of continuous diffusion, such that the latent is generated in negligible time compared to discrete decoding.

</details>


### [129] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典校正扩散模型用于气象场统计降尺度，在瓶颈层引入变分量子电路，在2020年验证集上多个配置中改善了MAE和CRPS，但在2021年分布外测试中泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计降尺度是气象建模领域的关键组件，需要从粗分辨率输入重建高分辨率输出，而动态细化的计算成本很高。研究旨在探索量子电路是否能作为紧凑的非线性特征映射，用于潜在通道混合，从而提升降尺度性能。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典校正扩散模型，将变分量子电路层插入扩散UNet最压缩的瓶颈层，同时保持回归分支完全经典。设计了通道内和跨通道的量子电路方案，并在10米风场分量上进行评估。

Result: 在2020年验证集上，混合模型保持稳定，保留风场的大尺度空间组织，多个配置中MAE和CRPS优于经典校正扩散模型。结构诊断显示混合变体保留动能谱和风速分布，同时产生受控的尾部行为变化。但在2021年分布外测试中，这些域内增益在时间偏移下未能均匀迁移。

Conclusion: 瓶颈层量子混合化可以对气象统计降尺度做出非平凡贡献，但电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素。研究揭示了泛化差距，未来需要通过稳定化和正则化来缓解，量子计算在气象应用中具有潜力但面临实际部署挑战。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure.
