Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Through Noise: Why Subliminal Learning Works and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23645)
*Vincent C. Brockers,Roman D. Ventzke,Valentin Neuhaus,Belén Hidalgo-Ogalde,Viola Priesemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了潜意识学习的真正机制：不需要初始化匹配，而是由兼容的输出头驱动。通过解耦辅助头和类别头，证明了即便架构完全不同，学生模型也能从无关噪声输入中学到教师的任务知识，并给出了理论解释和失败边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为潜意识学习（在任务无关输入上进行蒸馏时，学生模型意外学到任务知识）的原因在于教师和学生模型的初始化非常接近。本文旨在挑战这一解释，探究潜意识学习发生的根本条件。

Method: 作者在受控的MNIST环境中，将网络输出解耦为辅助头（处理任务无关噪声信号）和类别头（负责分类）。在此框架下，实验随机初始化隐藏层、移除或添加层、甚至将架构从MLP变为CNN，以验证初始化匹配是否必要，并观察兼容输出头的作用。同时，建立了理论模型来解释信号传递机制并推导失败上界。

Result: 实验表明，初始化匹配并非潜意识学习的必要条件。只要辅助头保持兼容，教师模型的信号就能传递给学生，使其表示逼近教师；若类别头也兼容，仅用噪声训练的学生甚至能达到教师级别的任务性能。理论分析成功界定了潜意识学习失效的上界。

Conclusion: 潜意识学习不再是一个偶然现象，而是一个具有可预测边界、理论支撑的机制。其核心驱动力是输出头的兼容性，而非初始化匹配，这为理解和利用该现象提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In the context of artificial neural networks, subliminal learning refers to the transfer of task-relevant knowledge or unintended biases from teacher to student models through distillation on task-unrelated input$\unicode{x2013}$output pairs. Prior explanations tie this effect to shared or closely matched teacher$\unicode{x2013}$student initialization. We show that a closely matched initialization is not necessary. Instead, subliminal learning is governed by compatible output heads. Using a controlled MNIST setting, we split outputs into an auxiliary head (for auxiliary, task-unrelated noise signals) and a class head (for classification) to demonstrate subliminal learning occurs$\unicode{x2014}$even when we randomly initialize hidden layers and remove layers, add new layers, or change the architecture (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of a recoverable teacher signal, bringing the student's representations closer to the teacher's. When the class heads remain compatible as well, students trained only on task-unrelated noise can approach, and in favorable regimes match, teacher-level task performance. Our setting enables us to develop a theory that explains the mechanism of subliminal learning and to derive upper bounds on when subliminal learning fails. Together, our results turn subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits.

</details>


### [131] [What Linear Probes Miss: Multi-View Probing for Weight-Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23410)
*Eunwoo Heo,Kyeongkook Seo,Jaejun Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MVProbe，一种多视角探测框架，通过融合一阶信号和基于Gram的交互视图来改进模型权重分析，在Model Jungle基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型仓库的爆炸式增长形成了"模型丛林"，大量模型检查点缺乏充分的文档和元数据。现有的探测方法采用单视角设计，仅捕获一阶结构，未能编码行列交互中固有的丰富高阶相关模式，而处理完整权重的计算成本又过于高昂。

Method: 提出MVProbe多视角探测框架，将一阶信号与交互感知（基于Gram）的视图进行合成。通过分析不同探测阶次的缩放定律，推导出原则性的标准化和融合策略，确保各分支的贡献均衡。该方法具有理论基础支撑。

Result: 在Model Jungle基准测试中，MVProbe在多种架构上持续超越当前最先进的ProbeX方法，包括判别式骨干网络（ResNet、SupViT、MAE、DINO）和大规模生成式LoRA适配器（Stable Diffusion LoRA）。

Conclusion: MVProbe通过多视角设计成功解决了现有探测方法的局限性，为"模型丛林"中的模型识别和分析提供了一种更有效、理论上有保障的解决方案。

Abstract: The explosive growth of open-source model repositories has created a Model Jungle, where checkpoints are frequently shared without adequate documentation or metadata. While weight-space learning offers a pathway to identify and analyze these models directly from their parameters, processing full-scale weights is computationally prohibitive. Probing-based methods have emerged as a lightweight alternative, extracting permutation-equivariant representations via learnable probe vectors. However, existing probing methods are limited by a single-view design: they capture first-order structures but fail to encode the rich, higher-order correlation patterns inherent in row-column interactions. To bridge this gap, we introduce MVProbe, a multi-perspective probing framework that synthesizes first-order signals with interaction-aware (Gram-based) views. Our approach is theoretically grounded; we analyze the scaling laws of different probing orders to derive a principled standardization and fusion strategy that ensures balanced contributions from all branches. On the Model Jungle benchmark, MVProbe consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art ProbeX across diverse architectures, including discriminative backbones (ResNet, SupViT, MAE, DINO) and large-scale generative LoRA adapters (Stable Diffusion LoRA).

</details>


### [132] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现地缘政治偏见主要源于后训练阶段而非预训练数据，模型会偏向开发者所在国家的立场，且偏见程度受提示语言影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设大语言模型中的地缘政治偏见源于预训练阶段使用的大规模互联网数据，但这一假设缺乏实证验证，需要探究偏见产生的真实来源。

Method: 选取来自七个AI实验室的七组开源权重LLM对（包括仅预训练的基础模型和经过后训练的聊天模型），在英语、法语和中文三种语言下，对28个国家对进行配对场景强制选择探测实验。

Result: 七个AI实验室中有六个在经过后训练后，偏见转向与模型开发者所在国家或地区相关的方向。阿里巴巴Qwen 2.5表现最强：基础模型对中国偏好呈中性（-0.15 log-odds），后训练聊天模型达到+2.91（p<10^-4），偏移18倍。Mistral模型仅在法语提示下表现为亲法立场（FR-EN偏移+1.91，p<10^-4）。

Conclusion: 地缘政治偏见并非简单继承自预训练数据，而是在后训练过程中被主动塑造形成。这突显了需要对影响模型如何表现国家、文化和政治视角的对齐过程加强透明度、审计和监督。

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [133] [Sample-wise Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Test-time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23411)
*Phuc Duc Nguyen,Quang Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对测试时适应（TTA）场景，提出了一种基于元学习的样本级定向攻击方法，通过优先级感知梯度对齐策略，在保持良性样本标签分布以逃避检测的同时，实现对携带特定触发器输入的高成功率定向攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类别级定向攻击在TTA场景中不实用：由于TTA基于批次操作，将部分样本强行拉向目标标签会无意中将相似的良性样本一同拉走，导致目标标签出现频率异常高，容易被检测发现。因此需要一种更隐蔽的样本级攻击方法。

Method: 提出一种基于元学习的攻击方法，采用新颖的优先级感知梯度对齐策略。该策略将梯度更新建模为椭球信任区域问题，缓解攻击成功与分布隐蔽性之间的错位，并在存在梯度错位的情况下为攻击目标的优化提供理论保证。攻击者仅对携带攻击者选择触发器的输入进行误分类，同时保持良性查询的全局标签分布。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C数据集上跨TTA协议的大量实验表明，该方法实现了高定向成功率，同时保持了与无攻击基线一致的标签分布，在无标签TTA部署场景中难以检测。此外，该攻击对现有防御方法表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的样本级定向攻击方法成功解决了TTA场景中类别级攻击的易检测问题，实现了隐蔽且高效的定向攻击，为TTA系统的安全性提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) effectively counters distribution shifts but exposes models to adversarial manipulation via the unlabeled test stream. Existing class-wise targeted attacks remain impractical for stealthy exploitation in this setting: since TTA operates on batches, forcing a subset of samples toward a target label unintentionally pulls similar benign samples along, resulting in a conspicuously high frequency of the target label that is easy to detect. To capture a more realistic threat, we introduce a sample-wise targeted attack. Unlike prior approaches, the attacker aims to misclassify only inputs carrying an attacker-chosen trigger, while preserving the global label distribution of benign queries to evade detection. To achieve this, we propose a meta-learning-based attack with a novel priority-aware gradient alignment strategy that explicitly prioritizes attack success. The strategy formulates the gradient update as an ellipsoidal trust-region problem, mitigating the misalignment between attack success and distributional stealth, while providing theoretical guarantees for effective optimization of the attack objective in the presence of gradient misalignment. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C across TTA protocols demonstrate that our method achieves high targeted success rates while maintaining a label distribution that is consistent with the no-attack baseline, making it difficult to detect in unlabeled TTA deployment scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our attack shows strong robustness against existing defenses.

</details>


### [134] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FM-CGM框架，利用预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力进行端到端视觉因果推理，实现了因果发现、干预和反事实生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果生成建模方法主要关注在生成模型训练过程中整合因果约束，但缺乏统一框架来利用预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力。需要开发能够进行反事实推理的可靠且透明的AI系统。

Method: 提出FM-CGM模块化框架，包含三个核心组件：概念提取器、概念操作器和反事实生成器。利用大型推理模型进行因果推断，文本到图像扩散模型进行生成。开发了基于交叉注意力的因果语义引导（CSG）机制，确保语义干预传播到后代概念同时保持不变区域。

Result: 经验证，该方法能够识别合理的因果结构，并适用于忠实的反事实图像生成。实现了零样本因果发现、干预和反事实生成。

Conclusion: FM-CGM框架成功地将预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力应用于视觉因果推理任务，为开发可靠透明的AI系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [135] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了香农缩放定律，将大语言模型训练建模为噪声信道的信号传输过程，解释了灾难性过训练和量化导致的非单调性能退化现象，并在实验中显著优于传统缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM缩放定律主要基于单调幂律，无法解释灾难性过训练、量化诱导退化等非单调现象，即在计算资源增加时性能反而下降的情况。

Method: 提出香农缩放定律，基于香农-哈特利定理，将LLM训练建模为噪声信道上的信息传输。将模型参数映射为信道带宽，训练词元映射为信号功率，显式建模学习信号与固有噪声之间的交互关系，揭示LLM存在香农容量极限。

Result: 在Pythia和OLMo2模型上，针对高斯噪声、量化和监督微调等扰动场景进行验证。该方法在R²分数上持续优于经典缩放定律和近期扰动感知定律，能准确捕捉先前方法遗漏的损失盆地。在≤6.9B模型和≤180B词元上拟合后，成功预测未见过的12B模型至307B词元，汇总R²达0.847，而单调基线方法完全失效。

Conclusion: 香农缩放定律为LLM性能退化现象提供了统一理论解释：当缩放模型规模或数据量时若未能维持足够的信噪比，噪声会被放大，导致从单调改进向U型性能退化的转变。这为LLM训练提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [136] [An Open-Source Training Dataset for Foundation Models for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23417)
*Aaron Klein,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Luca Thale-Bombien,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布了BBO-Pile，首个大规模开源黑盒优化轨迹数据集（超过500K轨迹、3095个黑盒函数），并基于此训练了参数规模从2M到80M的基础模型，证明大规模预训练能有效模仿黑盒优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒优化方法需要大量超参数调优，难以跨领域泛化；而基于基础模型的方法虽具潜力，但受限于缺乏公开的大规模真实世界预训练数据，此前工作多依赖非公开或纯合成数据，限制了可复现性和向真实问题的泛化。

Method: 构建并开源BBO-Pile数据集，包含超过500K条优化轨迹，覆盖3095个不同黑盒函数及多种优化器；基于该数据集，训练了一系列不同规模的基础模型（参数量2M-80M，训练tokens 200M-2B），并研究其计算 scaling 行为。

Result: 发布了迄今最大规模的公开黑盒优化数据集；训练的模型在多尺度下均展现出良好性能；实验证明大规模预训练是模仿黑盒优化方法可行且有效的途径，模型性能随计算资源增长呈现可预测的 scaling 特性。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练为黑盒优化基础模型研究开辟了新路径，BBO-Pile数据集的公开将促进该领域的可复现研究，为未来开发更强泛化能力的优化方法奠定基础。

Abstract: Most black-box optimization methods require extensive hyperparameter tuning, often limiting their ability to generalize across different optimization domains. Foundation models for black-box optimization that learn optimization principles from a large collection of optimization trajectories offer a promising alternative, with the potential to outperform manually designed methods across diverse problem classes. However, prior work has either relied on non-public datasets or on purely synthetic data, limiting reproducibility and generalization to real-world problems. As a result, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world, publicly available pre-training data. We introduce BBO-Pile, the first open-source dataset comprising over 500K optimization trajectories evaluated across 3095 different black-boxes for different optimizers, which represents by far the largest public dataset for this task. Using this dataset, we train a family of foundation models at multiple scales, ranging from 2M to 80M parameters and from 200M to 2B training tokens, and study their scaling behavior with respect to compute. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pre-training is a viable and effective approach to imitate black-box optimization methods, paving the way for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [137] [Hinge Regression Trees and HRT-Boost: Newton-Optimized Oblique Learning for Compact Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23422)
*Hongyi Li,Jun Xu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hinge Regression Tree (HRT)框架，将斜决策树的分裂优化问题重构为非线性最小二乘问题，并在此基础上提出了集成扩展HRT-Boost，在性能和模型紧凑性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习高质量的斜决策树面临重大挑战，主要原因是分裂优化具有离散和非凸的特性。传统方法难以有效处理这种优化问题，限制了斜决策树的性能和实用性。

Method: 提出Hinge Regression Tree (HRT)框架，将每个斜分裂重构为关于两个线性预测器的非线性最小二乘问题，这两个预测器的max/min包络产生类似ReLU的表示能力。节点级优化可解释为阻尼牛顿法，并证明了回溯线搜索变体的节点目标函数单调递减。基于此基学习器，提出HRT-Boost集成扩展，将节点级牛顿更新与阶段式函数梯度下降耦合。

Result: 理论上证明了HRT是通用逼近器，具有明确的O(δ²)逼近率；HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有阶段式经验风险降低保证。在合成和真实基准测试中，HRT与已建立的单树基线高度竞争，HRT-Boost与强集成基线相比表现良好，且通常产生更紧凑的模型。

Conclusion: HRT框架成功解决了斜决策树的优化难题，提供了理论保证和实际性能的双重优势。HRT-Boost进一步提升了性能，在保持竞争力的同时实现了模型紧凑性，为决策树学习提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Learning high-quality oblique decision trees remains a significant challenge due to the discrete and non-convex nature of split optimization. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT) framework, which reframes each oblique split as a nonlinear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like representation capacity. We show that the resulting node-level optimization can be interpreted as a damped Newton method, and we establish the monotonic decrease of the node objective for its backtracking line-search variant. We establish, theoretically, that HRT is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate. Building upon this base learner, we propose HRT-Boost, a mathematically synergistic ensemble extension that couples node-level Newton updates with stage-wise functional gradient descent. We show that this ensemble construction admits a stage-wise empirical risk reduction guarantee under the squared loss. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that HRT is highly competitive with established single-tree baselines, and HRT-Boost compares favorably with strong ensemble baselines and often yields substantially more compact models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost.

</details>


### [138] [Onsager-Machlup Posterior Transport for Deep Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23434)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OM-Path方法，通过将DGP推理框架化为后验传输问题，使用Doob桥参考扩散和Onsager-Machlup正则化学习确定性采样器。在大规模数据集上显著优于DBVI基线，但严格ELBO变体表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度高斯过程(DGPs)中，近似推断在诱导变量上的计算是主要瓶颈。现有方法要么通过ELBO拟合显式密度(DSVI, IPVI, DDVI, DBVI)，要么使用MCMC采样(SGHMC)。作者希望将DGP推断重新框架化为后验传输问题，学习一个确定性采样器来映射可处理的参考测度到后验相关的诱导变量。

Method: 提出OM-Path方法(formally FBVI-bridge-Path)：1) 将DGP推断构建为后验传输问题；2) 使用Song的概率流ODE应用于DBVI的Doob桥前向SDE；3) 参考漂移从桥边际系数闭式获得(无需分数匹配)；4) 路径正则化器使用Onsager-Machlup作用；5) 训练目标为回火Doob桥路径后验的负对数非归一化密度；6) 通过Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差原理识别为后验的小噪声MAP路径。同时推导了两个严格路径空间ELBO变体作为消融实验。

Result: 在7个UCI回归基准上与DBVI进行匹配种子的配对Wilcoxon检验：1) 在两个最大数据集上取得统计显著胜利(power: p=0.014, NLL 0.012; protein: p=0.002, RMSE 0.716 vs 0.764, NLL 1.086 vs 1.149)；2) 在yacht/qsar上统计持平；3) 在boston/energy/concrete上输给DBVI(小N噪声数据)；4) 严格ELBO变体在任何UCI指标上都未能超越DBVI，表明在该制度下减少路径目标方差比精确密度跟踪更重要。

Conclusion: OM-Path在大规模数据集上证明了后验传输方法的有效性，特别是在power和protein数据集上取得了显著提升。然而，对于小规模噪声数据，传统DBVI仍具优势。研究还发现，在当前制度下，降低路径目标方差比精确密度追踪更重要，这为未来DGP推断方法的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Approximate inference over inducing variables is the central computational bottleneck of Deep Gaussian Processes (DGPs). Existing methods either fit an explicit density $q_φ(\bU)$ by an ELBO (DSVI, IPVI, DDVI, DBVI) or sample by MCMC (SGHMC). We instead frame DGP inference as \emph{posterior transport}: learn a deterministic sampler that maps a tractable reference measure to posterior-relevant inducing variables, regularised by a path prior derived from the Doob-bridged reference diffusion. Our realisation, \textbf{OM-Path} (formally FBVI-bridge-Path), uses Song's probability-flow ODE applied to DBVI's Doob-bridged forward SDE; the reference drift is closed-form from the bridge marginal coefficients (no score matching) and the path regulariser is the \textbf{Onsager--Machlup action}. At the finite-$ε$ value used at training, the objective is the negative log unnormalised density of a tempered Doob-bridge path posterior, and Theorem 1 identifies it with the same posterior's small-noise MAP path via the Freidlin--Wentzell LDP. Two strict path-space ELBO variants on the same bridge backbone (FFJORD log-det; OM-regularised CNF) are derived as ablations. Under a matched-seed paired Wilcoxon test against DBVI on seven UCI regression benchmarks, OM-Path delivers statistically significant wins on the two largest datasets (\textit{power}: $p\!=\!0.014$, NLL $\mathbf{0.012}$ matching the DSVI baseline of $0.017$; \textit{protein}: $p\!=\!0.002$, RMSE $\mathbf{0.716}$ vs.\ $0.764$, NLL $\mathbf{1.086}$ vs.\ $1.149$), statistical ties on \textit{yacht} / \textit{qsar}, and concedes \textit{boston} / \textit{energy} / \textit{concrete} to DBVI on small-$N$ noisy data. The strict-ELBO variants do not clear DBVI on any UCI metric: in this regime, reducing the variance of the path objective dominates exact-density tracking.

</details>


### [139] [Weisfeiler-Leman Is Incomplete on Simple Spectrum Graphs, so Canonicalize Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23446)
*Snir Hordan,Nadav Dym,Tim Seppelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了k-WL测试无法区分所有具有简单谱的非同构图，并提出PRiSM方法实现了简单谱特征分解的完备规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有简单谱的图可以在立方时间内测试同构，但研究者发现k-WL测试（及其作为上界的GNN）无法区分所有非同构的简单谱图，存在表达性不足的问题。这引发了对简单谱图完备规范化方法的需求。

Method: 提出PRiSM（Partition, Refine, Solve, Match）方法，这是第一个可证明完备的简单谱特征分解规范化算法。该方法可与DeepSets或Transformer组合使用，实现对简单谱图的通用近似。

Result: 理论方面：PRiSM获得了先前规范化方法可证明缺失的完备性保证，解决了在简单谱图上实现完备表达性的开放问题。实验方面：在图回归、分类和表达性任务上，PRiSM表现与现有谱规范化方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: PRiSM成功填补了k-WL及GNN在简单谱图上的表达性空白，为规范化拉普拉斯位置编码的使用提供了理论依据，是一个具有完备性保证的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Graphs with a simple spectrum admit cubic-time isomorphism testing, yet we prove that for every natural number $k$, the $k$-Weisfeiler-Leman ($k$-WL) test cannot distinguish all non-isomorphic graphs with a simple spectrum. As the WL hierarchy upper-bounds the distinguishing power of widely-used Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), this incompleteness applies to all such GNNs, ruling out completeness for every $k$-WL-aligned GNN family. To close this gap, we introduce PRiSM (Partition, Refine, Solve, Match), the first provably complete canonicalization of simple-spectrum eigendecompositions. PRiSM obtains the completeness guarantee that prior canonicalizations provably lack, and resolves the open problem of achieving complete expressivity on simple-spectrum graphs. When composed with DeepSets or a Transformer, PRiSM achieves universal approximation on simple-spectrum graphs, justifying the use of canonicalized Laplacian positional encodings. Empirically, PRiSM performs comparably to or outperforms existing spectral canonicalizations on graph regression, classification, and expressivity

</details>


### [140] [Commutator-Induced Uncertainty in VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23449)
*Tahereh Dehdarirad,Michael Felsberg,Gabriel Eilertsen,Ziliang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种Lie群变分自编码器框架，通过两阶段训练显式诊断和处理潜在空间中的非交换结构，解决了传统对称感知VAE因强制交换性而抑制有意义非交换结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对称感知VAE通过代数正则化强制执行交换性，这虽然适用于交换变换群，但当非交换性是数据内在特性时会抑制有意义的结构。作者认为非交换性应当被显式诊断并在重构行为中体现。

Method: 提出两阶段Lie群VAE框架：（1）无结构约束训练，通过有限Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff偏差测量代数非交换性，通过重构顺序交换测试测量解码器顺序敏感性，诊断潜在非交换性与重构行为之间的尺度失配；（2）引入带有数据驱动校准常数的变形稳定性约束，使解码器敏感性与代数非交换性对齐。该框架结合几何与代数视角处理不确定性，同时分离离散生成因子与连续几何变换。

Result: 在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA数据集上，相比beta-VAE、CLG-VAE和CFASL等基线，该方法在合成基准测试中改善了重构质量，解码器行为与潜在非交换结构更一致。定性分析显示更清晰的顺序依赖潜在组合和更稳定的重构。在CelebA上，模型产生比CFASL更忠实和因子特定的潜在遍历。

Conclusion: 该方法成功显式诊断并处理了潜在空间中的非交换结构，通过将解码器敏感性与代数非交换性对齐，实现了更符合数据内在特性的表示学习，在重构质量和潜在空间可解释性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) often struggle to represent non-commutative structure in learned latent spaces. Symmetry-aware VAEs commonly address this issue by enforcing commutativity through algebraic regularization, which is appropriate for commutative transformation groups but can suppress meaningful non-commutative structure when it is intrinsic to the data. We argue that non-commutativity should instead be explicitly diagnosed and reflected in reconstruction behavior. We introduce a Lie Group VAE framework that combines geometric and algebraic perspectives on uncertainty while separating discrete generative factors from continuous geometric transformations. In a first phase, the model is trained without structural constraints while algebraic non-commutativity is measured through finite Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff deviations and decoder order sensitivity is measured through reconstruction order-swap tests. These diagnostics reveal a scale mismatch between latent non-commutativity and reconstruction behavior under unconstrained training. In a second phase, we introduce a deformation-stability constraint with a data-driven calibration constant that aligns decoder sensitivity with algebraic non-commutativity. We evaluate the framework on dSprites, 3DShapes, 3DCars, and CelebA against generic and symmetry-aware baselines, including beta-VAE, CLG-VAE, and CFASL. Across synthetic benchmarks, the method improves reconstruction quality and yields decoder-level behavior more consistent with latent non-commutative structure. Qualitative analyses show clearer order-dependent latent compositions and more stable reconstructions. On CelebA, the model yields more faithful reconstructions and factor-specific latent traversals than CFASL, while also exhibiting meaningful order-dependent interactions between learned latent directions.

</details>


### [141] [Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23453)
*Elvin Somón,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过修正数据泄漏和指标偏差重新评估偏头痛分类研究，提出临床驱动的类别聚合、类别依赖的混合增强策略和比例约束增长概念，显著提升了分类性能和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的偏头痛分类研究存在数据泄漏和指标偏差等方法论缺陷，导致性能估计虚高，亟需进行可重复性导向的重新评估与改进。

Method: （1）依据ICHD-3 §1.2.3对两种偏瘫亚型进行临床驱动的类别聚合；（2）提出类别依赖的混合增强策略，根据每类样本量分配生成方法；（3）引入保真度不对称概念，采用比例约束增长替代完全类别平衡。采用分层5折交叉验证，以宏平均F1为主要评价指标。

Result: 修正方法缺陷后，宏F1基线降至0.71。所提框架在八个分类器上的平均宏F1为0.862，优于单独增强方法（Gaussian Copula 0.836、CTGAN 0.815、无增强基线0.801），在比例增强下的FT-Transformer达到最佳0.914。无增强FT-Transformer基线为0.896，表明临床驱动的类别聚合贡献了主要绝对提升。

Conclusion: 问题表述（如临床驱动的类别聚合）在性能提升中起主导作用，所提框架的主要贡献在于提升不同分类器间的平均稳健性，强调了正确问题构建的重要性。

Abstract: We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric.
  Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.

</details>


### [142] [Unextractable Protocol Models: Collaborative Training and Inference without Weight Materialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23464)
*Alexander Long,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Thalaiyasingam Ajanthan,Yan Zuo,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Sameera Ramasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出不可提取协议模型(UPMs)，通过在分布式训练/推理参与者边界周期性注入时变随机可逆变换，确保模型分片在不同时间步不兼容，从而在保持模型性能的同时防止模型被完整提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化协作训练和服务大神经网络的场景中，各参与者仅处理模型子集。本文探索如何防止任何单一参与者获取完整权重集，从而保护模型知识产权，并为社区驱动的去中心化训练中嵌入程序化激励机制创造条件。

Method: 提出不可提取协议模型(UPMs)框架，利用分片模型设置，周期性地在参与者边界注入时变的、随机的、可逆的变换。这些变换保持整体网络功能不变，但使跨时间步组装的模型分片变得不连贯，从而使模型权重不可被完整提取。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-1B上的实验表明：10,000次变换后FP32困惑度变化小于0.01，Jensen-Shannon漂移小于4×10⁻⁵；每30秒应用一次变换在推理时增加3%延迟、0.1%带宽和10%GPU内存开销，训练开销仅为1.6%时间和<1%内存；直接攻击需求不切实际且易于防御，基于梯度的拼接分区微调需要≥60%从头训练所需的token数。

Conclusion: UPMs成功实现了协作训练却不可提取的目标，使得在社区驱动的去中心化训练中嵌入程序化激励机制变得切实可行，为分布式环境下的模型知识产权保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We consider a decentralized setup in which the participants collaboratively train and serve a large neural network, and where each participant only processes a subset of the model. In this setup, we explore the possibility of unmaterializable weights, where a full weight set is never available to any one participant. We introduce Unextractable Protocol Models (UPMs): a training and inference framework that leverages the sharded model setup to ensure model shards (i.e., subsets) held by participants are incompatible at different time steps. UPMs periodically inject time-varying, random, invertible transforms at participant boundaries; preserving the overall network function yet rendering cross-time assemblies incoherent. On Qwen-2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-1B, 10,000 transforms leave FP32 perplexity unchanged ($Δ$PPL $< 0.01$; Jensen-Shannon drift $< 4 \times 10^{-5}$), and we show how to control growth for lower precision datatypes. Applying a transform every 30s adds 3% latency, 0.1% bandwidth, and 10% GPU-memory overhead at inference, while training overhead falls to 1.6% time and $< 1$% memory. We consider several attacks, showing that the requirements of direct attacks are impractical and easy to defend against, and that gradient-based fine-tuning of stitched partitions consumes $\geq 60$% of the tokens required to train from scratch. By enabling models to be collaboratively trained yet not extracted, UPMs make it practical to embed programmatic incentive mechanisms in community-driven decentralized training.

</details>


### [143] [S$^3$GNN: Efficient Global Mixing and Local Message Passing for Long-Range Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23467)
*Dai Shi,Luke Thompson,Linhan Luo,Lequan Lin,Andi Han,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S³GNN模型，通过轻量级重新引入被省略的组件来缓解消息传递神经网络中的过度压缩（OSQ）问题，在多个领域实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的消息传递神经网络（MPNN）在捕获长程依赖时存在信息瓶颈，导致过度压缩（OSQ）现象。虽然谱滤波方法可以通过全局信息混合来缓解OSQ，但本文发现其相关的雅可比灵敏度下界在实践中难以实现，现有方法要么需要强理论假设，要么需要高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出S³GNN模型，通过轻量级方式重新引入被省略的组件来缓解OSQ，无需限制性假设，计算复杂度显著降低。该方法在新的动力学下保持特征变换的标准稳定性约束有效，同时实现全局信息混合。

Result: 在多个领域（长程基准测试、知识图谱问答KGQA、基于网格的流体动力学）的广泛实验中，S³GNN实现了高达一个数量级的误差降低，同时参数量减少多达50%。

Conclusion: S³GNN成功缓解了MPNN中的过度压缩问题，在无需限制性假设的情况下，以更少的参数和更低的计算复杂度实现了显著性能提升，为长程依赖学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) often suffer from an information bottleneck when capturing long-range dependencies, leading to the oversquashing (OSQ) phenomenon. Alongside spatial connectivity enrichment (e.g., rewiring), recent studies have shown that spectral filtering can yield strong long-range learning outcomes, as spectral operators enable global information mixing that alleviates OSQ. These approaches achieve this either by stabilizing the Jacobian energies in deep propagation or by guaranteeing OSQ mitigation under strong theoretical assumptions. We revisit these conclusions and show that the associated Jacobian sensitivity lower bound is generally difficult to achieve in practice. We then propose S$^3$GNN, which mitigates OSQ without such restrictive assumptions by lightweightly reintroducing omitted components with substantially lower computational complexity, while standard stability constraints on feature transformations remain effective under our new dynamics. Extensive experiments across diverse domains (e.g., long-range benchmarks, KGQA, and mesh-based fluid dynamics) demonstrate that S$^3$GNN achieves up to an order-of-magnitude error reduction with up to 50\% fewer parameters. Our code can be found in https://github.com/EEthanShi/S3-GNN.git.

</details>


### [144] [Non-normal spectral signatures of instability in neural network training dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23476)
*Souvik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度学习优化器（Adam、SGD with momentum）的线性化更新算子具有非正规性，导致传统谱半径判据失效。提出的条件数κ(V)可作为训练不稳定性的早期预警指标，比谱半径更能有效区分稳定与不稳定训练阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络训练中的不稳定性现象（损失尖峰、震荡收敛、梯度病理）在实践中普遍存在，但缺乏严格的算子理论解释。传统基于谱半径的稳定性分析无法有效预测这些瞬态放大现象。

Method: 1. 证明Adam和SGD with momentum的线性化更新算子具有非正规性（Adam由Hessian与对角自适应预条件子的交换子控制，SGD momentum源于增广状态空间结构）
2. 应用非正规稳定性理论，推导保守伪谱前驱界
3. 建立例外点与κ(V)→∞极限情形的联系
4. 在双层网络上进行数值实验验证

Result: 1. 谱半径ρ(J)无法区分稳定与不稳定训练阶段
2. 条件数κ(V)能将两者分离约一个数量级
3. κ(V)作为瞬态放大的早期预警指标，补充了经典尖锐性准则
4. 提供了非正规放大严重程度的连续度量

Conclusion: 非厄米特算子理论为神经网络优化稳定性提供了有用且未被充分探索的框架，为理解自适应优化稳定性提供了诊断语言和概念验证基准。

Abstract: Training instabilities in deep networks - loss spikes, oscillatory convergence, and gradient pathologies - are empirically prevalent but lack a rigorous operator-theoretic explanation. We show that the linearized update operators for practically used optimizers are generically non-normal: for Adam, non-normality is controlled by the commutator [H, M] between the Hessian and the diagonal adaptive preconditioner, while for SGD with momentum it arises from the augmented state-space structure of the update map. Applying non-normal stability theory to these operators, we derive a conservative pseudospectral precursor bound in which κ(V) serves as an early-warning indicator of transient amplification even when the spectral radius remains below one, and we establish that exceptional points of the update operator appear as the κ(V) -> \infty limiting case of this framework. Numerical experiments on two-layer networks confirm that the spectral radius ρ(J) provides no separation between stable and unstable training phases while κ(V) separates them by approximately one order of magnitude, complementing the classical sharpness criterion with a continuous severity measure of non-normal amplification. These results establish non-Hermitian operator theory as a useful and underexplored framework for neural network optimization stability, offering a diagnostic language and proof-of-concept benchmark for understanding adaptive optimization stability.

</details>


### [145] [Learning partially observed systems with neural Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23510)
*Sunniva Meltzer,Sølve Eidnes,Alexander Johannes Stasik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NHODE框架，结合哈密顿神经网络与神经ODE，在仅观测部分状态变量的情况下学习动力系统，通过嵌入物理结构显著提升预测精度与长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数物理信息模型假设可获取系统全状态，但实际常面临部分观测场景，某些状态变量完全未被观测且无直接监督信号，限制了这些模型的应用。

Method: 将哈密顿神经网络（HNN）与神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）结合，哈密顿结构强制能量守恒，神经ODE框架支持仅在观测变量上定义损失的灵活训练；引入对称性感知坐标变换与可分离能量形式以嵌入额外物理约束。

Result: 在线性/非线性质量-弹簧系统及混沌三体问题等复杂度递增的系统上评估，嵌入的物理结构越多，预测精度与长期稳定性越好；NHODE可同时捕捉观测与潜在动力学，纯数据驱动基线在高难度场景下失稳。

Conclusion: NHODE框架成功解决了部分观测动力系统的学习问题，在保持物理一致性的同时实现了对未观测状态的有效推断，为复杂动力系统的数据驱动建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: When learning dynamical systems from data, embedding physical structure can constrain the solution space and improve generalization, but many physics-informed models assume access to the full system state. This limits their use in partially observed settings, where some state variables are completely unobserved and must be inferred without direct supervision. Here, we present neural Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations (NHODE), a framework that combines Hamiltonian neural networks (HNNs) with neural ordinary differential equations (neural ODEs) to learn partially observed dynamical systems from data. The Hamiltonian structure enforces energy conservation by construction, while the neural ODE framework enables a flexible training procedure that allows the loss to be defined only on observed variables. We also incorporate additional physical constraints through symmetry-aware coordinate transformations and separable energy formulations. The framework is evaluated on systems of increasing complexity, from linear and nonlinear mass-spring systems to the chaotic three-body problem. Across all examples, increasing the amount of embedded physical structure improves the accuracy and long-horizon stability of the predictions. Even in the most challenging regimes, the NHODE framework captures both observed and latent dynamics, whereas purely data-driven baselines become unstable.

</details>


### [146] [When One Point Is Not Enough: Addressing Ambiguous Instances in Dimensionality Reduction by Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23540)
*Diede P. M. van der Hoorn,Alessio Arleo,Fernando V. Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对降维投影中被忽视的“模糊实例”问题提出图论方法，通过复制这些在多个互不相似邻域中均高度相似的实例，从而揭示隐藏邻域成员关系并减少部分邻域嵌入失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维（DR）被广泛用于高维数据可视化，邻域发现依赖于投影的细粒度局部结构。然而DR本质是有损过程，投影不可避免地包含视觉伪影。先前研究忽略了一类伪影来源：模糊实例——这些实例在高维空间中同时与多个互不相似的邻域高度相似。标准DR将每个实例映射为视觉空间中的单一点，因此无法忠实投影此类实例，只能将其置于某一邻域（或都不在），导致邻域结构仅部分呈现。本文将此失真称为“部分邻域嵌入”。

Method: 提出一种基于图的方法识别模糊实例，并将它们在投影中复制为多个点，每份副本置于其各自的邻域内。方法以UMAP为实验基础，同时可推广至其他基于局部图的降维技术。

Result: 所提方法在多个示例中揭示了此前隐藏的邻域成员关系，减少了部分邻域嵌入失真；定量分析进一步支持该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 模糊实例是降维投影中视觉伪影的重要来源；通过图论方法复制并合理安置这些实例，可以更完整地呈现邻域结构，提升降维可视化分析的可靠性与准确性。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) methods are widely used to visualize high-dimensional data. One key task in DR-based analysis is discovering neighborhoods, which relies on analyzing the fine-grained local structure of a projection. However, DR is an inherently lossy process; no technique can perfectly preserve the high-dimensional relationships, and projections therefore contain visual artifacts. In this paper, we highlight a typically overlooked source of visual artifacts: ambiguous instances. These are instances that are highly similar to multiple mutually dissimilar neighborhoods in the high-dimensional space. Standard DR methods cannot faithfully project such instances, since each data instance is mapped to a single point in the visual space. As a result, such an instance is placed in only one of its neighborhoods (or in none at all), so only part of its neighborhood structure is represented. We call this distortion partial neighborhood embedding. In this paper, we introduce a graph-based approach that identifies ambiguous instances and replicates them as multiple points in the projection, placing each copy within its respective neighborhood. We use UMAP for our results, but our approach also generalizes to other local graph-based DR techniques, and we show that our approach reveals previously hidden neighborhood memberships in projections and reduces partial neighborhood embedding across multiple examples, and is further supported by quantitative analyses.

</details>


### [147] [Is Dimensionality a Barrier for Retrieval Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23556)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Jonathan Kogan,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在嵌入检索模型中，维度d=O(k log(n/k))是实现最大边际的充分必要条件，并发现sigmoid损失在产生大边际嵌入方面优于InfoNCE损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于嵌入的检索模型通常使用低维表示(d≈1000)，却能扩展到数十亿甚至万亿级别的数据点。本文旨在研究为什么低维表示不会阻碍检索模型的扩展能力，通过研究最大边际嵌入来回答这一问题。

Method: 研究者使用最大边际框架分析嵌入检索模型，其中查询和文档嵌入需要满足：当查询与文档相关时内积≥m，不相关时内积≤-m。作者建立了理论分析框架，推导出实现最大边际所需维度的上下界，并针对k-稀疏行矩阵给出了完整分析。同时进行了InfoNCE和sigmoid损失的实验对比。

Result: 主要定理证明：在维度d=O(m^(-2) log n)下可以实现接近无维度限制时的最大边际m。对于k-稀疏行矩阵，维度d=O(k log(n/k))是实现最大边际Θ(k^(-1/2))的充分必要条件。当d=o(k log(n/k))时也给出了大边际的构造方案。实验表明sigmoid损失在产生大边际嵌入方面明显优于InfoNCE损失。

Conclusion: 低维表示能够支持大规模检索的理论基础在于：只需维度d=O(k log(n/k))即可实现理论最优的最大边际，这解释了为何d≈1000的维度足以支持十亿级数据的检索。研究还建议在实际应用中使用sigmoid损失而非InfoNCE损失以获得更好的表示质量。

Abstract: Why does the low dimensionality of representations, typically $d\approx 1000$, not prevent modern embedding-based retrieval models from scaling to billions, or even trillions, of data points? To answer this question, we study maximal-margin embeddings in the following retrieval model, classically studied in communication complexity [PS86] and more recently in embedding-based retrieval [WBNL26]. Let $A\in \{0,1\}^{N\times n}$ be a matrix indicating whether each of $N$ queries is relevant to each of $n$ documents. We are interested in the largest margin $m>0,$ denoted by $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(d, A),$ for which there exist unit norm embeddings of the queries and documents $\{U_j\}_{j = 1}^N, \{V_i\}_{i = 1}^n$ with the following property. $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \ge m$ whenever $A_{ji} = 1$ and $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \le -m$ otherwise. A large margin is a key proxy for representation quality: it controls both robustness to perturbations and compositional generalization across queries. Our main theorem establishes that the best possible margin without a restriction on the dimension, $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A),$ can be nearly achieved in dimension $d = O(\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A)^{-2}\log n)$ which improves a theorem of [BDES02]. Together with a matching lower bound in Theorem 1.5, we conclude that when $A\in \{0,1\}^{\binom{n}{k}\times n}$ is the matrix containing all possible $k$-sparse rows once, dimension $d = O(k\log (n/k))$ is necessary and sufficient for the maximal possible margin $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A) = Θ(k^{-1/2})$ in this setting. This fully resolves the setup of [WBNL26]. We also give several constructions for large margins when $d = o(k\log (n/k)).$ Finally, we empirically test the InfoNCE and sigmoid losses for producing large margin embeddings and demonstrate a clear advantage of the sigmoid loss.

</details>


### [148] [MARS: Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23563)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Afsaneh M. Nejad,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标准Critical Difference (CD) 图表因依赖离散排名而忽视性能差异幅度的问题，提出了Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS)方法，通过引入相对边际系数作为权重，提供了更真实的模型性能差异统计表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Critical Difference (CD) 图表依赖于离散排名来总结实验结果并选出最佳模型，这种方式丢弃了模型间性能差距的具体幅度信息，导致出现“幅度盲区”（magnitude-blindness）问题，无法准确反映模型性能差异的实际大小，因此需要一种能够感知性能差异幅度的评估方法。

Method: 提出Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS)方法，引入相对边际系数（relative margin coefficient）作为离散排名的权重。该系数基于最佳表现者和最差表现者之间的距离来缩放排名，并采用动态投影机制处理边界情况，随后计算CD值，从而将性能差异幅度纳入统计考量。

Result: MARS方法能够产生更真实的模型性能差异统计表示，解决了标准CD图表的幅度盲区问题，并在大规模和广泛的实验设置中提供更深入的方法性能洞察。

Conclusion: MARS方法成功克服了传统CD图表的局限性，通过将性能差异幅度融入排名统计，为机器学习模型的综合评估提供了更准确、更有洞察力的工具，有助于更合理地选择最优模型。

Abstract: Comprehensive evaluation of machine learning models is the key to make sure that they perform as robustly and consistently as desired. In order to summarize the experimental results and pick a winner, Critical Difference (CD) diagrams are used. Standard CD diagrams rely on discrete ranks, discarding the magnitude of performance gaps between models, raising an issue which we call magnitude-blindness. In order to address this issue, we propose Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS) that incorporates a relative margin coefficient as a weight for the discrete ranks. This coefficient scales ranks based on the distance between the best and worst performers, with a dynamic projection to handle boundary cases. Followed by the calculation of a CD value, MARS results in a more realistic statistical representation of differences of model performances and more insights on how methods actually perform in vast and extensive experimental settings.

</details>


### [149] [Push Your Agent: Measuring and Enforcing Quantitative Goal Persistence in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23574)
*Yuandao Cai,Yuzhang Zhu,Liyou Gao,Wensheng Tang,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了定量目标持久性（QGP）问题，即智能体在完成指定数量的任务时往往无法坚持到底。通过PushBench基准测试发现，状态跟踪控制器可将成功率提升至69-78%并消除重复提交，黑盒评估显示在100个任务规模时成功率显著下降，揭示了数量目标对智能体可靠性提出了新的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体评估通常只关注最终成功标志，掩盖了智能体在完成数量化目标时的系统性缺陷。作者观察到长视程语言智能体能做出合理的局部工具调用，但在需要持续工作直到收集足够数量有效条目时表现不佳，这种差距被定义为定量目标持久性问题。

Method: 提出了PushBench基准测试框架，专注于仓库-工件收集和验证器支持的工作单元，直接测量重复工作、重复提交、虚假完成和进度漂移问题。设计了两种控制器：状态跟踪检索控制器和积压跟踪工作单元控制器，与标准控制器和完成门控控制器进行对比实验。同时评估了Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6)和Codex CLI (gpt-5.4)等前沿黑盒智能体在50和100工件任务上的表现。

Result: 状态跟踪检索控制器成功率达到69-78%并完全消除重复提交；积压跟踪工作单元控制器在标准控制器无法完成任何任务的场景下达到25-50%成功率。前沿黑盒智能体在50工件任务上表现良好，但在100工件规模时成功率降至每条件3/9。实验证明定量目标对智能体提出了与局部任务能力不同的可靠性要求。

Conclusion: 定量目标持久性是评估语言智能体的重要新维度，传统的成功标志隐藏了智能体在数量化任务中的系统性失败。有效的状态跟踪机制能显著提升性能，但随着任务规模增加，即使是前沿智能体也面临严峻挑战，表明智能体需要在维持已验证进度和准确判断任务完成方面进行改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents can make many plausible local tool calls yet fail to persist until a requested count is actually complete. We study this gap as Quantitative Goal Persistence (QGP): whether an agent keeps working until an external verifier confirms enough distinct valid items. PushBench turns this into a benchmark for repository-artifact collection and verifier-backed work units, so repeated work, duplicate submissions, false completion, and progress drift are measured directly rather than hidden behind a final success flag. In matched controller comparisons, a state-tracking retrieval controller reaches 69-78% success while eliminating duplicate submissions, and a backlog-tracking work-unit controller reaches 25-50% success in settings where standard and completion-gated controllers complete no task instances. Black-box frontier-agent evaluations with Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) and Codex CLI (gpt-5.4) solve many 50-artifact tasks but drop to 3 out of 9 successes per condition at 100 artifacts. The results show that quantitative goals stress a different reliability requirement from local task competence: agents must maintain verified progress and stop only when the requested work is complete.

</details>


### [150] [How Hard is it to Rig a Benchmark? A Social Choice Analysis of Leaderboard Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23628)
*Polina Gordienko,Georg Schollmeyer,Frauke Kreuter,Christoph Jansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基准测试游戏化问题建模为计算社会选择理论中的"转移贿赂"问题，证明了在Borda计数和平均胜率下该问题是NP难的，并提出了实例级鲁棒性指标来量化操纵难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务基准测试已成为机器学习研究的核心支柱，但其日益增长的影响力催生了基准测试游戏化现象——研究人员采取策略性行为（如在训练中加入基准数据）来提高特定模型的排行榜排名，这威胁了基准测试的公平性和有效性。

Method: 将数据集视为选民、模型视为候选人，把基准特定训练问题映射为计算社会选择理论中的转移贿赂问题。理论证明了在Borda计数和平均胜率聚合规则下该问题的NP困难性，并推导出算术平均、中位数、平均胜率和成对多数四种聚合规则下的实例级鲁棒性（使目标模型登顶所需包含的最小训练数据集数量）的解析表达式。

Result: 在HELM框架下的MMLU基准和Open LLM排行榜下的BIG-Bench Hard基准上的实验表明，平均胜率最难被操纵。在BBH基准（24个任务、4507个模型）上，平均胜率的中位数鲁棒性为22个任务（92%），显著高于算术平均的13个任务（54%）、中位数和成对多数的12个任务（50%）。

Conclusion: 不同的聚合规则对基准测试操纵的鲁棒性差异显著，平均胜率聚合方法具有最强的抗操纵能力，这为设计更公平、更难以被游戏化的基准测试聚合规则提供了理论指导和实证依据。

Abstract: Multi-task benchmarks have become a central pillar of machine learning research, yet their growing influence has incentivised benchmark gaming -- strategic actions taken to improve the leaderboard rank of a specific model. Treating datasets as voters and models as candidates, we consider benchmark-specific training -- the inclusion of benchmark data in training -- as a form of election manipulation. For any ordinal benchmark, the problem of choosing datasets to train on so that a target model becomes top-ranked corresponds to shift bribery, a class of manipulation problems from computational social choice. Leveraging this identification, we show that the benchmark-specific training problem is NP-hard under Borda count and mean win rate. Complementing this worst-case perspective, we introduce the instance-level robustness, the minimum number of datasets a model developer must include in training to top a given leaderboard, and derive expressions for it under arithmetic mean, median, mean win rate and pairwise majority. We evaluate these expressions on MMLU under HELM and on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) under the Open LLM Leaderboard. Across both suites, mean win rate is hardest to manipulate: this gap is clear on BBH (24 tasks, 4507 models), where its median robustness is 22 tasks (92%), compared with 13 (54%) under arithmetic mean and 12 (50%) under median and pairwise majority.

</details>


### [151] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CopFITi，首个满足边际一致性构造的IMTS连接函数模型，在联合密度建模上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS）的概率预测问题，现有方法在建模联合分布时往往难以兼顾边际分布的质量与变量间的依赖结构，需要一种能够解耦边际与联合、且保持边际一致性的模型。

Method: 将基于正规化流的单变量边际分布建模与高斯混合Copula建模联合依赖结构相结合，实现边际与联合分布的解耦，并构造性地保证了边际一致性。

Result: 实验表明，解耦边际与联合的Copula方法比直接拟合联合分布的架构能获得更好的边际模型，CopFITi在IMTS联合密度建模任务上达到了新的最优性能。

Conclusion: CopFITi为IMTS概率预测提供了首个边际一致性构造保证的Copula模型，证明了Copula方法在复杂时序建模中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint. With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [152] [Relevant Walk Search for Explaining Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23673)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Michael Gastegger,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络层相关性传播(GNN-LRP)在寻找相关游走时存在的指数级计算复杂度问题，提出了多项式时间算法来高效地找到Top-K相关游走，大幅提升了GNN-LRP在大规模问题中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络的可解释性对于安全性、公平性和鲁棒性至关重要。现有的GNN-LRP方法通过评估游走的相关性来揭示网络中的重要信息流，提供比节点/边级别更低阶解释更优的高阶解释。然而，GNN-LRP识别相关游走需要相对于网络深度的指数级计算复杂度，这严重限制了其在实际大规模问题中的应用，因此迫切需要降低计算复杂度的解决方案。

Method: 本文提出了基于最大乘积算法(max-product algorithm)的多项式时间算法，该算法是概率图模型中寻找最大似然配置的常用工具。所提出的算法能够在神经元级别精确地、在节点级别近似地找到最相关的游走，从而将原本指数级的计算复杂度降低到多项式级别，实现了Top-K相关游走的高效查找。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的算法在流行病学、分子和自然语言处理等多个应用领域的基准测试上均展现出良好的性能。算法成功扩展到大规模问题上，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性和实用性，显著提升了GNN-LRP方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了GNN-LRP方法计算复杂度过高的瓶颈问题，提出的多项式时间算法既保证了解释质量又大幅降低了计算开销，使高阶图神经网络解释方法能够应用于大规模实际问题，为图神经网络可解释性的研究和应用提供了重要的技术支撑。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become important machine learning tools for graph analysis, and its explainability is crucial for safety, fairness, and robustness. Layer-wise relevance propagation for GNNs (GNN-LRP) evaluates the relevance of \emph{walks} to reveal important information flows in the network, and provides higher-order explanations, which have been shown to be superior to the lower-order, i.e., node-/edge-level, explanations. However, identifying relevant walks by GNN-LRP requires {\em exponential} computational complexity with respect to the network depth, which we will remedy in this paper. Specifically, we propose {\em polynomial-time} algorithms for finding top-$K$ relevant walks, which drastically reduces the computation and thus increases the applicability of GNN-LRP to large-scale problems. Our proposed algorithms are based on the \emph{max-product} algorithm -- a common tool for finding the maximum likelihood configurations in probabilistic graphical models -- and can find the most relevant walks exactly at the neuron level and approximately at the node level. Our experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithms at scale and their utility across application domains, i.e., on epidemiology, molecular, and natural language benchmarks. We provide our codes under \href{https://github.com/xiong-ping/rel_walk_gnnlrp}{github.com/xiong-ping/rel\_walk\_gnnlrp}.

</details>


### [153] [Optimization of randomized neural networks for transfer operator approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23689)
*Mohammad Tabish,Stefan Klus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种优化RaNNDy随机神经网络激活函数的算法，在保持权重和偏置固定的情况下提升传递算子逼近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RaNNDy架构虽然通过固定隐藏层权重和偏置实现了闭式解和低训练成本，但其性能受限于初始权重和偏置的选择。由于基函数由激活函数决定，激活函数的选择对网络性能至关重要，因此需要优化激活函数本身以获得更合适的字典。

Method: 提出一种算法，在保持随机神经网络中权重和偏置固定的前提下，对激活函数本身进行优化，从而为算子逼近提供更合适的基函数字典。

Result: 通过包括随机微分方程和图算子上随机游走在内的多个基准问题验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 优化激活函数的方法能够有效提升RaNNDy架构在复杂动力学系统传递算子逼近中的性能，同时保持其低训练成本的优势。

Abstract: RaNNDy is a randomized neural network architecture for the data-driven approximation of transfer operators associated with complex dynamical systems. The weights and biases of the hidden layers of the network are randomly initialized and kept fixed, only the output layer is trained. This has several advantages over fully optimized neural networks, notably a closed-form solution for the output layer and significantly lower training costs. Despite these advantages, RaNNDy is restricted to the initial selection of weights and biases that parametrize the basis functions required for the operator approximation. Since the basis functions are determined by the activation function, choosing an appropriate activation function for the hidden layers is crucial. In this work, we propose an algorithm that optimizes the activation function itself, while keeping the weights and biases in the randomized neural network fixed, providing a more suitable dictionary. We illustrate the efficacy of the approach using various benchmark problems, including stochastic differential equations and random walks on graphons.

</details>


### [154] [Graph-based Complexity Forecasts in UK En Route Airspace Using Relevant Aircraft Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23696)
*Edward Henderson,George De Ath,Nick Pepper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率预测方法，通过预测相关飞机对数量作为ATCO工作负载的代理指标，提前45分钟预测空域扇区的工作负载，优于传统流量预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效管理空中交通管制官（ATCO）的工作负载对维护运行安全至关重要。现有的行业标准模型难以准确捕捉即将到来的空中交通复杂性细微差别，因此需要更准确的预测工具来辅助班组主管进行决策。

Method: 1. 改进现有过滤算法以适用于伦敦中部扇区（LMS），通过迭代反馈优化算法处理几何和操作考量；
2. 构建LMS航路网络的图表示，标准化航路段的空间保真度；
3. 通过建模飞机在未来查询时间占用航路段的概率，考虑飞机到达时间的不确定性；
4. 结合历史相关交互分布和实时运行数据流进行预测。

Result: 1. 更新后的算法在50个标记交通场景上表现优于原始算法，F1分数从0.69提升至0.84；
2. 提出的方法与实际相关交互的相关性（Spearman's ρ=0.68）显著强于标准交通流量预测（ρ=0.55）；
3. 能够提前45分钟预测即将到来的ATCO工作负载。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动工具显示出良好的应用前景，可用于辅助班组主管制定扇区配置和ATCO排班决策，有效提升空管工作负载管理的准确性和安全性。

Abstract: Effectively managing Air Traffic Control Officer (ATCO) workload is crucial in maintaining operational safety. Group supervisors use tools that estimate upcoming traffic load to aid decision-making. However, industry-standard models can fail to capture the nuances of upcoming air traffic complexity. This study presents a probabilistic approach to forecast the complexity of an airspace sector using the number of relevant aircraft pairs, i.e., those that require monitoring or deconfliction by a controller, as a proxy measure for ATCO workload. We adapted an existing filter algorithm to make it suitable for use in London Middle Sector (LMS), a complex airspace sector with multiple flows of traffic above some of the busiest airports in Europe. Through iterative feedback with ATCOs, the algorithm was refined and extended to handle specific geometric and operational considerations. The updated algorithm outperformed the original, with an F1-score of 0.84 compared to 0.69 on a labelled set of 50 traffic scenarios. To produce forecasts of future numbers of relevant aircraft pairs in the sector, a graph representation of the LMS route network was constructed, standardising the spatial fidelity of route legs. The forecasting method accounts for uncertainty in aircraft arrival times by modelling the probability of each aircraft occupying route segments at future query times. When combined with historic distributions of relevant interactions and a live operational data stream, predictions of upcoming ATCO workload could be made up to 45 minutes in advance. The proposed method to forecast upcoming workload showed a significantly stronger correlation with actual relevant interactions (Spearman's $ρ= 0.68$) than a standard traffic volume prediction ($ρ= 0.55$). The resulting data-driven tool shows promise for use by group supervisors to inform sector configuration and ATCO rostering decisions.

</details>


### [155] [Learning Dynamic Stability Landscapes in Synchronization Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23708)
*Christian Nauck,Junyou Zhu,Michael Lindner,Frank Hellmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的上游任务——学习稳定性地貌，并首创了从图拓扑到图像预测的范式，用于深入理解同步行为的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的同步鲁棒性分析方法通常依赖标量节点稳定性指标，这些指标对拓扑结构的依赖性通过网络科学或图神经网络进行研究。然而，标量指标无法全面反映复杂的同步行为特征，缺乏对同步动态的深入洞察。

Method: 提出学习稳定性地貌（stability landscapes）这一新颖上游任务，首创图到图像预测范式：使用图神经网络编码拓扑结构，结合CNN解码器直接从图拓扑学习图像化的节点稳定性地貌。发布了两个包含10,000个图的数据集（分别为20和100节点规模），基于概念振荡器模型生成节点级地貌标签。

Result: 该方法实现了端到端学习，在分布内具有良好精度，能够跨图规模泛化，并成功应用于真实电网拓扑。证明了稳定性地貌虽然超越传统网络科学的分析能力，但可以从拓扑结构中学习得到。

Conclusion: 稳定性地貌可以通过拓扑结构学习获得，这为超越传统标量稳定性指标开辟了新途径，在生物学、神经科学和电网领域具有重要的应用潜力。

Abstract: The robustness of synchronization is typically characterized by scalar, per-node stability indices whose dependence on topology is studied via network science or graph neural networks (GNNs). We propose a novel upstream task, learning stability landscapes, which provide deeper insights into synchronization behavior and from which many such scalar indices can be derived. Crucially, we pioneer a graph-to-image prediction paradigm: learning image-like landscapes as per-node targets directly from graph topology, a formulation we are not aware of having been established elsewhere in the literature. To support this task, we release two datasets of 10,000 graphs each at 20 and 100 nodes with per-node landscape labels, based on a conceptual oscillator model, capturing power grid synchronization behavior. A GNN encodes topology and a CNN decoder renders per-node images, learned end-to-end with good in-distribution accuracy, generalizing across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies. This demonstrates that stability landscapes, while beyond the reach of conventional network science, are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

</details>


### [156] [Optimal Dimension-Free Sampling for Regularized Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23726)
*Meysam Alishahi,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了广泛Lipschitz连续分类损失函数在多种正则化项下实现(1±ε)相对误差的最优采样界限，包括逻辑回归、sigmoid损失、hinge损失和ReLU损失等。主要结果包括：‖·‖₂/k正则化的k²/ε²上下界，‖·‖₁/k正则化的k/ε²上下界。对于‖·‖₂²/k正则化，采样复杂度取决于有界导数性质。研究通过改进的高阶矩界限和经验过程分析，避免了传统VC维和敏感性框架中的重复计数问题，改进了ICML'24的k³/ε²结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的敏感性采样框架（如ICML'24提出的k³/ε²界限）在处理Lipschitz连续分类损失函数时存在过度计数问题，导致采样复杂度界限不够紧。传统VC维和敏感性分析方法无法精确刻画这些重要损失函数（如逻辑回归、hinge损失、ReLU损失）的采样复杂度特性，因此需要开发新的分析框架来获得最优采样界限。

Method: 本文采用改进的分析方法：1) 使用简单均匀采样或（平方）范数采样；2) 通过高阶矩界限（higher moment bounds）进行精细分析；3) 采用经验过程分析（empirical process analyses）来避免传统VC维和敏感性框架中出现的重复计数问题。这种方法使得作者能够精确刻画不同正则化条件下的采样复杂度，并获得紧的上下界。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) ‖·‖₂/k正则化：上界和下界均为k²/ε²（相差polylog项）；2) ‖·‖₁/k正则化：上界和下界均为k/ε²（相差polylog项）；3) ‖·‖₂²/k正则化：若满足|g'(x)|≤g(x)、g(0)>0且g单调或凸，则采样复杂度线性于k；否则为k²/ε²；4) 若g(0)=0，则不存在无维度界限，甚至排除了亚线性界限的可能性。所有上界都有匹配的下界（相差polylog项），显著改进了ICML'24的k³/ε²立方界限。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了广泛Lipschitz连续分类损失函数的最优采样界限，通过改进的分析方法避免了传统框架的缺陷，获得了匹配的上下界。研究表明不同的正则化策略和损失函数性质会显著影响采样复杂度，为实际应用中的算法选择提供了理论指导。特别是，‖·‖₁/k正则化相比‖·‖₂/k正则化在采样效率上具有k倍的改进优势，这一发现对实践具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: We prove optimal sampling bounds achieving $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-relative error for a broad class of Lipschitz continuous classification loss functions under various regularization terms. This includes important functions such as logistic and sigmoid loss, hinge loss, and ReLU loss, as prominent and popular representative examples. In particular, we prove $k^2/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_2/k$ regularization, and $k/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_1/k$ regularization. For $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$ regularization, the sampling complexity depends mainly on a bounded derivative property: if $|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$, and $g(0)>0$, and $g$ is monotonic or convex, then it admits linear in $k$ sampling complexity; otherwise the general bound is $k^2/\varepsilon^2$. However, if $g(0)=0$, our results indicate that no dimension-free bounds are possible, and even sublinear bounds are ruled out. All upper bounds are complemented by matching lower bounds up to polylogarithmic terms. Moreover, our work relies conceptually and algorithmically on simple uniform or (squared) norm sampling and hereby improves over recent cubic $k^3/\varepsilon^2$ sensitivity sampling bounds of (Alishahi and Phillips, ICML'24). This is achieved by refined arguments involving higher moment bounds and empirical process analyses to avoid overcounting that appears in the de-facto standard VC-dimension and sensitivity framework.

</details>


### [157] [Contrast to Detect: Dynamic Graph Contrastive Regularization for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23744)
*Yunhua Pei,Zixing Song,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ContrastAD是一个无监督多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过动态图对比学习将结构演化转化为学习信号，在五个真实数据集上取得了最佳平均F1分数，并在三个数据集上获得了最高AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时间序列异常检测方法存在两大局限：1）基于重构的方法倾向于像恢复正常模式一样恢复异常；2）现有图对比方法强制视图间不变性，假设平稳的关系结构，这在真实系统的结构漂移下失效。作者旨在解决动态变量间依赖、频谱噪声下的特征纠缠以及缺乏异常标签的实践难题。

Method: ContrastAD框架包含三个核心组件：1）多视角嵌入器，从时间、属性和结构视角编码输入；2）频率感知注意力混合器，在注意力计算前进行频谱Top-K滤波以防止噪声泄漏到查询-键相似度中；3）动态图对比学习器，从批次级DTW距离构建幂律启发的稀疏图快照，对比最发散的图快照对与稳定锚点作为正则化，而非强制刚性不变性。

Result: 在五个真实世界基准数据集上，ContrastAD在所有五个数据集上获得最高平均F1分数，在三个数据集上获得最高AUC（SWaT 93.60、SMD 98.66、PSM 97.79）。在SWaT和PSM上相对于最强基线有统计显著的F1和AUC优势。在MSL和SMAP上，AUC落后领先者不到0.7分但仍保持F1领先。消融和敏感性研究进一步证实对比目标作为软正则化器效果最佳。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在非平稳动态下，严格不变性是次优的，对比目标更适合作为软正则化器。ContrastAD成功将结构演化本身转化为学习信号而非抑制它，为无监督MTS异常检测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series (MTS) is hindered by dynamic inter-variable dependencies and feature entanglement under spectral noise, and in practice, is further complicated by the absence of anomaly labels. Existing reconstruction-based detectors tend to recover anomalies as faithfully as normal patterns, while prevailing graph contrastive methods enforce invariance across views and thus assume a stationary relational structure, an assumption that breaks under structural drift in real systems. We propose ContrastAD, an unsupervised framework that turns structural evolution itself into a learning signal rather than suppressing it. A Multi-Perspective Embedder encodes inputs from temporal, attribute, and structural perspectives. A Frequency-Aware Attention Mixer then performs spectral top-K filtering before attention, preventing noise from leaking into query-key similarities. The core component, a Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learner, builds power-law-inspired sparse graph snapshots from batch-level DTW distances and contrasts the most divergent pair against a stable anchor, regularizing the latent space without imposing rigid invariance. Across five real-world benchmarks, ContrastAD attains the highest mean F1 on all five datasets and the highest AUC on three (SWaT 93.60, SMD 98.66, PSM 97.79), with statistically significant F1 and AUC margins over the strongest baseline on SWaT and PSM. On MSL and SMAP, it trails the AUC leader by under 0.7 points while still leading on F1. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm that the contrastive objective works best as a soft regularizer, supporting our claim that strict invariance is suboptimal under non-stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [158] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的注意力计算技术，将I/O复杂度从二次依赖降低为近似线性依赖，并证明了其接近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力计算方法（如FlashAttention及其变体）的I/O成本与序列长度n呈二次方关系，而理论下界仅需Ω(nd)的I/O操作，存在显著差距。

Method: 受Alman和Song近期提出的近似注意力框架启发，开发了I/O高效的注意力计算算法，在大多数参数设置下实现了几乎线性的I/O复杂度。

Result: 提出的算法I/O成本对n的依赖接近线性，显著优于现有二次方法；同时在各参数设置下证明了相应的下界，表明算法接近I/O最优。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将注意力计算的I/O复杂度降低到接近理论下界，弥合了实践算法与理论极限之间的差距，为大规模语言模型的高效实现提供了新途径。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [159] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeedER框架，通过结合轻量级检索和强化学习引导的图扩展策略，解决了知识图谱检索中结构不规则和查询成本高的问题，在紧凑候选集上显著提升了召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱（KG）虽然提供了丰富的关系知识表示，但其不规则结构给检索带来了挑战：自我图扩展增长迅速，稠密嵌入方法在处理多跳组合查询时表现不佳；现有的基于智能体的图探索方法虽然表达能力强，但在大规模检索中成本过高。因此需要一种既能利用KG结构又能控制检索成本的检索框架。

Method: 提出SeedER（Seed-and-Expand Retrieval）框架，采用两阶段策略：首先通过轻量级稠密检索和基于实体的检索确定一组紧凑的核心节点（seeding），然后通过强化学习训练的图感知策略有选择地扩展这组节点（expand）。该设计将全局推理分解为可重用的局部决策，从而在高效发现查询相关节点的同时严格控制扩展成本。

Result: 从组合泛化和图约束子模优化两个理论角度证明了SeedER的优势，并揭示了稠密检索在组合图查询上的理论局限性。在实验中，SeedER在紧凑候选集的情况下，相比强稠密基线和图增强基线，显著提高了召回率，验证了其作为知识密集型推理系统第一阶段检索器的有效性。

Conclusion: SeedER通过显式利用知识图谱结构，采用迭代式的低成本扩展策略，成功平衡了检索的表达能力和计算效率，为大规模知识图谱检索提供了一个有效的解决方案，特别适合作为知识密集型推理系统的第一阶段检索器。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-driven design of physics-constrained constitutive models: two agents are better than one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23754)
*Marius Tacke,Matthias Busch,Kian Abdolazizi,Jonas Eichinger,Kevin Linka,Roland Aydin,Christian Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个多智能体大语言模型（LLM）方法用于生成材料本构模型，通过Creator智能体提出模型、Inspector智能体审查物理约束，将物理有效模型的比例从37%-91%提升至56%-100%，实现了物理有效、高精度且可靠外推的本构模型自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型开发需要多年专业经验，而现有单智能体LLM方法缺乏对基本物理定律的系统性检查，无法保证生成的模型满足物理约束，限制了其在实际工程中的应用可靠性。

Method: 提出多智能体LLM框架：(1) Creator智能体根据数据定制提出本构模型；(2) Inspector智能体针对九项物理约束对模型进行严格审查；(3) 检测到违规时返回模型进行迭代修正。采用本构人工神经网络(CANNs)实现，在脑组织、实验橡胶和合成橡胶数据集上测试，使用Claude Opus 4.7和Kimi K2.5两种LLM骨干网络。

Result: 添加Inspector后，Claude Opus的物理有效模型比例从91%提升至100%，Kimi从37%提升至56%，同时保持接近基线的精度和对未见加载路径的优异泛化能力。生成的模型物理有效、高精度且可靠外推，具备实际可用性。

Conclusion: 将模型生成与物理检查分离使LLM驱动的本构建模成为真正可信的过程。该范式具有技术无关性，可随LLM能力提升自动扩展，为自动化、物理感知的模型发现开辟了有前景的道路。

Abstract: Developing constitutive models that capture how materials deform under load traditionally requires years of specialized expertise in continuum mechanics, machine learning, and scientific programming. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been shown to lower this barrier by generating constitutive models on demand, but existing single-agent pipelines lack systematic checks that the resulting models respect fundamental physical laws. To close this gap, we introduce the first multi-agent LLM-driven approach for constitutive model generation: a Creator agent proposes a model tailored to the data, while an Inspector agent critically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement whenever a violation is detected. We demonstrate this concept with constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) and benchmark it on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber, using two different LLM backbones (Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5). Adding the Inspector raises the share of exported models that truly satisfy all physical constraints from 91% to a perfect 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, while preserving near-baseline accuracy and remarkable generalization to unseen loading paths. In combination, the generated models are physically valid, highly accurate, and extrapolate reliably beyond the training data - properties that together make them directly usable in practice. Separating generation from inspection thus turns LLM-driven constitutive modeling into a genuinely trustworthy process. The paradigm is deliberately technique-agnostic and scales automatically with advances in LLM capability, opening a promising path toward automated, physics-aware model discovery.

</details>


### [161] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去偏负标签挖掘方法，通过理论框架校正采样偏差，提升了基于预训练视觉-语言模型的分布外检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于预训练视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的分布外（OOD）检测方法依赖启发式规则从无标注语料库中挖掘负标签，但未解决假负样本问题，限制了检测性能。

Method: 提出理论框架，通过间接近似负标签分布校正采样偏差，将去偏负标签挖掘转化为基于ID标签和无标注语料数据的蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多种OOD检测设置中达到新的最优性能，有效提升了VLM-based OOD检测能力。

Conclusion: 本文解决了OOD检测中负标签挖掘的假负样本问题，提出的去偏方法显著提升了检测效果。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [162] [Entrywise Error Bounds for Spectral Ranking with Semi-Random Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23854)
*Dongmin Lee,Anuran Makur,Japneet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半随机对抗条件下Bradley-Terry-Luce模型谱算法的逐项误差，发现未加权谱方法的性能严重依赖生成图的谱性质，并提出了通过重新加权边以恢复谱隙的方法来恢复渐近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BTL模型估计是给定成对比较数据集对项目集合进行排序的成熟策略。尽管谱估计和最大似然估计等BTL估计方法在均匀采样图领域的理论性能已得到充分研究，但将此类结果推广到更广泛的随机图类别仍具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对非均匀采样情况的理论保证。

Method: 研究谱算法在半随机对抗条件下的逐项误差，其中对抗者可以任意提高某些边的采样概率。分析未加权谱方法的性能依赖性，并设计适当的边重加权策略来对抗对抗者干扰并恢复谱隙。

Result: 未加权谱方法的性能严重依赖于生成图的谱性质。通过适当重新加权观测边来对抗对抗者并恢复谱隙，可以恢复接近均匀采样图的渐近性能。数值模拟支持了理论发现。

Conclusion: 在半随机对抗条件下，传统未加权谱方法的性能会因图谱性质的变化而显著下降，但通过设计合适的边重加权机制可以有效缓解这一问题，使算法性能恢复到接近均匀采样的水平，为非均匀采样场景下的BTL估计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model estimation is a well-established strategy to rank a collection of items given a dataset of pairwise comparisons. Although the theoretical performance of BTL estimation methods, such as spectral and maximum likelihood estimation, is well studied in the regime of uniformly sampled graphs, generalizing such results to a wider class of random graphs has proved challenging. In this work, we investigate the entry-wise error of spectral algorithms against a semi-random adversary that can arbitrarily boost the sampling probabilities of certain edges. We find that the performance of the unweighted spectral method is heavily dependent on the spectral properties of the generated graph. Furthermore, we show that asymptotic performance approaching that of uniformly sampled graphs can be recovered by appropriately reweighting the observed edges to counteract the adversary and restore the spectral gap. Finally, we provide numerical simulations that support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [163] [Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23857)
*Taiming Lu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 挑战了知识蒸馏中“教师越强学生越好”的普遍假设。研究发现教师模型不必很强：经适当损失混合，弱教师也能改进强学生；且过强的教师反而可能导致蒸馏收益饱和甚至逆转。蒸馏在泛化性提升上优于域内拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏研究普遍假设教师与学生之间存在“强到弱”的关系，即更强的教师能指导出更好的学生。本文旨在检验这一假设在大语言模型预训练场景中是否成立，探究不同能力差距的教师-学生配对下蒸馏的有效性。

Method: 通过调整模型架构规模和训练词元预算，构建三种教师-学生关系配置：强到弱、同级、弱到强。在每种配置下研究知识蒸馏的效果，重点考察语言建模损失与知识蒸馏损失的适当混合策略。同时评估模型在域内拟合、分布外泛化及下游任务上的表现。

Result: 实验发现：1）教师不必强于学生，适当混合损失后，小且训练不足的教师仍能改进更大的学生；2）更强的教师不总是更好，通过增加参数或训练词元来强化教师，可能导致蒸馏收益饱和甚至逆转；3）蒸馏相比域内拟合，更易提升模型的泛化能力（分布外表现和下游任务性能）。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了“蒸馏预训练总是需要强教师”的普遍观念，为知识蒸馏在大语言模型预训练中的应用提供了新的理解和指导方向。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation generally assumes a strong-to-weak relationship where stronger teachers yield better students. In this work, we examine this assumption about distillation in large language model pretraining. By varying architecture sizes and training token budgets, we create strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student relationships, and study distillation's effectiveness under each. We find that the teacher need not be strong: with proper mixing of the language modeling and knowledge distillation losses, even small and undertrained teachers improve larger students. At the same time, a stronger teacher is not always better: pushing the teacher further, through more parameters or more training tokens, can saturate or even reverse the distillation gains. We further observe that distillation improves generalization (out-of-distribution and downstream performance) more readily than in-domain fitting. Together, these results challenge the common belief that distillation pretraining always requires a strong teacher.

</details>


### [164] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的循环Transformer方法，通过在推理时对冻结模型的中间层块进行循环调用，在不进行额外微调或架构修改的情况下，显著提升了多种大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环Transformer方法通常需要在训练时就引入循环结构并进行端到端训练，这限制了其在已预训练模型上的应用。作者希望找到一种方法，能够在推理时对现有的冻结预训练模型引入循环机制，从而提升模型性能，同时避免额外的训练成本。

Method: 作者提出了一种轻量级的推理时包装器，对冻结检查点的连续中间层块进行循环调用。作者将预归一化Transformer块视为常微分方程（ODE）上的前向欧拉步，将循环视为对同一近似的细化过程，即用多个较小的阻尼子步骤替代一个大的更新步骤，而非简单地重复应用层块。

Result: 在七个密集、稀疏MoE和MLA+MoE模型家族上的实验表明：Qwen3-4B-Instruct在MMLU-Pro上提升+2.64个百分点，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct在CommonsenseQA上提升+1.14个百分点，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct在OpenBookQA上提升+1.20个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了可以在无需额外训练的情况下，通过推理时的循环策略对预训练模型进行性能提升，为预训练模型的测试时优化提供了一种低成本且有效的方法。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [165] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Complete-muE框架，实现从稠密FFN到任意MoE配置的超参数迁移，通过双桥系统解决架构和专家token数同时变化的挑战，实现"一次调参稠密模型，迁移至所有MoE配置"的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数迁移工具存在局限：μP要求固定架构，SDE要求固定每步token数。而稠密到MoE迁移或MoE专家数量缩放会同时改变架构和每个专家的token数，导致现有方法无法直接应用于MoE场景。MoE模型扩容时需要进行昂贵的超参数搜索。

Method: 提出Complete-muE框架，采用双桥系统：Bridge I通过active-width μP和归一化路由器缩放在稠密FFN和稠密MoE之间建立映射；Bridge II通过激活专家缩放在稠密MoE和稀疏MoE之间建立映射，其中一阶SDE学习率/权重衰减修正相互抵消，仅保留有界的残差σ₀偏移。

Result: 大量语言模型和扩散模型预训练实验表明，Complete-muE在不同模型架构和参数规模下产生相对稳定的超参数最优值，仅有与Bridge II非严格SDE行为一致的微小漂移。实践中该漂移足够小，使得在单个稠密参考模型上调优的超参数能近似最优地迁移到所有MoE配置。

Conclusion: Complete-muE实现了MoE模型在扩容时无需昂贵超参数搜索即可获得比稠密模型更快的收敛加速。核心实用配方是"调参稠密一次，迁移至所有MoE"，显著降低了MoE模型的超参数调优成本。

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale.
