Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [How to Steer Your Multi-Agent System: Human-LLM Collaborative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23023)
*Zeyu He,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMBIPOM系统，通过过程级监督和语义/结构交互来解决多智能体系统中人类难以管理复杂计划的问题，揭示了混合工作流和权衡关系，为人机协同规划提供了设计见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协调多智能体系统中，由于计划复杂性和透明度有限，人类难以有效管理计划。现有方法依赖结果级监督，用户只能验证最终输出而无法了解中间推理过程，缺乏过程级可见性和控制。

Method: 形式化了人机协同规划交互的设计空间，包含三个维度：模式（语义vs结构）、范围（全局vs目标）、层级（低级vs高级编辑）。实现了AMBIPOM原型系统，支持通过语义和结构交互进行过程级监督，并进行了用户研究和控制基准测试。

Result: 用户研究揭示了混合工作流以及努力-控制-风险权衡；控制基准分析展示了LLM在不同范围和修订策略下如何修订计划；获得了更透明、可控和有效的人机协同规划的设计见解。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的设计空间和实证研究，为人机协同规划提供了重要的设计见解，有助于构建更透明、可控和有效的人机协同规划系统。发布了代码和数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: In orchestrated multi-agent systems, humans often struggle to manage plans due to their complexity and limited transparency. Existing approaches rely on outcome-level supervision, where users verify only final outputs without visibility into intermediate reasoning. We formalize a design space for human-LLM co-planning interactions along three axes: mode (semantic vs. structural), scope (global vs. targeted), and level (low vs. high-level edits). We realize it in AMBIPOM, a prototype supporting process-level supervision through both semantic and structural interactions. Through a user study, we characterize how users navigate this space, revealing hybrid workflows and effort-control-risk trade-offs; through a controlled benchmark, we analyze how LLMs revise plans under varying scope and revision strategies. Our findings yield design insights for more transparent, controllable, and effective human-AI co-planning. We release code and data at https://github.com/megagonlabs/ambipom.

</details>


### [167] [SVR-MAD: A Bayesian-Inspired Framework for Posterior-Guided Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23099)
*Weifan Jiang,Rana Shahout,Minghao Li,Zhenting Qi,Yilun Du,Michael Mitzenmacher,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出SVR-MAD框架，通过贝叶斯启发的后验证据动态构建通信图，在保持或提升准确率的同时减少最多61%的token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论(MAD)方法存在上下文快速增长的可扩展性问题，且现有基于先验信号(如token似然或置信度)的剪枝方法在幻觉情况下不可靠，导致准确性下降。

Method: 提出SVR-MAD框架，采用贝叶斯启发方法，将辩论前信号视为先验、辩论结果视为后验式证据来估计智能体正确性；利用该证据增量式构建通信图，优先选择答案能经受同伴挑战的智能体进行通信。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试上，SVR-MAD相比最准确的MAD基线，减少多达61%的token成本，同时匹配或提升准确率。

Conclusion: SVR-MAD通过后验证据指导的动态通信图构建，有效解决了MAD的可扩展性问题，在成本与准确率间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) improves LLM-agent accuracy but suffers from rapid context growth, limiting scalability in larger multi-agent settings. Existing methods prune low-utility communications using prior signals, such as token-level log-likelihoods or LLM self-reported confidence. However, these signals become unreliable under hallucination, degrading the accuracy of MAD methods that rely on them. We propose SVR-MAD, a Bayesian-inspired MAD framework that treats pre-debate signals as priors and debate outcomes as posterior-style evidence for estimating agent correctness. SVR-MAD uses this evidence to incrementally construct the communication graph, prioritizing agents whose answers survive peer challenges. Experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmarks show that SVR-MAD reduces token cost by up to 61% while matching or improving accuracy relative to the most accurate competing MAD baseline.

</details>


### [168] [Self-Refining Topology Optimization via an LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23273)
*Hyunjee Park,Hayoung Chung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了TopOptAgents多智能体系统，通过迭代自精炼过程自动化拓扑优化的设计流程和决策制定，特别适用于预训练语言模型接触较少的问题类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化工作流中，工程师需要基于领域专业知识做出一系列决策（如参数设置、物理可行性评估等），这些决策会干扰自主设计过程，难以实现完全自动化。

Method: 构建了由六个基于大语言模型的智能体组成的TopOptAgents系统，这些智能体通过迭代自精炼循环协作完成问题构建、验证、代码生成与执行以及优化结构质量评估等关键阶段，实现错误纠正和逐步改进。

Result: 在涵盖不同文献覆盖度和数值特征的优化问题上验证了框架有效性，对于预训练语言模型接触较少的问题类型（如文献和开源实现稀缺的公式），该框架能可靠地生成收敛设计，而单一先进LLM则表现不佳。

Conclusion: 迭代自精炼机制显著拓展了基于LLM的自动化能够可靠处理的拓扑优化问题范围，对于文献和开源资源稀缺的问题类别优势尤为明显。

Abstract: Topology optimization is a widely used design method that produces optimized material distributions for prescribed objectives and constraints through well-established numerical algorithms. Throughout the workflow, engineers make a series of decisions ranging from setting and adjusting numerical parameters to assessing whether the converged design meets considerations beyond those explicitly included in the optimization problem, such as physical feasibility. These decisions, which draw on domain expertise, interfere with the autonomous design process. To address this difficulty, this study presents TopOptAgents, a multi-agent system for automating not only the design process but also decision-making during the key stages of the topology optimization process. TopOptAgents consists of six LLM-based agents collaborating through iterative self-refinement cycles spanning problem formulation, validation, code generation and execution, and quality assessment of the optimized structure. This process enables error correction and progressive improvement of both the optimization setup and resulting design. The framework is demonstrated on optimization problems selected to cover a range of settings that differ in their literature coverage and numerical characteristics The benefits of iterative self-refinement are found to be particularly pronounced for problem classes where the pretrained language model has limited prior exposure, such as formulations whose literature and open-source implementations are comparatively sparse. In such cases, the proposed framework reliably produces converged designs where a single state-of-the-art LLM struggles, suggesting that self-refinement broadens the range of topology optimization problems that LLM-based automation can reliably address.

</details>


### [169] [ARMS: Automatic Reward Shaping for Sparse-Reward Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23562)
*Elie Abboud,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARMS框架，这是首个基于博弈论均衡保持结果的MARL自动奖励塑造方法，通过轨迹排名学习稠密奖励信号，在保持纳什均衡的同时提高了采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）中稀疏奖励是一个主要瓶颈，同时学习导致非平稳性使奖励设计尤为困难。现有的奖励塑形方法虽然可以加速学习，但在多智能体环境中必须保持问题的战略结构，而不仅仅是改善短期优化。

Method: 提出ARMS（Automatic Reward-shaping in Multi-agent Systems）框架：1）通过轨迹排名从稀疏环境奖励中学习稠密塑造信号的自监督框架；2）通过条件最佳响应推理重新表述策略不变性，证明在特定条件下使用塑造奖励能保持每个智能体在固定对手策略下的最佳响应集，从而保持纳什均衡集；3）在策略学习和奖励学习之间交替进行，同时在智能体间共享塑造参数以提高效率。

Result: 在部分可观察的多智能体路径规划领域实验表明：1）ARMS在奖励稀疏性增加和智能体数量增加时提高了采样效率；2）能够泛化到未见过的环境；3）发现了MARL特有的失效模式：有限探索和耦合的策略-奖励动态引起振荡行为；4）增加探索可以缓解这种效应并稳定学习。

Conclusion: ARMS是首个基于博弈论均衡保持理论驱动的MARL自动奖励塑造框架，成功解决了多智能体环境中奖励塑造的理论保证问题，并揭示了多智能体学习中特有的动态行为模式，为MARL中的奖励设计提供了新的理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are a major bottleneck in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), where simultaneous learning induces non-stationarity and makes reward design especially delicate. Reward shaping can accelerate learning, but in the multi-agent setting it must preserve the strategic structure of the problem rather than merely improve short-term optimization. We propose Automatic Reward-shaping in Multi-agent Systems (ARMS), a self-supervised reward shaping framework for MARL that learns dense shaping signals from sparse environmental rewards through trajectory ranking. Since single-agent trajectory-ranking guarantees do not directly transfer to MARL, we reformulate policy invariance through conditional best-response reasoning, and show that if certain conditions hold, then using shaping rewards preserves each agent's best-response set under fixed opponent policies, and consequently preserve the set of Nash equilibria. Guided by this perspective, ARMS alternates between policy learning and reward learning while sharing shaping parameters across agents for efficiency. Experiments in a partially observable multi-agent pathfinding domain show that ARMS improves sampling efficiency under increasing reward sparsity and agent count, generalizes to unseen environments, and reveals a MARL-specific failure mode in which limited exploration and coupled policy--reward dynamics induce oscillatory behavior. Increasing exploration mitigates this effect and stabilizes learning. To the best of our knowledge, ARMS is the first automatic reward shaping framework for MARL whose design is motivated by a game-theoretic equilibrium-preservation result.

</details>


### [170] [Safety, Liveness, and Fairness in Quantitative Argumentation Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23578)
*Arunavo Ganguly,Julian Alfredo Mendez,Timotheus Kampik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将时序推理中的安全性、活性与公平性概念引入定量（双极）论证对话，定义了强弱安全性、活性与公平性，并分析了它们之间的关系及一般性保证的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序推理中的安全性、活性与公平性概念在验证系统属性方面具有重要价值，作者希望将这些成熟的概念扩展到定量论证对话领域，以更好地分析和保证论证过程中论点强度的动态变化特性。

Method: 在权重节点论证图的重复推理过程中引入时序属性概念：强安全性和弱安全性分别保证论点强度保持在特定阈值之上和最终达到该阈值；活性要求论点强度跨越阈值波动；公平性评估安全论点在论证图序列中的分布情况。

Result: 形式化证明了安全性、活性与公平性这些概念之间的关系，揭示了它们在论证框架中的相互联系和层次结构。

Conclusion: 论文为定量论证对话建立了时序属性的理论基础，但针对这些属性提供一般性保证仍面临分析挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce notions of safety, liveness, and fairness, as commonly used in temporal reasoning, to quantitative (bipolar) argumentation dialogues where repeated inferences are drawn from argumentation graphs with weighted nodes. Between inferences, these graphs undergo updates. Strong and weak safety capture that arguments' (final) strengths remain above a specific threshold of justification and always reach the threshold eventually, respectively. Liveness requires that arguments' strengths fluctuate across the threshold of justification. Fairness notions assess how safe arguments are spread within a sequence of argumentation graphs. We formally show how these notions are related, and discuss some analytical challenges with respect to providing general guarantees for our properties.

</details>


### [171] [The Communication Complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23743)
*Élie de Panafieu,François Durand,Jérôme Lang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文确定了即时决选投票（IRV）的通信复杂度为Θ(n(log m)²)，并在单峰偏好限制下降至Θ(n log m)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conitzer和Sandholm [2005]给出了IRV通信复杂度的上界O(n (log m)²)，但下界仅为Ω(n log m)，留有显著差距。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，确定IRV的精确通信复杂度。

Method: 采用欺骗集（fooling set）技术证明下界，将IRV的通信复杂度下界从Ω(n log m)提升至Ω(n (log m)²)。同时分析单峰偏好限制、IRV-Average变体及单记可转移投票（STV）的通信复杂度。

Result: 证明了IRV的通信复杂度为Θ(n (log m)²)，解决了开放问题。发现单峰偏好限制下复杂度降至Θ(n log m)，且IRV-Average变体和STV与IRV具有相同的渐进通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文通过紧匹配上下界，完整刻画了IRV的通信复杂度特性，为投票规则的理论分析提供了重要基础，同时揭示了偏好限制和变体对通信效率的影响。

Abstract: The communication complexity of a voting rule is the worst-case number of bits that n voters must transmit to a central authority under the most efficient elicitation protocol in an election with m candidates. We study the communication complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting (IRV). Conitzer and Sandholm [2005] established an upper bound of O(n (log m)${}^2$), but did not provide a matching lower bound beyond $Ω$(n log m). We resolve this open problem by raising the lower bound to $Ω$(n (log m)${}^2$) using the fooling set technique, thereby showing that the communication complexity of IRV is $Θ$(n (log m)${}^2$). We further show that this complexity drops to $Θ$(n log m) under the single-peakedness restriction, and that both the IRV-Average variant and Single Transferable Vote (STV), the multiwinner extension of IRV, have the same asymptotic communication complexity as IRV.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [ACALSim: A Scalable Parallel Simulation Framework for High-Performance System Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22936)
*Wei-Fen Lin,Jen-Chien Chang,Yen-Po Chen,Zi-Yi Tai,Yu-Cheng Chang,Chia-Pao Chiang,Yu-Yang Lee,Yu-Jie Wan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ACALSim是一个可扩展的并行模拟框架，通过可插拔线程管理架构解决了高性能计算系统架构模拟的性能瓶颈问题。相比SST框架，ACALSim实现了超过14倍加速和41%的内存占用降低，成功模拟了SST无法在合理时间内完成的LLaMA transformer层，为GPU和AI加速器的设计空间探索提供了新的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构模拟框架存在显著局限：SST专注于多节点MPI可扩展性但难以实现节点内扩展，GPGPU-Sim基本保持单线程模式。最关键的是，现有框架均未提供用户针对特定工作负载优化线程的机制。现代GPU和AI加速器包含数百到数千个紧密耦合的组件，亟需能够提供高效并行性和可扩展单节点执行的模拟框架。

Method: ACALSim提出三大核心技术创新：(1)可插拔线程管理架构，允许开发者实现针对特定模拟模式定制的调度策略；(2)事件驱动执行配合快速前跳机制，消除空闲周期开销；(3)共享内存数据模型实现零拷贝通信，以及两阶段并行执行模型确保确定性线程扩展。通过HPCSim（针对A100级架构的GPU模拟器）验证框架性能。

Result: 在使用相同共享时序核心隔离框架开销的对比实验中，ACALSim相比SST实现超过14倍加速，内存占用降低41%。硬件验证显示与A100实测结果的周期数相关性在0.72-1.22倍范围内。ACALSim成功模拟完整LLaMA transformer层（单块）：LLaMA-7B耗时17.7分钟，LLaMA-13B耗时30.4分钟；而SST无法在实用时间限制内完成256+线程块工作负载。

Conclusion: ACALSim填补了现有模拟框架的关键空白，成功实现了SST等现有框架无法完成的设计空间探索任务。该框架为构建高性能模拟器提供了基础设施和API，为未来GPU和AI加速器的架构设计与优化研究奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Architectural simulation has become the critical bottleneck limiting design space exploration for high-performance computing systems. Modern GPUs and AI accelerators -- with hundreds to thousands of tightly-coupled components -- demand simulation frameworks that deliver efficient parallelism and scalable single-node execution. Existing frameworks fall short: SST focuses on multi-node MPI scalability but struggles with intra-node scaling, while GPGPU-Sim remains largely single-threaded. Critically, none expose a mechanism for users to optimize threading for their specific workloads. We introduce ACALSim, a scalable parallel simulation framework providing infrastructure and APIs for building high-performance simulators -- timing-model accuracy remains the responsibility of simulator developers. Its key innovation is a pluggable thread-management architecture that lets developers implement custom scheduling strategies tailored to specific simulation patterns, absent in existing frameworks. Complementing it are (1) event-driven execution with fast-forward to eliminate idle-cycle overhead, (2) a shared-memory data model enabling zero-copy communication, and (3) a two-phase parallel execution model for deterministic thread scaling. We demonstrate ACALSim through HPCSim, a GPU simulator targeting A100-class architectures. Against an SST implementation using identical shared timing cores to isolate framework overhead, ACALSim achieves over 14x speedup with 41% lower memory footprint; hardware validation confirms 0.72--1.22x cycle-count correlation with A100 measurements. While SST fails to complete 256+ thread-block workloads within practical time limits, ACALSim simulates full LLaMA transformer layers (single block) in 17.7 minutes for LLaMA-7B and 30.4 minutes for LLaMA-13B -- enabling design space exploration that SST cannot achieve.

</details>


### [173] [MASQ: Accelerating Masked Diffusion via Stage-Wise Multi-Precision Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23226)
*Seeyeon Kim,Jaehun Lee,Sungyeob Yoo,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了MASQ，一种面向掩码扩散模型的软硬件协同设计加速器，通过阶段式混合精度分配和时分感知调度，在保持生成质量的同时实现了相比A100和Orin NX最高16.06倍加速和4.93倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型能够实现区域特定的图像合成，但存在计算冗余问题：在每个时间步中，即使只有掩码区域需要生成，整个图像仍被处理，导致大量无效计算。

Method: 提出MASQ加速器，采用软硬件协同设计。软件层面实现阶段式MXINT8/4/2精度分配，动态反映空间和语义重要性，配合时分感知调度和优化的非矩阵运算；硬件层面设计块级多精度计算引擎和掩码管理单元，高效支持该方法。

Result: 在保持生成质量的前提下，相比NVIDIA A100 GPU实现最高16.06倍加速和4.18倍能效提升，相比Orin NX实现最高5.39倍加速和4.93倍能效提升。

Conclusion: MASQ通过针对掩码扩散模型特点的软硬件协同优化，有效解决了计算冗余问题，显著提升了掩码扩散任务的计算速度和能效，证明了专用加速器在生成式AI领域的优势。

Abstract: Masked diffusion enables region-specific image synthesis but suffers from computational redundancy, since the entire image is processed each timestep even though only the masked region requires generation. To address this, we introduce MASQ, a hardware-software co-designed accelerator for masked diffusion. Our approach performs stage-wise MXINT8/4/2 precision assignment that dynamically reflects spatial and semantic importance, complemented by timestep-aware scheduling and optimized non-matrix operations. MASQ features a block-wise multi-precision compute engine and mask management unit, efficiently handling our approach. It achieves up to 16.06x and 5.39x speedup and 4.18x and 4.93x energy-efficiency gain over A100 and Orin NX, respectively, while preserving quality.

</details>


### [174] [NASiC: 3D NAND-based CAM-Selected Multibit CIM Architecture for Efficient On-Device Mixture-of-Experts LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23294)
*Weikai Xu,Meng Li,Shuzhang Zhong,Tianyang Luo,Dongxue Zhao,Ling Liang,Zongwei Wang,Qianqian Huang,Yimao Cai,Ru Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向MoE模型的3D NAND存内计算架构NASiC，通过融合动态专家选择与计算，实现了4-114.8倍性能提升和3.9-70倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家(MoE)模型已成为大规模语言模型的主流范式，但其设备端部署面临巨大内存需求挑战。现有3D NAND存内计算架构虽具备高存储容量和减少数据移动的优势，但不适合具有动态稀疏专家激活特性的MoE模型，导致计算并行性下降且未能充分利用Flash单元的多比特存储能力。

Method: 提出NASiC架构，利用3D NAND的内在串结构，将基于CAM掩码机制的动态专家选择与激活专家的存内计算融合到单个计算周期中。采用电路级优化与多比特CIM单元协同设计，实现块级并行计算及原位有符号多比特输入和权重扩展。

Result: 与现有最先进设计相比，NASiC实现了4-114.8倍的性能提升和3.9-70倍的能效提升，同时保持高精度，展示了其在高效设备端MoE LLM推理中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: NASiC架构成功解决了MoE模型在3D NAND存内计算架构上的部署挑战，通过消除冗余计算、增强计算并行性并充分利用3D NAND高密度技术，为高效设备端MoE大语言模型推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have emerged as the state-of-the-art paradigm for scaling up large language models (LLMs) without proportionally increased computational cost. However, its on-device deployment faces a critical challenge due to the large memory requirement for storing all expert parameters. 3D NAND-based computing-in-memory (CIM) architectures uniquely offer high storage capacity and reduced data movement, while they are ill-suited for MoE models with dynamically sparse expert activation, leading to a degradation of effective computational parallelism, along with underutilization of multibit storage capability of Flash cells. In this work, we proposed a 3D NAND-based content addressable-selected CIM architecture, dubbed as NASiC, which is tailored to MoE models. By leveraging the intrinsic string structure of 3D NAND technology, NASiC fuses the dynamical expert selection through CAM-based masking mechanism and activated expert computation through CIM into a single computation cycle, eradicating redundant computation and enhancing computational parallelism. Moreover, circuit-level optimizations and multibit CIM cell are co-designed with proposed NASiC architecture, featuring block-wise parallel computation with in-situ signed multibit input and weight expansion, substantially improving the throughput and energy-efficiency of NAND CIM array, as well as the utilization of high-density 3D NAND technology for MoE models. With extensive experimental results, we demonstrate NASiC achieves 4-114.8x improved performance and 3.9-70x improved energy efficiency over state-of-the-art designs, along with high accuracy, showing its great potential for efficient on-device MoE LLM inference.

</details>


### [175] [DAE4HLS: Exposing Memory-Level Parallelism for High-Level Synthesis using Explicit Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23549)
*David Metz,Magnus Själander*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAE4HLS，一种用于高层次综合（HLS）的解耦访问-执行（DAE）范式。通过新的编程模型显式解耦请求与响应，解锁了编译器无法自动提供的存储级并行性。该方法在AMD Vitis HLS工具链上实现，支持动态HLS框架，在处理不规则工作负载时性能提升达10-79倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HLS工具在简单存储访问模式（如顺序访问、小数据集）上表现良好，但限制了其在复杂访问模式和大数据集应用中的加速潜力。传统HLS无法自动挖掘复杂场景下的存储级并行性，亟需一种方法解锁这些并行性以扩展HLS的应用范围。

Method: 提出DAE4HLS范式，引入新编程模型显式解耦存储请求与响应；复用AMD Vitis HLS工具链中的AXI stream和AXI burst接口实现显式解耦；将该方法应用于动态HLS框架，以更好地处理不规则工作负载。

Result: 在AMD Vitis HLS工具链上成功实现显式解耦支持；在动态HLS框架上验证了该方法对不规则工作负载的适用性；实验表明支持显式解耦后总体加速比达到10-79倍。

Conclusion: DAE4HLS范式成功解锁了传统编译器无法自动提供的存储级并行性，扩展了HLS在复杂访问模式和大数据集应用中的适用性，同时保持了HLS在设计与验证效率方面的优势，为处理不规则工作负载提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) performs well for simple memory access patterns, such as for sequential accesses that can be turned into bursts, or for memory accesses into small datasets that can be stored in scratchpads. This limits HLS to accelerating only the low-hanging fruit, where memory-level parallelism is either trivially abundant, due to simple access patterns, or latency is low, due to the small dataset. Applications with more complex access patterns on large datasets would also benefit from acceleration, and would especially benefit from the reduction in design and verification effort that HLS promises.
  In this paper, we present DAE4HLS, a decoupled access-execute (DAE) paradigm for HLS. We propose a new programming model for explicitly decoupling requests and responses, which unlocks memory-level parallelism that otherwise cannot be automatically provided by a compiler. We apply the DAE4HLS paradigm to the commercial AMD Vitis HLS toolchain and show that the existing AXI stream and AXI burst interfaces can be repurposed for explicit decoupling. We further apply the paradigm to a dynamic-HLS framework, which is better suited for handling irregular workloads as compared to statically scheduled HLS. We show that support for explicit decoupling improves the performance and achieves a total speedup of 10-79$\times$.

</details>


### [176] [To Overlay or to Customize? Revisiting Architectural Choices in Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23630)
*Xingzhen Chen,Shixin Ji,Zheng Dong,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究从部署角度系统分析了FPGA加速器中覆盖层架构与定制化架构的权衡，发现覆盖层架构适合频繁模型切换场景，但随着技术发展，最优部署策略可能发生逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将覆盖层和定制化加速视为孤立的设计点，缺乏在实际部署条件下（包括工作负载变化、架构设计、重配置延迟和切换频率）对这两种方法适用性的系统性分析，特别是在自动驾驶场景中需要明确何时选择哪种方案。

Method: 从以部署为中心的视角系统研究覆盖层与定制化架构的权衡，综合考虑实际部署条件（工作负载变化、架构设计、重配置延迟、切换频率），分析在不同条件下各方案的适用性。

Result: 覆盖层架构在当前最先进架构下更适合高频模型切换场景；随着比特流重载开销持续降低，定制化架构对效率要求高的工作负载吸引力增加；若覆盖层架构变得更强大和灵活，其相对定制化架构的优势将进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 最优部署策略会随技术发展而翻转，这些观察为未来架构设计提供了设计洞察，需要根据具体的技术发展阶段和工作负载特征选择合适的加速方案。

Abstract: In this work, we present a systematic study of this trade-off from a deployment-centric perspective, focusing on an autonomous driving scenario. Instead of treating overlay and customized acceleration as isolated design points, we analyze when each approach is preferable under practical conditions, including workload variation, architectural design, reconfiguration latency, and switching frequency. Our analysis shows that overlay-based architecture is more suitable for highly frequent model switching under the state-of-the-art architecture. However, as bitstream reload overhead continues to reduce, customized architectures may become increasingly attractive, especially for workloads with efficiency requirements. Conversely, if overlay architectures become more capable and flexible, they may further expand their advantage over customized architectures. These observations provide design insights for future architectural design, and the optimal deployment strategy will be flipped according to the technique development.

</details>


### [177] [DORA: Dataflow-Instruction Orchestration Architecture for DNN Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23833)
*Xingzhen Chen,Zhuoping Yang,Jinming Zhuang,Shixin Ji,Sarah Schultz,Zheng Dong,Weisong Shi,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了DORA，一种基于指令的覆盖层架构，通过显式描述数据流来支持多样化DNN工作负载的高效执行，在AMD Versal VCK190平台上实现了相比现有加速器最高5倍的吞吐量提升，并在不同工作负载间保持稳定的硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度神经网络的复杂性和多样性显著增加，在复杂DNN模型上实现高性能和高效能面临紧迫挑战。现代DNN工作负载在操作类型、张量形状和执行依赖性方面日益多样化，使得难以在不同模型间维持高硬件效率。此外，通用加速器在执行多样化工作负载时通常会产生大量开销。

Method: DORA采用基于指令的覆盖层架构，通过提出的ISA显式描述数据流，在层级实现数据移动、计算和同步的细粒度控制。为在支持灵活性的同时实现高性能，DORA采用新颖的片上内存管理和计算并行性管理机制。DORA提出了一个编译框架，通过两阶段设计空间探索为给定DNN工作负载生成指令，并集成了基于MILP和启发式的搜索引擎以满足不同需求和约束。系统在AMD Versal VCK190平台上完成原型实现。

Result: 实验结果显示，DORA在单一向量处理器上保持稳定效率，在操作数量变化高达6倍的工作负载中，效率变化小于5%。与最先进加速器相比，DORA持续实现更高性能，吞吐量提升最高达5倍。基于启发式的调度器在实际时间约束下达到最高90%的最优性。

Conclusion: DORA成功解决了多样化DNN工作负载的高效执行问题，通过显式数据流描述和灵活的资源管理机制，在保持高效率的同时提供了良好的可编程性和可移植性，证明了其在现有可重构系统上的部署可行性。

Abstract: As deep neural networks develop significantly more diverse and complex, achieving high performance and efficiency on complicated DNN models faces pressing challenges. Modern DNN workloads are increasingly diverse in operation types, tensor shapes, and execution dependencies, making it difficult to sustain high hardware efficiency across models. In addition, a generic accelerator often incurs substantial overhead when executing diverse workloads.
  To address these problems, we propose DORA, an instruction-based overlay architecture that explicitly describes dataflow via a proposed ISA, enabling fine-grained control of data movement, computation, and synchronization at the layer level. To support flexibility while achieving high performance, DORA adopts a novel on-chip memory management and computation parallelism management mechanism. DORA proposes a compilation framework that can generate instructions for given DNN workloads after a two-stage design space exploration. DORA framework also incorporates a MILP-based and a heuristic-based search engine to generate the schedule solution for different needs and constraints.
  We prototype DORA on the AMD Versal VCK190 platform, demonstrating its deployability on existing reconfigurable systems. Experimental results show that DORA maintains stable efficiency, with less than 5\% variation on a single vector processor across workloads exhibiting up to 6$\times$ variation in operation counts. Compared to state-of-the-art accelerators, DORA consistently achieves higher performance, delivering up to 5$\times$ throughput improvement. The heuristic-based scheduler further achieves up to 90\% optimality under practical time constraints. DORA is open-sourced at https://github.com/arc-research-lab/DORA.git.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [An AI-Driven Framework for Energy-Efficient Environmental Monitoring in Smart Cities Using Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22824)
*Yichen Liu,Imam Akintomiwa Akinlade,Xiaochong Jiang,Wenting Yang,Shiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边缘智能的AI驱动框架，通过动态激活传感器来实现智慧城市的节能环境监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市环境监测的大规模传感器部署面临能耗过高、数据冗余采集和传感器寿命受限等问题，亟需一种能够提高能源效率的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种利用TinyML边缘设备和情境感知自适应决策的分层边缘智能架构。通过一个综合考虑实时环境条件、传感器位置和剩余电池寿命等因素的效用函数，动态地激活传感器，从而减少不必要的感知和通信。

Result: 基于真实多传感器环境轨迹的城市规模仿真评估表明，与静态、周期性和基于UCB的自适应感知策略相比，该机制显著降低了能耗并延长了传感器寿命，同时保持了高监测覆盖率。

Conclusion: 边缘智能和自适应AI技术在构建可持续、高效的智慧城市监测系统方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Environmental monitoring is a crucial component of the smart city infrastructure. It enables informed decision making which enhances sustainability, public health and urban planning. However, the large-scale deployments of the smart sensors have raised concerns on excessive energy consumption and redundant data collection as well as limited sensor lifespan. To resolve these issues, we present an AI-driven framework for energy-efficient environmental monitoring in smart cities utilizing edge intelligence. Our proposed framework leverages TinyML-enabled edge devices and context-aware adaptive decision-making in order to dynamically activate the sensors based on the spatiotemporal conditions, environmental statistics and energy constraints. The sensors will be dynamically activated based on a utility function that takes in factors such as real-time environmental conditions, sensor location, and remaining battery lifespan. Our framework will reduce unnecessary sensing and communication while maintaining high coverage for monitoring. We introduce a hierarchical Edge Intelligence architecture to support deployments in city-wide scales. We conducted evaluation using a city-scale simulation driven by real multi-sensor environmental traces, which demonstrates that the proposed mechanism significantly reduces energy consumption and extends sensor lifespan when compared to static, periodic, and UCB-based adaptive sensing strategies. The results highlight the potential of edge intelligence and adaptive AI techniques for building sustainable and efficient smart city monitoring systems.

</details>


### [179] [KPI2KVI: A Multi Agent Workflow for Calculating Key Value Indicators from Service Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22825)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Yan Chen,Qize Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了KPI2KVI工具，利用大语言模型驱动的多智能体工作流，将自然语言服务描述自动转换为可计算的关键价值指标(KVI)估计，实现了从文本描述到KVI区间输出的端到端映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键价值指标(KVI)能够通过总结运营绩效如何转化为利益相关者价值、风险和结果，提供服务的决策导向视图。然而在许多领域中，KVI的计算在实践中很困难，因为它需要选择相关的KVI类别、定义可测量的关键绩效指标(KPI)、收集KPI值并应用一致的计算逻辑，这些工作通常是从非结构化服务文档中手动且不一致地完成的。

Method: 提出了KPI2KVI工具，该工具通过编排由大语言模型(LLM)驱动的确定性多智能体工作流来工作，包括五个主要步骤：(i)引导缺失的服务上下文，(ii)从分类法中提取并确定相关的KVI类别，(iii)生成带有单位和描述的服务特定KPI，(iv)通过交互式对话收集KPI值并支持对不可用KPI值进行智能估计，(v)计算区间值KVI输出(最小值、精确值、最大值)并为每个KVI代码提供可追溯的解释。

Result: 通过代表性服务描述的仿真实验表明，KPI2KVI能够一致地产生从描述到KVI区间的完整端到端映射，并提供透明的计算叙述，支持事后审计和交互式咨询查询。

Conclusion: KPI2KVI工具成功实现了从自然语言服务描述到计算KVI估计的自动化转换，解决了传统方法中手动操作和不一致性的问题，提供了可追溯的解释和透明的计算过程，为服务管理和决策提供了有效的支持工具。

Abstract: Key Value Indicators (KVIs) provide a decision oriented view of a service by summarizing how operational performance translates into stakeholder value, risk, and outcomes. However, in many domains KVIs are difficult to compute in practice because they require selecting relevant KVI categories, defining measurable Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), collecting KPI values, and applying consistent calculation logic, all of which is typically performed manually and inconsistently from unstructured service documentation. This paper presents KPI2KVI, a tool that transforms a natural language service description into computed KVI estimates by orchestrating a deterministic multi agent workflow powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) that (i) elicits missing service context, (ii) extracts and finalizes relevant KVI categories from a taxonomy, (iii) generates service specific KPIs with units and descriptions, (iv) collects KPI values through an interactive dialogue and also supports intelligent estimation for KPI values that are unavailable, and (v) computes interval valued KVI outputs (minimum, exact, maximum) with traceable explanations for each KVI code. Simulations with representative service descriptions demonstrate that KPI2KVI consistently produces a complete end to end mapping from description to KVI intervals and provides transparent calculation narratives that support post hoc auditing and interactive advisory queries.

</details>


### [180] [Intercloud: Eventual Consistency for Decentralised Economies via Chilling-Effect Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22830)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需全局共识的去中心化经济网络Intercloud，通过涟漪去重和寒蝉效应共识机制，在约35个观察者节点规模下即可实现双花防护，并构建了完整的经济激励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链系统依赖全局共识机制，存在扩展性瓶颈和隐私泄露问题。作者希望构建一个既能保护数据隐私，又能避免全局协调开销的去中心化经济网络，同时解决共识效率与安全性之间的矛盾。

Method: 采用两个核心机制：（1）涟漪去重，通过纪元时间戳标识符防止涟漪在同一纪元内重复传播同一节点，无需全局协调即可保证终止性；（2）寒蝉效应共识，观察者群通过证明无冲突证据而非投票来达成最终性，任何冲突证明自动生成自认证的腐败证明。观察者群约35个节点，由可验证随机函数分配，独立于网络总规模。

Result: 证明了四个主要结论：（1）执行涟漪通过涟漪ID机制在有限时间内终止；（2）约35个观察者节点即可防止双花，达到Hoepman下界；（3）只有攻击者妥协超过2/3的观察者群或屏蔽双方客户端与所有诚实节点的通信，才会出现冲突的最终性证明；（4）布里丹原则不适用，因为共识问题是证据缺失而非连续输入的二选一。

Conclusion: Intercloud成功构建了一个无需全局共识、保护隐私的去中心化经济网络，在保证安全性的同时大幅降低了节点需求，并通过完整的经济模型确保系统激励相容，实现了货币层与内容层的严格分离，为可扩展的隐私保护区块链系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present Intercloud, a decentralised economic network in which streams of private data are secured by Watcher swarms that observe only cryptographic hashes, never plaintext. Intercloud requires no global consensus beyond a single shared random seed per epoch. Two mechanisms provide security: (i) ripple deduplication via epoch-stamped identifiers, preventing any ripple from propagating through the same node twice per epoch, guaranteeing termination without global coordination; and (ii) chilling-effect consensus, in which a swarm reaches finality by attesting to the absence of conflicting evidence rather than voting between alternatives. Any conflicting attestation automatically yields a self-certifying Proof of Corruption.
  We prove four main results. First, execution ripples terminate in bounded time via the ripple-ID mechanism. Second, a swarm of about 35 Watchers -- assigned by a verifiable random function, independent of total network size -- suffices for double-spending prevention, matching Hoepman's lower bound. Third, two correct clients can hold conflicting finality attestations only if the adversary compromises a supermajority of the assigned swarm or eclipses both clients from all honest nodes; we prove necessity and sufficiency. Fourth, Buridan's Principle does not apply: the consensus question is absence of evidence, not a binary choice on a continuous input.
  We also develop a complete economic model. Local coins are issued and retired by currency streams; security weight tracks value automatically as Intercoin weight adjusts at each epoch shuffle. Junior nodes detect corruption and earn lottery rewards for propagating Proofs of Corruption; vesting makes corruption economically irrational. The coin and content layers are strictly separated: regulators observe weight flows without learning amounts, coin types, identities, or rules.

</details>


### [181] [Monte Cimone v3: Where RISC-V Stands in High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22831)
*Emanuele Venieri,Simone Manoni,Giacomo Madella,Federico Proverbio,Federico Ficarelli,Luca Benini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于RISC-V的HPC集群Monte Cimone v3，其使用的SG2044处理器相比前代单核性能翻倍，能效达到3.08 GFLOPs/W，性能接近主流x86-64和Arm服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高性能计算（HPC）领域提供基于RISC-V架构的测试平台，探索RISC-V在HPC场景下的性能潜力和能效表现，推动开源指令集架构在高性能计算领域的发展与应用。

Method: 构建集成SOPHGO Sophon SG2044处理器的Monte Cimone v3 RISC-V HPC集群；使用HPL（High-Performance Linpack）和STREAM基准测试结合功耗测量对系统进行表征；将结果与Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+ (Sapphire Rapids)和NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip两个参考平台进行对比分析。

Result: SG2044相比SG2042单核性能提升超过两倍，可扩展性有所改善；MCv3实现3.08 GFLOPs/W的能效，相比MCv1提升10倍，与x86-64和Arm服务器能效相当；在SIMD/向量长度归一化条件下，MCv3在16核峰值效率点达到Intel Sapphire Rapids服务器46%的性能和NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip 91%的性能。

Conclusion: RISC-V架构在高性能计算领域展现出显著的进步潜力，SG2044处理器的性能和能效表现已接近主流商业服务器处理器水平，证明了RISC-V作为HPC平台的可行性和竞争力，为未来RISC-V在超算领域的发展提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The Monte Cimone project provides a RISC-V testbed for High-Performacne Computing cluster. This paper presents Monte Cimone v3 (MCv3), the third iteration of the Monte Cimone RISC-V HPC cluster, integrating the SOPHGO Sophon SG2044 processor, an evolution of the SG2042 used in MCv2. We characterize MCv3 using HPL and STREAM benchmarks coupled with power measurements, and compare it against two reference platforms: the Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+(Sapphire Rapids) and the NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip. Our results show that the SG2044 more than doubles single-core performance and improves scalability compared to SG2042. MCv3 achieves an energy efficiency of 3.08GFLOPs/W which improves of 10x w.r.t. MCv1 and is in the range of x86-64 and Arm servers. On pure performance when normalized on the SIMD/Vector length MCv3 on its peak efficiency point (16 cores) achieves 46% performance of Intel Sapphire Rapids server and 91% performance of NVIDIA Grace CPU superchip.

</details>


### [182] [Mathematical Foundations for Peer-to-Peer Lattice Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22832)
*Danil Gorinevski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了五个关于二维网格图上同步点对点计算的命题，涵盖了传输功下界、网格到集群延迟、调度无关归约、故障容错路由以及小世界网络增强等理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立网格图上同步点对点计算的理论基础。作者通过提出五个命题，分别解决了以下问题：（1）传输任务的几何下界约束；（2）稀疏激活模型下网格与集群架构的性能对比；（3）保证归约语义调度无关性的代数条件；（4）故障环境下的路由路径期望长度估计；（5）通过长程捷径提升网格网络的连接性能。

Method: 研究采用理论分析框架，运用了运输理论、范畴论、渗流理论和网络科学等多种数学工具。具体方法包括：运输功不等式与支撑半径分析、αβγ集体通信成本模型、Lawvere理论与阿贝尔幺半群范畴构造、Aizenman-Barsky集群大小衰减引理、以及Watts-Strogatz小世界网络模型与平均场普适类分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）建立了运输功、完成深度和压缩归约边的三个下界，并否定了角汇维度序路由的负载集中性；（2）证明了当集群固定开销占优时，网格到集群延迟比随激活率单调改善；（3）给出了调度无关归约语义的充分代数判据及PCC可验证性；（4）在亚临界故障区间得到了条件期望路由长度的附加绕行上界；（5）证明k条长程捷径可将典型最短路径从Θ(√P)降至O(log P)，并确定了渗流的平均场普适类。

Conclusion: 该论文系统性地建立了二维网格上同步点对点计算的理论边界与性能保证。核心贡献在于：通过几何、代数和概率方法的结合，为分布式计算的调度独立性、容错性和网络增强提供了严格的数学基础，为实际系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Five propositions on synchronous peer-to-peer computation on a grid graph in $\mathbb{Z}^2$.
  \textbf{Proposition~1} gives three lower bounds: a transport-work bound $\sum_i a_i \ell_i \ge W_1(μ,ν)$, a completion-depth bound $D_{\min} \ge r_μ$ on the support radius, and a compressive-reduction edge bound $|E'| \ge \operatorname{St}_G(\operatorname{supp}(μ)\cup\{x_\star\})$. A negative result: for corner-sink dimension-order routing, the sink-trunk route-load functional has variance $Θ(f(1-f)P^2)$ under i.i.d.\ Bernoulli activation, refuting $O(fP^{3/2})$ concentration.
  \textbf{Proposition~2}: under an $αβγ$ collective-communication cost model and a sparse-activation model, the grid-to-cluster latency ratio improves monotonically as $f_{\mathrm{act}}$ shrinks whenever the cluster's fixed overhead dominates the grid's geometric constant.
  \textbf{Proposition~3}: a sufficient algebraic criterion for schedule-independent reduction semantics is that update rules decompose into a local map and an abelian-monoid merge, as a product-preserving functor from the Lawvere theory of commutative monoids into a hardware-state category. Under an idealised non-interfering scheduler this yields a wall-clock bound; the criterion is PCC-certifiable.
  \textbf{Proposition~4} bounds the conditional expected route length under i.i.d.\ site failure in the subcritical regime $δ< p_c^{\mathrm{site}}(\mathbb{Z}^2)$ by an additive detour term, via Aizenman--Barsky cluster-size decay and a boundary-length bound.
  \textbf{Proposition~5} augments the grid with $k$ long-range Watts--Strogatz shortcuts per node, collapsing typical shortest-path length from $Θ(\sqrt{P})$ to $O(\log P)$; percolation is placed in the mean-field universality class (rigorous for the $1$-D-ring base, conjectural for the $2$-D-grid base).

</details>


### [183] [ObjectCache: Layerwise Object-Storage Retrieval for KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22850)
*Yu Zhu,Aditya Dhakal,Yunming Xiao,Dejan Milojicic,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ObjectCache系统，将KV缓存存储在S3兼容对象存储中，通过协同设计存储协议和传输调度，在保持低延迟的同时解决了GPU内存和本地DRAM容量限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀KV缓存是LLM服务中的关键机制，可减少共享前缀请求的首次令牌时间（TTFT）。然而，累积的KV缓存往往超出GPU内存和本地DRAM容量，现有系统将KV缓存保存在远程DRAM池中，增加了服务集群规模和成本。

Method: 提出ObjectCache系统，将KV缓存存储在S3兼容对象存储中以消除容量限制，并协同设计存储协议和传输调度，使存储服务器按GPU消费顺序交付KV缓存数据，在并发请求间重叠数据传输与计算。在100 Gbps RoCE集群上基于NIXL、Ceph RGW和DAOS构建原型。

Result: 对于64K上下文（当前系统常见），ObjectCache相比本地DRAM仅增加5.6%延迟；对于4K上下文（计算较少难以掩盖传输），相比最优本地分层基线增加56-75ms。在共享带宽限制下，调度器相比等带宽共享将额外TTFT降低1.2-1.8倍。

Conclusion: ObjectCache成功证明了将KV缓存存储在对象存储中的可行性，在保持可接受延迟性能的同时消除了容量约束，为LLM服务系统提供了一种成本效益更高的存储解决方案。

Abstract: Prefix KV caching has become a key mechanism in LLM serving: it reduces time to first token (TTFT) by avoiding redundant computation across requests that share a prefix (i.e., the system prompt). However, the accumulated KV cache is often larger than what GPU memory and local DRAM can hold. To preserve latency, current systems keep the KV cache in remote DRAM pools, increasing serving-cluster size and cost. In this paper, we explore a different approach: storing the KV cache in S3-compatible object storage so that capacity is no longer the constraint, while minimizing the impact on TTFT. We propose ObjectCache, which co-designs the storage protocol and transfer schedule so that the storage server delivers KV cache data in the order the GPU consumes it, overlapping data transfer with compute across concurrent requests. We prototype ObjectCache on a 100 Gbps RoCE cluster with NIXL (an inference library that abstracts storage and memory), Ceph RGW (an Object Gateway for clusters), and DAOS (an open source storage system). For 64K contexts, common in today's systems, ObjectCache adds only 5.6\% latency over local DRAM; for 4K contexts, where less compute is available to mask transfer, ObjectCache adds 56--75\,ms over the optimal local layerwise baseline. Under shared bandwidth caps, our scheduler reduces added TTFT by 1.2--1.8x compared with equal bandwidth sharing.

</details>


### [184] [Budgeted Dynamic Trace Structures for Token-Efficient Sequential Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22879)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了预算动态追踪结构（BDTS）框架，能在严格的字节或令牌预算下高效维护追踪图和追加式历史，将百万级令牌的历史压缩至千级令牌，同时保持毫秒级的构建和查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序计算产生的长追踪数据包含嵌套分支、状态转换、文本负载和早期执行摘要，需要一种能够在显式字节或令牌预算约束下维护追踪图和历史的数据结构框架。

Method: 提出预算动态追踪结构（BDTS）框架，结合状态过滤可达性、游标分页、软上限近期日志、引用计数观察键、增量覆盖、有界成本缓存和摘要+后缀压缩等技术，并给出形式化不变式和渐近界，提供Rust实现和可复现基准测试。

Result: 在包含10,000-40,000顶点的合成追踪上，图构建时间为0.58-2.72ms，后代枚举为0.24-1.42ms；将350k-2.71M近似令牌的历史压缩至1,048-4,120近似令牌；针对三个公共模型目标的分词器和前向测量将3,359-3,360追踪令牌减少至432-433令牌。

Conclusion: BDTS框架成功实现了在显式预算约束下对大规模追踪数据的高效压缩和维护，显著降低了令牌数量同时保持了毫秒级性能，为处理复杂顺序计算追踪提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential computation increasingly produces long traces containing nested branches, status transitions, textual payloads, and compact summaries of earlier execution. This paper introduces budgeted dynamic trace structures (BDTS), a data-structural framework for maintaining rooted trace graphs and append-only histories under an explicit byte or token budget. BDTS combines status-filtered reachability, cursor pagination, soft-capped recency logs, reference-counted observation keys, delta overlays, bounded cost caches, and summary-plus-suffix compaction. We give formal invariants, asymptotic bounds, and an ancillary Rust implementation with reproducible benchmarks. Across synthetic traces with 10,000-40,000 vertices, the prototype builds graphs in 0.58-2.72 ms, enumerates all descendants in 0.24-1.42 ms, and compacts histories of 350k-2.71M approximate tokens to 1,048-4,120 approximate tokens. Tokenizer and forward measurements with three public model targets reduce 3,359-3,360 trace tokens to 432-433 tokens.

</details>


### [185] [Orbax: Distributed Checkpointing with JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23066)
*Colin Gaffney,Shutong Li,Daniel Ng,Anastasia Petrushkina,Niket Kumar,Adam Cogdell,Mridul Sahu,Yaning Liang,Nikhil Bansal,Justin Pan,Angel Mau,Abhishek Agrawal,Marco Berlot,Ruoxin Sang,Kiranbir Sodhia,Rakesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Orbax是JAX生态的原生检查点库，性能显著优于PyTorch同类方案（保存快3.5倍，加载快2倍），解决了JAX缺乏标准化检查点方案的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX凭借模块化设计成为高性能分布式机器学习系统的主流框架之一，但其模块化哲学导致缺乏标准化的检查点解决方案，亟需一个既能抽象分布式加速器系统复杂性、又能在模型全生命周期提供灵活检查点操作的库。

Method: 提出Orbax——一个模块化、JAX原生的检查点库，核心设计包括：抽象分布式加速器系统的复杂性，提供用户友好的检查点操作接口，支持ML模型全生命周期的灵活检查点管理。

Result: 性能测试显示，Orbax在保存检查点时性能超越同类PyTorch竞争方案最高达3.5倍，在加载检查点时性能提升最高达2倍。库已在GitHub开源：https://github.com/google/orbax。

Conclusion: Orbax成功填补了JAX生态系统在标准化检查点解决方案上的空白，通过模块化设计实现了高性能与易用性的平衡，为分布式机器学习系统提供了可靠的生产级检查点能力。

Abstract: In a landscape of high-performance distributed ML systems, JAX has emerged as a framework of choice. However, JAX's modular design philosophy leaves it without a standardized checkpointing solution. In this paper, we introduce Orbax, a modular, JAX-native checkpointing library that abstracts the complexities of distributed accelerator systems while also providing flexibility for user-friendly checkpoint manipulations throughout the ML model lifecycle. We demonstrate performance exceeding comparable PyTorch competitors by up to 3.5$\times$ for saving and 2$\times$ for loading. The library is available at https://github.com/google/orbax.

</details>


### [186] [XWind: A Cross-site Router for Large Language Model Inference Serving at Renewable Energy Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23348)
*Tella Rajashekhar Reddy,Atharva Deshmukh,Liangcheng Yu,Chaojie Zhang,Mike Shepperd,Rohan Gandhi,Anjaly Parayil,Srinivasan Iyengar,Ajay Manchepalli,Debopam Bhattacherjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Greenferencing部署模型，将模块化AI计算设施迁移至风能丰富的地区，并设计XWind路由器，在动态风能供应下有效降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI电力需求快速增长给老化的电网带来巨大压力，电网扩建成本高昂且存在输电损耗。可再生能源在源头充足，但供需不匹配，亟需新的部署模式来缓解电网压力并利用可再生能源。

Method: 提出AI Greenferencing部署模型，将模块化AI计算设施部署到风力发电站附近；设计XWind轻量级推理路由器，仅利用推理延迟、KV-cache利用率和队列深度三个实时信号，动态配置站点并分发请求，适应风力发电的波动性。

Result: 可行性分析显示Azure数据中心50ms网络延迟范围内有890+ GW风能容量；在64-GPU A100测试平台上，XWind相比最强竞争方案降低P99端到端延迟达52%，相比功率限制和GPU空闲等基线方案降低达98%，在不同工作负载、负载水平和GPU代际间均保持一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: AI Greenferencing模型能够有效匹配可再生能源供应与AI计算需求，XWind路由器成功解决了风能波动性下的推理服务问题，为AI基础设施的可持续发展提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: AI power demand is growing at an unprecedented rate while power grids are often ailing and struggle to keep up. Grid expansion comes with high capital expenditure and long-distance transmission losses, yet there is abundant renewable energy at the source, just not matched to demand.
  This paper proposes a complementary AI infrastructure deployment model, AI Greenferencing, that brings modular AI compute to renewable energy sources, focusing on wind, allowing AI footprint expansion, generating local behind-the-meter demand for renewable sites, and helping ease the growing strain on power utilities. Our feasibility analysis shows that 890+ GW of wind capacity lies within 50 ms network round trip time of Azure data centers, and that site-wise right-sizing combined with spatial complementarity of wind energy keeps aggregate fleet utilization on par with traditional deployments.
  To serve inference requests under variable wind power, we build XWind, a lightweight, reactive, and workload-agnostic AI inference router that uses only real-time signals: inference latency, KV-cache utilization, and queue depth, to dynamically configure sites and distribute requests. Evaluated on a real 64-GPU A100 testbed emulating three wind-powered sites with Azure production traces, XWind reduces P99 end-to-end latency by up to 52% over the strongest contender (also our idea) and by up to 98% over baselines such as power-capping and GPU idling, with consistent gains across workload types, load levels, and GPU generations.

</details>


### [187] [AlignedServe: Orchestrating Prefix-aware Batching to Build a High-throughput and Computing-efficient LLM Serving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23389)
*Fengyao Bai,Hongbin Zhang,Zhitao Chen,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AlignedServe通过前缀感知批处理策略对齐同一批次中请求的KV缓存长度，有效减少了LLM推理服务中的迭代级气泡，将解码吞吐量提升了1.98倍，延迟降低了7.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务框架通过批处理和调度减少了请求级和批次级的气泡，但忽略了解码迭代内部的气泡。同一迭代中生成的Token依赖不同长度的KV缓存，导致计算成本不同；具有长KV缓存的Token会成为瓶颈并延迟下一次迭代。

Method: 提出AlignedServe框架，核心是前缀感知批处理策略，将具有相似KV缓存长度的请求分组到同一批次以减少迭代级气泡。利用大容量CPU内存维护足够的进行中请求以支持批处理，应用批次级调度策略减少批次级气泡，并引入GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU架构，通过一个GPU为另一个GPU预取KV缓存来减少CPU到GPU的传输延迟。

Result: 在合成和应用工作负载上的实验表明，AlignedServe将解码吞吐量提高了1.98倍，并将延迟降低了7.4倍，优于最先进的系统。

Conclusion: AlignedServe成功解决了LLM推理服务中被忽视的迭代级气泡问题，通过创新的批处理策略和架构设计显著提升了服务性能，为高吞吐量LLM应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-throughput inference serving is essential for applications built on large language models (LLMs). Existing serving frameworks reduce request-level and batch-level bubbles through batching and scheduling, but often overlook bubbles within each decode iteration. Tokens generated in the same iteration may incur different costs because they depend on KV caches of different lengths; tokens with long KV caches can become bottlenecks and delay the next iteration. We propose AlignedServe, an LLM serving framework built around prefix-aware batching. It groups requests with similar KV-cache lengths into the same batch to reduce iteration-level bubbles. To support this policy efficiently, AlignedServe uses large CPU memory to maintain sufficient in-flight requests for batching and applies a batch-level scheduling policy to reduce batch-level bubbles. It also introduces a GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU architecture, where one GPU prefetches KV cache for another to reduce CPU-to-GPU transfer latency. Experiments on synthetic and application workloads show that AlignedServe improves decoding throughput by up to 1.98 times and reduces latency by up to 7.4 times over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Round Visibility: A Post-Consensus Ordering Layer for DAG-Based BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23432)
*Pengkun Ren,Dong Hai,Nasrin Sohrabi,Zahir Tari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MRV（多轮可见性）机制，作为DAG-BFT协议的后共识结构化排序层，通过利用已提交DAG中固有的元数据（创建者、轮次、祖先关系）来推导排序证据，在不增加共识关键路径开销的前提下，解决了DAG-BFT中因并发提交导致的排序模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG-based BFT协议通过解耦传播与共识并允许并发提交来实现高吞吐量，但这种并发性削弱了执行边界处的排序证据：一旦单元在共享DAG边界被提交，其最终线性化由遍历或确定性打破规则驱动，而非可验证的结构优先级。现有的公平排序设计通过在共识工作流中收集或重建交易级排序证据来解决歧义，但这将排序与共识耦合，并将排序逻辑置于关键路径上。

Method: 提出Multi-Round Visibility (MRV)后共识结构化排序层，重新解释已提交DAG作为排序证据基底。MRV利用已提交顶点固有的认证创建者、轮次和祖先元数据，使副本能够在无额外共识路径消息的情况下推导多轮结构可见性。MRV在有界证据视野内积累可见性，在原子公平单元（AUFs）共存于DAG后比较并发提交的AUFs，并从拜占庭鲁棒的可见性优势推导优先约束。当DAG缺乏此类约束时，MRV通过确定性图补全暴露并解决剩余歧义。

Result: 在基于Narwhal/Tusk的原型上实现MRV，在5-50个副本及各种故障设置下的评估显示：MRV保持了DAG-BFT栈的高吞吐量特性，证明了其能够在不增加共识关键路径负担的情况下提供后共识结构化排序。

Conclusion: MRV成功地将排序逻辑从共识关键路径中分离出来，通过利用DAG结构的固有元数据提供可验证的排序证据，既保持了DAG-BFT的高吞吐量优势，又解决了并发提交带来的排序模糊性问题，为DAG-based BFT协议提供了一种高效的后共识排序解决方案。

Abstract: Directed acyclic graph (DAG)-based Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) protocols achieve high throughput by decoupling dissemination from agreement and allowing many vertices to be committed concurrently. This same concurrency, however, weakens ordering evidence at the execution boundary: once units are committed in a shared DAG frontier, their final linearization is driven by traversal or deterministic tie-breaking rather than verifiable structural precedence. Prior fair-ordering designs address ambiguity by collecting or reconstructing transaction-level ordering evidence within the consensus workflow. While effective, this couples ordering with agreement and places ordering logic on the critical path. This paper presents Multi-Round Visibility (MRV), a post-consensus structural ordering layer for DAG-based BFT that reinterprets the committed DAG as an ordering evidence substrate. Committed vertices inherently carry authenticated creator, round, and ancestry metadata, enabling replicas to derive multi-round structural visibility without extra consensus-path messages. MRV accumulates this visibility within a bounded evidence horizon, compares concurrently committed atomic units of fairness (AUFs) after they coexist in the DAG, and derives precedence constraints from Byzantine-robust visibility advantages. When the DAG lacks such constraints, MRV exposes and resolves the remaining ambiguity through deterministic graph completion rather than hiding it inside traversal rules. We implement MRV on a Narwhal/Tusk-based prototype. Evaluation across 5-50 replicas under various fault settings shows MRV preserves the high-throughput regime of the DAG-BFT stack, proving it provides post-consensus structural ordering without burdening the consensus-critical path.

</details>


### [189] [Multi-Factor Trust-Driven Secure Communication Model for Cloud-Based Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23566)
*Deepika Saxena,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MT-SeCom框架，通过多因素信任监测、自适应信任评估、基于Transformer的可信客户端分类和弹性通信管理四个阶段，解决了云数字孪生平台中异构客户端行为、资源竞争和对抗威胁下的安全通信问题，实现了18.7%的威胁检测准确率提升和24.3%的异常减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数字孪生平台面临异构客户端行为、资源竞争和不断演变的对抗威胁等挑战，确保安全和可信的通信仍然是一个关键难题，需要一种能够适应动态网络环境和威胁强度的安全通信框架。

Method: MT-SeCom框架通过四个协调阶段运行：(i) 多因素信任监测，捕获时间、上下文和联邦信任信号；(ii) 自适应信任评估，根据网络动态和威胁强度调整信任权重；(iii) 基于Transformer的可信客户端分类，结合异常检测与监督学习准确识别恶意或不可靠节点；(iv) 弹性通信管理，优化路由、隔离受损客户端并确保服务连续性。

Result: 在真实测试平台和综合实验中，MT-SeCom相比现有方法实现了平均18.7%的威胁检测准确率提升和24.3%的异常发生减少，在波动攻击条件下显著增强了安全通信能力，缓解了级联对抗效应并保持了高弹性。

Conclusion: MT-SeCom框架证明了其在异构云数字孪生生态系统中的鲁棒性、可扩展性和实用性，能够有效应对动态威胁环境下的安全通信挑战，为云数字孪生平台提供了可靠的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud-based Digital Twin (DT) platforms enable real-time monitoring, simulation, and collaborative decision-making across distributed clients. However, ensuring secure and trustworthy communication remains a critical challenge due to heterogeneous client behavior, resource contention, and evolving adversarial threats. This paper proposes the Multi-Factor Trust-Driven Secure Communication (MT-SeCom) framework to enforce resilient and intelligent collaboration in DT-enabled cloud environments. MT-SeCom operates through four coordinated phases: (i) Multi-Factor Trust Monitoring, capturing temporal, contextual, and federated trust signals; (ii) Adaptive Trust Evaluation, adjusting trust weights based on network dynamics and threat intensity; (iii) Transformer-Based Trusted Client Classification, combining anomaly detection with supervised learning to accurately identify malicious or unreliable nodes; and (iv) Resilient Communication Management, optimizing routing, isolating compromised clients, and ensuring service continuity. A real-world testbed and comprehensive experiments demonstrate that MT-SeCom significantly enhances secure communication, mitigates cascading adversarial effects, and maintains high resilience under fluctuating attack conditions. MT-SeCom achieves an average 18.7% improvement in threat detection accuracy and a 24.3% reduction in anomaly occurrences compared to existing methods, confirming its robustness, scalability, and practical suitability for heterogeneous cloud-based DT ecosystems.

</details>


### [190] [Herring: Parallel Batch-Order-Fairness on DAG-based Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23648)
*Marko Putnik,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Herring，首个支持并行化的γ-batch-OF DAG BFT协议，解决了现有批量顺序公平性协议的性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFT共识协议无法防止交易排序攻击，导致MEV问题；而现有的批量顺序公平性(batch-OF)协议存在严重性能问题：领导者类协议将公平决策集中在单一副本，基于DAG的协议(FairDAG, DoD)将公平层强制串行执行。

Method: 提出Herring协议，结合共识后图构建与显式缺失边解析（搭载在DAG可靠广播层上），将公平排序从每轮串行瓶颈转变为CPU密集型任务；同时在已提交的子DAG上并行化主导的图构建开销。此外发现并修复了FairDAG-RL和DoD中的活跃性漏洞。

Result: Herring吞吐量紧密跟踪Narwhal & Tusk达约10,000 tx/s；饱和吞吐量比FairDAG-RL高约90%，比DoD-W高100%；显著降低饱和时的执行延迟。

Conclusion: Herring成功实现了公平排序层的并行化，在不牺牲公平性保证的前提下大幅提升了性能，填补了现有协议的安全漏洞，证明了高效且安全的批量顺序公平性协议的可行性。

Abstract: Transaction ordering attacks extract billions of dollars annually from decentralized finance users in the form of Maximal Extractable Value (MEV). Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols guarantee total order but place no constraint on how that order is chosen, leaving the door open for adversarial reordering. Batch-order-fairness (batch-OF) protocols close this gap, but existing designs pay a steep performance price for this guarantee. Leader-based protocols such as Themis concentrate all fairness decisions at a single replica, while recent DAG-based proposals FairDAG and DAG of DAGs (DoD) force their fairness layer into strictly serial execution despite running on multi-proposer DAGs.
  We present Herring, the first $γ$-batch-OF DAG BFT protocol whose fairness layer parallelizes the dominant graph construction cost across committed subdags. Herring combines post-consensus graph construction with explicit missing edge resolution piggybacked on the DAG's reliable broadcast layer, a pairing that turns fair ordering from a per-round serial bottleneck into a CPU-bound task. We also uncover previously unreported liveness vulnerabilities in both FairDAG-RL and DoD that a malicious client can trigger to halt the fairness layer indefinitely, and propose patches that we integrate into our reimplementations.
  We implement Herring on top of the Rust implementation of Narwhal \& Tusk and evaluate it against FairDAG-RL, DoD-W, and Themis. Herring tracks the throughput of Narwhal \& Tusk closely up to roughly $10{,}000$\,tx/s, achieves roughly $90\%$ higher saturation throughput than FairDAG-RL and $100\%$ higher than DoD-W, and substantially reduces execution latency at saturation.

</details>


### [191] [AMP: Arc Multi-Proposer Protocol with Bounded Inclusion Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23677)
*Daniel Cason,Gordon Liao,Sergio Mena,Nenad Milošević,Adi Seredinschi,Alessandro Sforzin,João Sousa,Preston Vander Vos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AMP协议，通过多提议者机制解决区块链系统中单一验证者对交易包含和排序的单边控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链金融系统面临结构性挑战：单一验证者对区块组装拥有单边权力，可控制交易包含和排序。传统市场通过法律限制此类权力，而区块链缺乏此类规则，监管机构已将其视为区块链金融基础设施的首要关切。

Method: 在Tendermint共识算法上构建AMP多提议者协议：1）引入专用提议者节点，位于用户和验证者之间；2）提议者收集交易并组成负载，广播给所有验证者；3）采用传播与共识分离的设计原则；4）验证者公开证明接收负载，通过共识决定下一区块包含的负载集合；5）对包含的负载运行确定性排序算法。

Result: AMP实现以下保证：当所有正确验证者证明某负载时，该负载必定包含在下一区块；区块包含来自多个提议者的负载；验证者无法控制区块内容包含或任意排序区块内容。

Conclusion: AMP通过有界的包含保证和确定性排序算法，有效限制单一验证者权力，实现了更强的抗审查和抗操纵能力，同时传播与共识分离的设计可能带来更高吞吐量。

Abstract: Blockchain systems that settle financial transactions face a structural tension: the single validator that assembles each block holds unilateral power over transaction inclusion and ordering. Traditional markets curb this very power through front-running and market-manipulation laws. Regulators have flagged the absence of such rules as a first-order concern for blockchain-based financial infrastructure. In response, we introduce AMP, a multi-proposer protocol, on top of the Tendermint consensus algorithm, where no validator can control the flow of transactions into blocks. Instead, dedicated nodes called proposers sit between users and validators. They collect user transactions, group them into payloads, and broadcast the payloads to all validators. Consequently, there is no mempool, and AMP applies the design principle of separating dissemination from agreement, which can lead to higher throughput. Validators publicly attest to receiving payloads and run consensus to decide the set of payloads to include in the next block. When all correct validators attest to a given payload, AMP guarantees that payload will be included in the next block; a block thus contains payloads from multiple proposers, allowing for bulk finalization. This bounded inclusion guarantee along with a deterministic ordering algorithm which is run over all payloads included in a block, curbs the power of any single validator. Validators no longer control what is included in a block, nor can they arbitrarily order the contents of blocks.

</details>


### [192] [Flare: Leveraging Serverless Elasticity to Absorb Microservice Load Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23707)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,David Schall,Antonios Katsarakis,Marios Kogias,Rakesh Kumar,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Flare，一种结合虚拟机(VM)和无服务器计算(Serverless)的混合微服务架构。通过VM处理稳态负载以控制成本，利用无服务器计算弹性吸收流量峰值，在检测到过载服务时仅将超量负载转移至无服务器平台，在保证性能的同时最小化成本开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线服务通常以VM中容器化的微服务链形式部署，在负载平稳时性能良好且经济，但面对不可预测的负载峰值时扩容缓慢，导致终端用户体验下降。为避免性能下降而进行过度配置虽然可行，但成本高昂且长期资源利用率低下。

Method: 提出Flare混合微服务架构：将VM与无服务器计算结合，VM承担稳态负载以实现成本效益，无服务器计算提供弹性能力吸收流量峰值。当峰值发生时，Flare检测具体过载服务，仅将这些服务的超量负载转移至无服务器平台，从而最小化成本开销。该方案无缝集成到现有自动扩缩容和无服务器基础设施中，仅需对控制平面做最小改动，无需修改应用程序。

Result: Flare能够有效检测过载服务并智能地将超量负载转移至无服务器平台，实现了对流量峰值的快速响应。该架构能够无缝集成到现有的自动扩缩容和无服务器基础设施中，仅需极小的控制平面改动且无需修改应用代码。

Conclusion: 通过VM与无服务器计算的协同，Flare成功平衡了成本效益与弹性扩容能力，为解决微服务架构中不可预测负载峰值问题提供了一种经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Online services strive to maintain application responsiveness even when the traffic is unpredictable and fluctuating. Today's online services are commonly deployed as chains of microservices, each microservice packaged as one or more containers inside virtual machines (VMs). While performant and affordable when the load is steady, VM-based deployments are known to be slow to scale when the load spikes, resulting in degraded performance for end-users of the service. To avoid such performance degradations, service providers can over-provision their deployments; however, such a strategy is costly and inefficient, leaving resources under-utilized for extended periods.
  To address the challenge of unpredictable load spikes, we propose Flare, a hybrid microservice architecture that combines VMs with serverless computing. Flare utilizes VMs to cost-effectively handle steady workloads and leverages serverless elasticity to absorb traffic spikes. When a spike occurs, Flare detects which specific service(s) are overloaded and shifts the excess load of only those services to serverless, thus minimizing the cost overhead. Flare seamlessly integrates into existing auto-scaling and serverless infrastructure, requiring minimal changes to the control plane and no modifications to the application.

</details>


### [193] [HyperParallel-MoE: Multi-Core Interleaved Scheduling for Fast MoE Training on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23764)
*Zewen Jin,Congkun Ai,Guangpeng Zhang,Hanbo Zhang,Haoran Wang,Shihan Xiao,Da Lei,Xuefeng Jin,Teng Su,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyperParallel-MoE，一个面向昇腾NPU的MoE训练编译调度框架。该框架将算子级执行转换为静态调度的tile级异构任务流，通过AIV驱动单边通信、依赖保持的tile任务生成和事件驱动静态调度，实现了通信、矩阵计算和向量计算的细粒度重叠，在DeepSeek风格MoE模型上使Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN延迟降低多达1.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型大规模训练依赖于AI加速器集群。昇腾NPU暴露了异构片上计算资源，包括面向矩阵的AIC单元和面向向量的AIV单元，并支持显式跨队列同步。然而，现有训练框架主要以串行逐内核方式执行MoE算子，导致大量异构并行性未被充分利用。

Method: HyperParallel-MoE框架将算子级MoE执行转换为静态调度的tile级异构任务流，涵盖AIC和AIV资源。核心技术包括：(1) AIV驱动单边通信消除主机侧集合同步；(2) 依赖保持的tile任务生成统一通信与计算的任务抽象；(3) 事件驱动静态调度协调跨队列执行并降低运行时开销；(4) 统一运行时在单次内核启动中并发驱动AIC和AIV workers，实现通信、矩阵计算和向量计算的细粒度重叠。框架在MindSpore和MindFormers技术栈上实现。

Result: 在昇腾A3集群上使用DeepSeek风格MoE模型进行评估，在多种专家并行配置下，HyperParallel-MoE将Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN延迟降低最多1.58倍，证明了tile级异构调度可以显著提升现代NPU上的MoE训练效率。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，tile级异构调度能够充分利用现代NPU的异构片上资源，通过细粒度重叠通信与计算，显著提升MoE训练效率。HyperParallel-MoE为大规模MoE模型训练提供了一种有效的编译和调度解决方案。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models increasingly rely on large-scale AI accelerator clusters for efficient training. Ascend NPUs expose heterogeneous on-chip compute resources, including matrix-oriented AIC units and vector-oriented AIV units with explicit cross-queue synchronization support. However, existing training frameworks largely execute MoE operators in a serialized kernel-by-kernel manner, leaving substantial heterogeneous parallelism underutilized.
  This paper presents HyperParallel-MoE, a compilation and scheduling framework for MoE training on Ascend NPUs. HyperParallel-MoE transforms operator-level MoE execution into a statically scheduled tile-level heterogeneous taskflow spanning AIC and AIV resources. It introduces AIV-driven one-sided communication to eliminate host-side collective synchronization, dependency-preserving tile task generation to unify communication and computation under a common task abstraction, and event-driven static scheduling to coordinate cross-queue execution with low runtime overhead. HyperParallel-MoE further executes the compiled taskflow within a unified runtime that concurrently drives AIC and AIV workers inside a single kernel launch, enabling fine-grained overlap among communication, matrix computation, and vector computation while preserving existing optimized operators. We implement HyperParallel-MoE in the MindSpore and MindFormers stack and evaluate it using DeepSeek-style MoE models on Ascend A3 clusters. Across multiple expert-parallel configurations, HyperParallel-MoE reduces Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN latency by up to 1.58x, demonstrating that tile-level heterogeneous scheduling can substantially improve MoE training efficiency on modern NPUs.

</details>


### [194] [Enhancing Energy Efficiency in Scientific Workflows through CFD based PIVAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23850)
*Ali Zahir,Ashiq Anjum,Mark Wilkinson,Jeyan Thiyagalingam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的AI辅助调度框架，通过集成CFD和物理信息变分自编码器(PIVAE)生成物理真实的合成工作负载数据，在HPC环境中实现了显著的节能效果（最高10%），同时仅带来微小的性能损失（约5-6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算(HPC)环境中科学工作流复杂性和规模的不断增长，在不牺牲计算性能的前提下管理能耗成为重大挑战。传统调度策略无法有效处理热动力学、工作负载多样性和系统可扩展性之间的复杂相互作用，导致能源使用效率低下且不可持续。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的可扩展AI辅助调度框架，核心方法包括：(1) 将计算流体动力学(CFD)与物理信息变分自编码器(PIVAE)相结合，生成物理真实的合成工作负载数据；(2) 基于资源利用配置文件对工作流进行分类；(3) 评估多种调度策略，如局部性感知调度和推测感知调度；(4) 涵盖从事件重建到异常检测等不同计算强度的多样化工作流。

Result: 实验结果表明，适度的CPU性能降低（如降至15%）可以实现显著的节能效果（最高达10%），而周转时间仅轻微增加（约5-6%），成功识别出最佳运行平衡点。研究证明了物理信息生成建模能够实现自适应、可持续且数据高效的调度方案。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了物理信息生成建模如何为下一代HPC基础设施实现自适应、可持续且数据高效的调度，证明了在不显著牺牲性能的前提下可以实现可观的能源节约，为HPC环境的可持续发展提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity and scale of scientific workflows in high performance computing (HPC) environments have led to significant challenges in managing energy consumption without compromising computational performance. Traditional scheduling strategies often fail to account for the complex interplay between thermal dynamics, workload diversity, and system scalability, leading to inefficient and unsustainable energy usage. This paper introduces a novel, scalable, and AI-assisted scheduling framework for optimizing energy consumption in HPC environments without compromising performance. Central to our approach is the integration of Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) with a Physics-Informed Variational Autoencoder (PIVAE), enabling the generation of physically realistic synthetic workload data that bridges the gap between thermodynamic behavior and scheduler decision-making in complex, multi-scale HPC environments. By categorizing workflows based on resource utilization profiles, we evaluate multiple scheduling strategies such as Locality Aware and Speculative Aware Scheduling. These workflows, ranging from event reconstruction to anomaly detection, represent diverse computational intensities. Our results show that modest reductions in CPU performance (e.g., to 15%) can yield substantial energy savings (up to 10%) with only minor turnaround time increases (approximately 5-6%), identifying an optimal operational sweet spot. This work demonstrates how physics-informed generative modeling can enable adaptive, sustainable, and data-efficient scheduling for next-generation HPC infrastructures.

</details>
